# ShellX V2-2a — Phone AI sessions Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** A paired phone lists the desktop's AI sessions, opens one and follows it live, sends prompts, answers permissions and questions with big buttons, stops a turn, opens a new session in a project folder of the computer, and gets a push when an agent needs it. Content travels only inside the existing end-to-end encrypted channel. Old phones and old desktops keep working exactly as today.

**Architecture:**
- **Protocol (`shellx_core`).** Sixteen flat `ai.*` messages. Their content is a `body` byte string of UTF-8 JSON, so the transport sees depth 3 and never a float. An `ai.*` message this version cannot read is skipped, never fatal. `session.list.result` gains `ai: 1` (capability).
- **Wire, mirror and subscription (`package:shellx_ai/remote.dart`, pure Dart).**
  - The desktop side encodes timeline items and the session header into records cut to phone size. A per-phone, per-session `AiSubscription` sends a snapshot (the last 60 items), then deltas keyed by item index and `rev` (streaming text as appended pieces), at most 256 KiB unacknowledged.
  - The phone side keeps an `AiMirror` of phone items (`MirrorText`, `MirrorTool`, …) and re-attaches with what it holds (`AiHave`) after a gap or a reconnect.
- **Hub and routing (`shellx_agent`).**
  - `AiPhoneHub` (the window's side) reads the window through `AiSessionSource`. It lists sessions, mirrors attached ones at most every 100 ms, checks every phone request against what is pending now, and asks for pushes.
  - `SessionRouter` routes `ai.*` to an `AiEndpoint`. `AiEndpointSlot` (in `SessionCore`) answers "unavailable" until the window plugs its hub in.
  - With the background service, `MuxAiForwarder` (daemon) and `MuxAiBinding` (window) carry `ai.*` over the mux link (`ai.in`/`ai.out`/`ai.gone`/`ai.serve`/`ai.push`).
- **Phone client (`shellx_client`).** `MachineClient.ai` (`MachineAi`) holds the listing, the mirrors (`AiSessionLink`), acknowledgements, requests with `rid`, and the resume on every new channel.
- **Desktop (`apps/desktop`).**
  - The agent side gets the slot. The daemon installs the forwarder.
  - After V2-1b: `DesktopAiSource` over the tab list and `AiRuntimes`, the hub provider, and its start-up wiring.
- **Mobile (`apps/mobile`).**
  - The AI session page: virtualized, anchored timeline; markdown; tool cards with diffs; permission panel; question form; queue; composer.
  - An "AI sessions" group in the session picker, "New AI session", a ✦ button with a waiting count, and a push tap that opens the session.

**Tech Stack:** Dart ^3.11 and Flutter 3.41.4 (`~/fvm/versions/3.41.4/bin`, not on PATH), `package:test`, `fake_async`, `flutter_test`, signals + get_it + auto_route + injectable + intl_utils on mobile (generated code is committed), `flutter_markdown_plus 1.0.12` (the desktop's pin, V2-1b D17), the V2-1a packages `shellx_acp` and `shellx_ai`.

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-v2-2-phone-ai-design.md` (binding, Vietnamese; D1–D16, §4 protocol, §5 phone UI, §8 tests). Also binding: spec V2-1 `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-ai-pane-design.md` (D9, D10, D13, D20, D22) and the demo's scenario 7 (`docs/superpowers/design/2026-10-14-ai-pane-demo/`).

## Rulings (each with "cost if wrong")

- **P1 — one hub in the window, two ways to it** (spec D1, D2).
  - In-process: router → `AiEndpointSlot` → hub.
  - With the background service: router → slot → `MuxAiForwarder` → mux → `MuxAiBinding` → hub.
  - The daemon never reads a body. It forwards only desktop → phone types that fit one sealed batch, and answers "unavailable" without a serving window.
  - *Cost if wrong:* one extra local hop (~1 ms).
- **P2 — the phone renders mirror items, not `TimelineItem`s** (spec D6). They come from the same timeline, cut for a phone, without desktop-only data, behind their own wire version. Questions keep their ACP shape, so the phone validates with the same `ElicitationForm.accepts`. *Cost if wrong:* ~7 small classes.
- **P3 — content is a JSON byte string in a flat CBOR envelope; a bad `ai.*` message is skipped** (spec D3). A known message with a bad field drops the whole channel today (`SecureChannel.open`), and CBOR allows depth 8 and no floats. *Cost if wrong:* one more decode step.
- **P4 — no byte chunking: every message is self-contained ≤ 48 KiB** (spec D4).
  - Long text travels as appended pieces.
  - Tool cards are cut: head and tail of output, diff regions with 3 lines of context, ≤ 3 diffs. Cards still too big become `big` stubs.
  - *Cost if wrong:* a huge card is read on the computer.
- **P5 — resync by what the phone holds (`AiHave`: generation + revs), not by sequence replay** (spec D5). A gap or a reconnect re-attaches; only changed items come back. *Cost if wrong:* after a gap, the changed items are sent again.
- **P6 — pushes only for "needs your answer" and for a long turn that ended** (spec D11).
  - No push while a phone has that session open, or while the window is active with the pane focused.
  - At most one needs-input push per session per 30 s.
  - A phone that only lists still gets the push; the existing in-app push banner shows it.
  - *Cost if wrong:* one in-app banner too many.
- **P7 — answering from the notification itself is V2-2b** (spec §11.2). It needs a relay change (FCM category / data-only messages), a native notification plugin and a background channel. Tonight a tap opens the session with the big-button panel. *Cost if wrong:* two taps instead of one on a locked phone.
- **P8 — no config message in V2-2** (spec D13). Mode and model are shown read-only on the phone, so "Bypass permissions" cannot be enabled from a phone at all. *Cost if wrong:* the mode is switched on the computer.
- **P9 — "New AI session" from the phone uses folder keys the desktop offers** (`ws:<id>`, `path:<folder>`) and ready agents only (spec D9). The new tab is not selected on the desktop. *Cost if wrong:* a project outside every workspace needs a root set on the computer first.
- **P10 — the AI session is its own route** (`AiSessionRoute`), over the machine page or, from a push, over the machine list; it is not a tab in the terminal tab strip (spec D8, D10). *Cost if wrong:* AI sessions are one tap away (✦ or picker), not in the strip.
- **P11 — a phone attaching to a restored, not-started AI tab starts its agent** (spec D15, like V2-1b B11 on the desktop). *Cost if wrong:* an agent starts when the user only looks at the session on the phone.
- **P12 — undo, changes panel, images, voice, history, config: V2-2b** (spec §11.2). *Cost if wrong:* those stay on the computer for now.

## Global Constraints

- **Branch and scope:**
  - Branch `shellx-v2` only. Never touch the relay.
  - Never change the V2-1a model (`packages/shellx_ai/lib/src/model/*`, `AiRuntime`), `packages/shellx_acp`, or V2-1b's AI tab UI (`apps/desktop/lib/src/ui/ai/*`).
  - Additions in `packages/shellx_ai` go only into `lib/remote.dart` and `lib/src/remote/`.
- **Protocol (exact values):**
  - `kAiProtocol = 1`;
  - `kAiMaxBodyBytes = 48 * 1024` (`== kAiBodyBytes` in `shellx_ai`, pinned by a test);
  - `kAiMaxPromptBytes = 32 * 1024` (UTF-8).
  - Envelope keys: `t`, `sid` (string), `rid`, `seq`, `body` (bytes), `available`, `before`, `reason`, `text`, `permission`, `option`, `ask`, `action`, `queued`, `agent`, `folder`, `ok`, `code`.
  - `ai` in `session.list.result` only in answers to `session.list {desktop: true}`, only with an endpoint.
  - The desktop never sends `ai.*` to a phone that did not send `ai.list`/`ai.attach`/a request first.
- **Wire (`shellx_ai` remote):**
  - `kAiWireVersion = 1`, `kAiWindowBytes = 256 * 1024`, `kAiAttachItems = 60`, `kAiPageItems = 60`, `kAiTextChunkBytes = 32 * 1024`;
  - tool output 4 KiB head + 4 KiB tail (`kAiOutputBytes`), diff side `kAiDiffSideBytes = 4 * 1024`, `kAiMaxDiffs = 3`, `kAiDiffContext = 3`, `kAiInputBytes = 2 * 1024`, `kAiAskBytes = 16 * 1024`;
  - `kAiRecordBytes = kAiBodyBytes - 1024`; `at` offsets in UTF-16 code units.
- **Hub:**
  - Flush ≤ once per 100 ms per session (leading edge after idle).
  - Listing pushes ≤ once per 250 ms, only when the JSON changed.
  - Request budget per phone: 60 burst, refilling 30/s (`ai.ack` and `ai.detach` are free); over budget a request gets `busy`.
  - Needs-input push ≤ once per 30 s per session; done push only for turns ≥ A4's threshold (`notifyThreshold`) that were not cancelled.
  - Session id `'<epoch>.<paneId>'`; the epoch is 6 random base-36 characters per hub.
- **Phone client:** at most one `ai.ack` per session per 50 ms; requests time out after the machine client's handshake timeout; a channel loss fails pending requests with `MachineConnectionException(agentOffline)` and never resends them.
- **Privacy (spec D12):**
  - No prompt, agent text, tool output, path or file name in any log.
  - `toString()` of every `ai.*` message is `'<Type>(…)'`; that of every remote type (`AiSessionInfo`, `AiListing`, `Mirror*`, `AiHave`, `AiSubscription`, `AiOutgoing`, `AiPush`, `AiSessionEntry`) is `'…'`.
  - New log lines print only `error.runtimeType`.
- **Never auto-allow (spec D13):**
  - Nothing answers a permission or a question without a user tap.
  - No "remember", no resend after a channel loss.
  - The desktop checks every answer against what is pending now.
- **Mobile:**
  - Strings in `lib/l10n/intl_en.arb` and `intl_vi.arb` (same keys; `test/l10n_test.dart` checks), generated with `PATH=~/fvm/versions/3.41.4/bin:$PATH dart run intl_utils:generate`.
  - Routes and DI generated with `PATH=~/fvm/versions/3.41.4/bin:$PATH dart run build_runner build --delete-conflicting-outputs`. Generated files are committed.
  - Format only the files a task touches: `PATH=~/fvm/versions/3.41.4/bin:$PATH dart format <files>` (page width 120 from `analysis_options.yaml`).
  - Colours and type from `context.colors` / `context.typo` only; no looping animation except a spinner while loading.
- **Commands:**
  - packages: `~/fvm/versions/3.41.4/bin/dart test <file>` and `~/fvm/versions/3.41.4/bin/dart analyze` from the package directory;
  - apps: `PATH=~/fvm/versions/3.41.4/bin:$PATH flutter test <file>` and `… flutter analyze` from the app directory.
  - After a pubspec change: `PATH=~/fvm/versions/3.41.4/bin:$PATH flutter pub get` at the repo root.
- **Commits:**
  - `feat(v2-ai): …` / `test(v2-ai): …` / `docs(v2-ai): …`, ending with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
  - `pubspec.lock` is git-ignored: never add it.

## Review Focus

These are the five failure modes most likely to hurt a user. Each has a pinning test in its owning task.

1. **One AI message drops the whole channel, terminals included.** A body over one sealed batch, too deep for the CBOR decoder, or carrying a float. A desktop sending an oversized `ai.out` through the daemon. [Task 1 "the largest body fits one sealed batch", "a body of any depth", "a malformed or unknown ai.* message is skipped"; Task 2 "a card too big … becomes a big stub"; Task 5 forwarder test (a phone → desktop type or another window's `ai.out` is dropped)]
2. **An answer lands on the wrong request.** The other device answered first, or the request was cancelled and a new one asked. [Task 4 "a permission answer must name the pending request and one of its options"; Task 6 "list, attach, stream, prompt and answer" (the tablet gets `stale`); Task 9 "answered on another device: says so"]
3. **Something answers by itself, or Bypass is reachable from the phone.** [Task 4 "… nothing is ever answered without one"; Task 9 "… one tap answers once, nothing is ever answered without a tap"; Task 12 sweep (no `ai.config`, no `bypassPermissions` write)]
4. **Phone and desktop drift apart after a lost body, a reconnect or a new runtime.** [Task 2 "a missing body is found", "a re-attach with what the phone holds brings only what changed", "another generation, or too much new, starts over"; Task 6 "a lost channel resumes with what the phone holds"]
5. **Old peers.**
   - An old phone receives `ai.*` it cannot read.
   - A new phone waits forever on an old desktop.
   - An old background service with a new window, or the reverse.

   [Task 1 frozen-decoder tests; Task 6 "new phone, old desktop" and "old phone, new desktop"; Task 5 "an older service (no forwarder) ignores the window's ai.* messages"; Task 3 "without an endpoint ai.* is ignored"]

### Task 1: `ai.*` messages and the `ai` capability in `shellx_core` [independent of V2-1b]

The sixteen messages of spec V2-2 §4.1, as a `part` of `messages.dart`, so they extend the sealed `AppMessage`.
- **Envelope:** flat; content is a `body` byte string, so the transport sees depth 3 and no float whatever the content (spec D3).
- **Bad input:** a malformed `ai.*` message, or a newer `ai.*` type, decodes to `null`, so it is skipped and the channel and its terminals stay.
- **`toString()`:** shows only the type.
- **`session.list.result`:** gains `ai` (written only when set).
- **Old peers:** a frozen copy of the decoder every released phone and desktop runs pins it (Review Focus 1, 5).

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/shellx_core/lib/src/protocol/ai_messages.dart`
- Modify: `packages/shellx_core/lib/src/protocol/messages.dart`
- Create: `packages/shellx_core/test/protocol/frozen/messages_v1.dart` (generated by Step 1, never edited)
- Test: `packages/shellx_core/test/protocol/ai_messages_test.dart`
- Modify: `docs/superpowers/ledgers/v2-ai.md` (heading `## V2-2a (phone) — planning rulings`, rulings P1–P12 from this plan, one line each with its cost if wrong)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: nothing new.
- Produces (exported from `package:shellx_core/shellx_core.dart`):
  - constants: `const int kAiProtocol = 1`, `const int kAiMaxBodyBytes = 48 * 1024`, `const int kAiMaxPromptBytes = 32 * 1024`.
  - `sealed class AiMessage extends AppMessage { String? get sid; String toString() => '$runtimeType(…)'; }`
  - phone → desktop:
    - `AiList()`;
    - `AiAttach({required String sid, Uint8List? body})`, `AiDetach({required String sid})`;
    - `AiAck({required String sid, required int seq})`, `AiEarlier({required String sid, required int before})`;
    - `AiPrompt({required String sid, required int rid, required String text})`;
    - `AiAnswer({required String sid, required int rid, int? permission, String? option, int? ask, String? action, Uint8List? body})`;
    - `AiCancel({required String sid, required int rid})`, `AiForceStop({required String sid, required int rid})` (`ai.stop`);
    - `AiUnqueue({required String sid, required int rid, required int queued})`;
    - `AiOpen({required int rid, required String agent, required String folder})`.
  - desktop → phone:
    - `AiListResult({required bool available, required Uint8List body})`;
    - `AiSnapshot({required String sid, required int seq, required Uint8List body})`, `AiUpdate({…same…})`;
    - `AiClosed({required String sid, required String reason})`;
    - `AiResult({required int rid, required bool ok, String? code, String? sid})`.
  - `SessionListResult(List<SessionInfo> sessions, {bool? desktopSharing, int? ai})` with `final int? ai`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Freeze the released decoder**

From the repo root:

````bash
mkdir -p packages/shellx_core/test/protocol/frozen
{ printf '%s\n' "// Frozen copy of lib/src/protocol/messages.dart at 5e04dc8 (the decoder of" \
    "// every released phone and desktop before V2-2). Never edit: tests decode" \
    "// what this version sends with it (V2-2 Review Focus 1)." \
    "// ignore_for_file: type=lint"
  git show 5e04dc8:packages/shellx_core/lib/src/protocol/messages.dart \
    | sed "s#import '../util/cbor_values.dart';#import 'package:shellx_core/src/util/cbor_values.dart';#"
} > packages/shellx_core/test/protocol/frozen/messages_v1.dart
````

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the failing test**

`packages/shellx_core/test/protocol/ai_messages_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:shellx_core/shellx_core.dart';
import 'package:test/test.dart';

import 'frozen/messages_v1.dart' as v1;

Uint8List _json(Object value) =>
    Uint8List.fromList(utf8.encode(jsonEncode(value)));

/// V2-2 §4: the `ai.*` messages, and what every older peer does with them.
void main() {
  final body = _json({'v': 1});
  final samples = <AiMessage, Map<String, Object?>>{
    const AiList(): {'t': 'ai.list'},
    AiListResult(available: true, body: body): {
      't': 'ai.list.result',
      'available': true,
      'body': body,
    },
    const AiAttach(sid: 'e.1'): {'t': 'ai.attach', 'sid': 'e.1'},
    AiAttach(sid: 'e.1', body: body): {
      't': 'ai.attach',
      'sid': 'e.1',
      'body': body,
    },
    const AiDetach(sid: 'e.1'): {'t': 'ai.detach', 'sid': 'e.1'},
    const AiAck(sid: 'e.1', seq: 9): {'t': 'ai.ack', 'sid': 'e.1', 'seq': 9},
    const AiEarlier(sid: 'e.1', before: 40): {
      't': 'ai.earlier',
      'sid': 'e.1',
      'before': 40,
    },
    AiSnapshot(sid: 'e.1', seq: 1, body: body): {
      't': 'ai.snapshot',
      'sid': 'e.1',
      'seq': 1,
      'body': body,
    },
    AiUpdate(sid: 'e.1', seq: 2, body: body): {
      't': 'ai.update',
      'sid': 'e.1',
      'seq': 2,
      'body': body,
    },
    const AiClosed(sid: 'e.1', reason: 'gone'): {
      't': 'ai.closed',
      'sid': 'e.1',
      'reason': 'gone',
    },
    const AiPrompt(sid: 'e.1', rid: 3, text: 'Sửa lỗi'): {
      't': 'ai.prompt',
      'sid': 'e.1',
      'rid': 3,
      'text': 'Sửa lỗi',
    },
    const AiAnswer(sid: 'e.1', rid: 4, permission: 7, option: 'allow'): {
      't': 'ai.answer',
      'sid': 'e.1',
      'rid': 4,
      'permission': 7,
      'option': 'allow',
    },
    AiAnswer(sid: 'e.1', rid: 5, ask: 2, action: 'accept', body: body): {
      't': 'ai.answer',
      'sid': 'e.1',
      'rid': 5,
      'ask': 2,
      'action': 'accept',
      'body': body,
    },
    const AiCancel(sid: 'e.1', rid: 6): {
      't': 'ai.cancel',
      'sid': 'e.1',
      'rid': 6,
    },
    const AiForceStop(sid: 'e.1', rid: 7): {
      't': 'ai.stop',
      'sid': 'e.1',
      'rid': 7,
    },
    const AiUnqueue(sid: 'e.1', rid: 8, queued: 11): {
      't': 'ai.unqueue',
      'sid': 'e.1',
      'rid': 8,
      'queued': 11,
    },
    const AiOpen(rid: 9, agent: 'claude', folder: 'ws:abc'): {
      't': 'ai.open',
      'rid': 9,
      'agent': 'claude',
      'folder': 'ws:abc',
    },
    const AiResult(rid: 9, ok: true, sid: 'e.4'): {
      't': 'ai.result',
      'rid': 9,
      'ok': true,
      'sid': 'e.4',
    },
    const AiResult(rid: 10, ok: false, code: 'stale'): {
      't': 'ai.result',
      'rid': 10,
      'ok': false,
      'code': 'stale',
    },
  };

  test('every ai.* message has the exact map and round-trips', () {
    for (final MapEntry(key: message, value: map) in samples.entries) {
      expect(message.toMap(), map, reason: message.type);
      expect(decodeBatch(encodeBatch([message])), [
        message,
      ], reason: message.type);
    }
  });

  test('toString never shows a prompt, a folder or a body', () {
    for (final message in samples.keys) {
      final text = message.toString();
      expect(text, isNot(contains('Sửa')));
      expect(text, isNot(contains('ws:abc')));
      expect(text, isNot(contains('bytes')));
      expect(text, endsWith('(…)'));
    }
  });

  test('a malformed or unknown ai.* message is skipped, never fatal', () {
    final bytes = encodeCbor([
      {'t': 'ack', 'id': 1, 'seq': 2},
      {'t': 'ai.prompt', 'sid': 5, 'rid': 1, 'text': 'x'}, // sid not a string
      {'t': 'ai.ack', 'sid': 'e.1'}, // seq missing
      {'t': 'ai.future', 'x': 1},
      {'t': 'session.reset', 'id': 1},
    ]);
    expect(decodeBatch(bytes), [
      const AckMessage(id: 1, seq: 2),
      const SessionReset(id: 1),
    ]);
  });

  test('a body of any depth stays one byte string (depth 3 on the wire)', () {
    var deep = <String, Object?>{'leaf': 1.5};
    for (var i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
      deep = {'n': deep};
    }
    final message = AiUpdate(sid: 'e.1', seq: 1, body: _json(deep));
    expect(decodeBatch(encodeBatch([message])), [message]);
  });

  test('the largest body fits one sealed batch with its envelope', () {
    final message = AiUpdate(
      sid: 'k3f9za.2147483647',
      seq: 1 << 40,
      body: Uint8List(kAiMaxBodyBytes),
    );
    expect(encodeBatch([message]).length, lessThan(kMaxSealedBatchBytes));
  });

  group('session.list.result carries the ai protocol (D3)', () {
    test('written only when set; read back', () {
      const without = SessionListResult([], desktopSharing: true);
      expect(without.toMap().containsKey('ai'), isFalse);
      const with1 = SessionListResult(
        [],
        desktopSharing: true,
        ai: kAiProtocol,
      );
      expect(with1.toMap()['ai'], 1);
      final decoded = decodeBatch(encodeBatch([with1])).single;
      expect((decoded as SessionListResult).ai, 1);
    });

    test('an older desktop sends none: null', () {
      final bytes = encodeCbor([
        {'t': 'session.list.result', 'sessions': [], 'desktop_sharing': true},
      ]);
      expect((decodeBatch(bytes).single as SessionListResult).ai, isNull);
    });
  });

  group(
    'compatibility with every released peer (frozen decoder at 5e04dc8)',
    () {
      test(
        'an old phone skips every ai.* message and keeps its neighbours',
        () {
          final bytes = encodeBatch([
            const AckMessage(id: 1, seq: 2),
            ...samples.keys,
            const SessionReset(id: 1),
          ]);
          final decoded = v1.decodeBatch(bytes);
          expect(decoded.map((m) => m.type), ['ack', 'session.reset']);
        },
      );

      test('an old phone reads session.list.result with ai as before', () {
        final bytes = encodeBatch([
          const SessionListResult(
            [
              SessionInfo(
                id: 1,
                index: 1,
                shell: 'zsh',
                createdAt: 1,
                attached: false,
              ),
            ],
            desktopSharing: true,
            ai: kAiProtocol,
          ),
        ]);
        final decoded = v1.decodeBatch(bytes).single as v1.SessionListResult;
        expect(decoded.sessions.single.id, 1);
        expect(decoded.desktopSharing, isTrue);
      });

      test('an old desktop skips what a new phone sends', () {
        final bytes = encodeBatch([
          const SessionList(desktop: true),
          const AiList(),
          const AiAttach(sid: 'e.1'),
          const AiPrompt(sid: 'e.1', rid: 1, text: 'x'),
        ]);
        expect(v1.decodeBatch(bytes).map((m) => m.type), ['session.list']);
      });
    },
  );
}
````

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement**

`packages/shellx_core/lib/src/protocol/ai_messages.dart`:

````dart
part of 'messages.dart';

/// V2-2 D3: the `ai.*` protocol a desktop reports in `session.list.result`
/// (`ai`). A phone shows AI sessions only when it is at least 1.
const int kAiProtocol = 1;

/// V2-2 D4: the largest `body` of an `ai.*` message. One sealed batch
/// ([kMaxSealedBatchBytes], 65 493 bytes) holds it with the envelope and
/// room to spare, so an AI message never makes `SecureChannel.seal` throw
/// (which would drop the whole channel, terminals included).
const int kAiMaxBodyBytes = 48 * 1024;

/// V2-2 D4: the largest prompt a phone sends, in UTF-8 bytes.
const int kAiMaxPromptBytes = 32 * 1024;

/// V2-2: messages between a phone and the AI sessions of a desktop (spec
/// V2-2 §4). Their content travels as [body] bytes (UTF-8 JSON, read by
/// `package:shellx_ai/remote.dart`), so the transport sees a flat map of
/// depth 3 whatever the content is, and never a float.
///
/// An `ai.*` message this version cannot read is skipped ([AppMessage.
/// fromMap] returns null), never fatal: only the AI view resynchronises,
/// the channel and its terminals stay. [toString] never shows a prompt, a
/// folder or a body (V2-1 D20).
sealed class AiMessage extends AppMessage {
  const AiMessage();

  /// The AI session the message is about; null for `ai.list`,
  /// `ai.list.result`, `ai.open` and `ai.result` without a session.
  String? get sid;

  @override
  String toString() => '$runtimeType(…)';

  /// The `ai.*` types this version reads; anything else under `ai.` is a
  /// newer peer's and is skipped.
  static AiMessage? _fromMap(String t, _Fields f) {
    try {
      return switch (t) {
        'ai.list' => const AiList(),
        'ai.list.result' => AiListResult(
          available: f.boolean('available'),
          body: f.bytes('body'),
        ),
        'ai.attach' => AiAttach(
          sid: f.string('sid'),
          body: f.optionalBytes('body'),
        ),
        'ai.detach' => AiDetach(sid: f.string('sid')),
        'ai.ack' => AiAck(sid: f.string('sid'), seq: f.integer('seq')),
        'ai.earlier' => AiEarlier(
          sid: f.string('sid'),
          before: f.integer('before'),
        ),
        'ai.snapshot' => AiSnapshot(
          sid: f.string('sid'),
          seq: f.integer('seq'),
          body: f.bytes('body'),
        ),
        'ai.update' => AiUpdate(
          sid: f.string('sid'),
          seq: f.integer('seq'),
          body: f.bytes('body'),
        ),
        'ai.closed' => AiClosed(
          sid: f.string('sid'),
          reason: f.string('reason'),
        ),
        'ai.prompt' => AiPrompt(
          sid: f.string('sid'),
          rid: f.integer('rid'),
          text: f.string('text'),
        ),
        'ai.answer' => AiAnswer(
          sid: f.string('sid'),
          rid: f.integer('rid'),
          permission: f.optionalInteger('permission'),
          option: f.optionalString('option'),
          ask: f.optionalInteger('ask'),
          action: f.optionalString('action'),
          body: f.optionalBytes('body'),
        ),
        'ai.cancel' => AiCancel(sid: f.string('sid'), rid: f.integer('rid')),
        'ai.stop' => AiForceStop(sid: f.string('sid'), rid: f.integer('rid')),
        'ai.unqueue' => AiUnqueue(
          sid: f.string('sid'),
          rid: f.integer('rid'),
          queued: f.integer('queued'),
        ),
        'ai.open' => AiOpen(
          rid: f.integer('rid'),
          agent: f.string('agent'),
          folder: f.string('folder'),
        ),
        'ai.result' => AiResult(
          rid: f.integer('rid'),
          ok: f.boolean('ok'),
          code: f.optionalString('code'),
          sid: f.optionalString('sid'),
        ),
        _ => null,
      };
    } on FormatException {
      return null; // Skipped, never fatal (see [AiMessage]).
    }
  }
}

/// Phone → desktop: list the AI sessions and keep the list current (the
/// desktop sends `ai.list.result` again whenever it changes, until the
/// channel ends).
final class AiList extends AiMessage {
  const AiList();
  @override
  String? get sid => null;
  @override
  String get type => 'ai.list';
  @override
  Map<String, Object?> toMap() => {'t': type};
}

/// Desktop → phone: the AI sessions, agents and folders ([body], an
/// `AiListing`); [available] is false while no window serves AI (the
/// desktop app is closed and only the background service runs).
final class AiListResult extends AiMessage {
  const AiListResult({required this.available, required this.body});
  final bool available;
  final Uint8List body;
  @override
  String? get sid => null;
  @override
  String get type => 'ai.list.result';
  @override
  Map<String, Object?> toMap() => {
    't': type,
    'available': available,
    'body': body,
  };
}

/// Phone → desktop: mirror session [sid]. [body] (an `AiHave`) says what
/// the phone already holds, so a re-attach only brings what changed.
final class AiAttach extends AiMessage {
  const AiAttach({required this.sid, this.body});
  @override
  final String sid;
  final Uint8List? body;
  @override
  String get type => 'ai.attach';
  @override
  Map<String, Object?> toMap() => {
    't': type,
    'sid': sid,
    if (body != null) 'body': body,
  };
}

/// Phone → desktop: stop mirroring [sid].
final class AiDetach extends AiMessage {
  const AiDetach({required this.sid});
  @override
  final String sid;
  @override
  String get type => 'ai.detach';
  @override
  Map<String, Object?> toMap() => {'t': type, 'sid': sid};
}

/// Phone → desktop: every mirror message of [sid] up to [seq] arrived
/// (cumulative); it opens the desktop's send window.
final class AiAck extends AiMessage {
  const AiAck({required this.sid, required this.seq});
  @override
  final String sid;
  final int seq;
  @override
  String get type => 'ai.ack';
  @override
  Map<String, Object?> toMap() => {'t': type, 'sid': sid, 'seq': seq};
}

/// Phone → desktop: the items of [sid] before index [before] ("load
/// earlier"); answered with an `ai.update` page.
final class AiEarlier extends AiMessage {
  const AiEarlier({required this.sid, required this.before});
  @override
  final String sid;
  final int before;
  @override
  String get type => 'ai.earlier';
  @override
  Map<String, Object?> toMap() => {'t': type, 'sid': sid, 'before': before};
}

/// Desktop → phone: the first mirror message after an attach.
final class AiSnapshot extends AiMessage {
  const AiSnapshot({required this.sid, required this.seq, required this.body});
  @override
  final String sid;
  final int seq;
  final Uint8List body;
  @override
  String get type => 'ai.snapshot';
  @override
  Map<String, Object?> toMap() => {
    't': type,
    'sid': sid,
    'seq': seq,
    'body': body,
  };
}

/// Desktop → phone: what changed, a continuation, or an earlier page.
final class AiUpdate extends AiMessage {
  const AiUpdate({required this.sid, required this.seq, required this.body});
  @override
  final String sid;
  final int seq;
  final Uint8List body;
  @override
  String get type => 'ai.update';
  @override
  Map<String, Object?> toMap() => {
    't': type,
    'sid': sid,
    'seq': seq,
    'body': body,
  };
}

/// Desktop → phone: [sid] is no longer mirrored. [reason]: `gone` (the tab
/// closed), `desktop_closed` (the window quit), `unavailable` (no window
/// serves AI); a newer desktop may send other words.
final class AiClosed extends AiMessage {
  const AiClosed({required this.sid, required this.reason});
  @override
  final String sid;
  final String reason;
  @override
  String get type => 'ai.closed';
  @override
  Map<String, Object?> toMap() => {'t': type, 'sid': sid, 'reason': reason};
}

/// Phone → desktop: send [text] to [sid] (queued while a turn runs).
final class AiPrompt extends AiMessage {
  const AiPrompt({required this.sid, required this.rid, required this.text});
  @override
  final String sid;
  final int rid;
  final String text;
  @override
  String get type => 'ai.prompt';
  @override
  Map<String, Object?> toMap() => {
    't': type,
    'sid': sid,
    'rid': rid,
    'text': text,
  };
}

/// Phone → desktop: the user's answer. Either [permission] with the
/// [option] picked, or [ask] with [action] (`accept`, `decline`,
/// `cancel`) and, for `accept`, the form values as [body] (JSON).
final class AiAnswer extends AiMessage {
  const AiAnswer({
    required this.sid,
    required this.rid,
    this.permission,
    this.option,
    this.ask,
    this.action,
    this.body,
  });
  @override
  final String sid;
  final int rid;
  final int? permission;
  final String? option;
  final int? ask;
  final String? action;
  final Uint8List? body;
  @override
  String get type => 'ai.answer';
  @override
  Map<String, Object?> toMap() => {
    't': type,
    'sid': sid,
    'rid': rid,
    if (permission != null) 'permission': permission,
    if (option != null) 'option': option,
    if (ask != null) 'ask': ask,
    if (action != null) 'action': action,
    if (body != null) 'body': body,
  };
}

/// Phone → desktop: stop the turn of [sid] (the desktop's Esc).
final class AiCancel extends AiMessage {
  const AiCancel({required this.sid, required this.rid});
  @override
  final String sid;
  final int rid;
  @override
  String get type => 'ai.cancel';
  @override
  Map<String, Object?> toMap() => {'t': type, 'sid': sid, 'rid': rid};
}

/// Phone → desktop: "Dừng hẳn" (only once the desktop offers it).
final class AiForceStop extends AiMessage {
  const AiForceStop({required this.sid, required this.rid});
  @override
  final String sid;
  final int rid;
  @override
  String get type => 'ai.stop';
  @override
  Map<String, Object?> toMap() => {'t': type, 'sid': sid, 'rid': rid};
}

/// Phone → desktop: drop queued prompt [queued] of [sid].
final class AiUnqueue extends AiMessage {
  const AiUnqueue({required this.sid, required this.rid, required this.queued});
  @override
  final String sid;
  final int rid;
  final int queued;
  @override
  String get type => 'ai.unqueue';
  @override
  Map<String, Object?> toMap() => {
    't': type,
    'sid': sid,
    'rid': rid,
    'queued': queued,
  };
}

/// Phone → desktop: a new AI session with [agent] in [folder] (a key from
/// the listing, never a path the phone made up).
final class AiOpen extends AiMessage {
  const AiOpen({required this.rid, required this.agent, required this.folder});
  final int rid;
  final String agent;
  final String folder;
  @override
  String? get sid => null;
  @override
  String get type => 'ai.open';
  @override
  Map<String, Object?> toMap() => {
    't': type,
    'rid': rid,
    'agent': agent,
    'folder': folder,
  };
}

/// Desktop → phone: how request [rid] ended. [code] when not [ok]:
/// `stale`, `refused`, `queue_full`, `too_large`, `busy`, `not_found`,
/// `unavailable` (a newer desktop may send other words). [sid]: the new
/// session of an `ai.open`.
final class AiResult extends AiMessage {
  const AiResult({required this.rid, required this.ok, this.code, this.sid});
  final int rid;
  final bool ok;
  final String? code;
  @override
  final String? sid;
  @override
  String get type => 'ai.result';
  @override
  Map<String, Object?> toMap() => {
    't': type,
    'rid': rid,
    'ok': ok,
    if (code != null) 'code': code,
    if (sid != null) 'sid': sid,
  };
}
````

- [ ] **Step 6: Ledger rulings**

Append to `docs/superpowers/ledgers/v2-ai.md`:

````markdown
## V2-2a (phone) — planning rulings

Plan: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-14-v2-2-phone-ai.md` (spec `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-v2-2-phone-ai-design.md`). The user is away and asked for both apps complete by morning; the controller fast-tracks as in V2-1 (no separate plan-review stop).

Ruling P1: one hub in the window — in-process router → slot → hub; with the background service router → slot → MuxAiForwarder → mux → MuxAiBinding → hub; the daemon never reads a body — cost if wrong: one local hop (~1 ms).
Ruling P2: the phone renders mirror items cut for a phone, not TimelineItems; questions keep their ACP shape — cost if wrong: ~7 small classes.
Ruling P3: JSON body bytes in a flat CBOR envelope; an unreadable ai.* message is skipped, never fatal — cost if wrong: one more decode step.
Ruling P4: no byte chunking; every message ≤ 48 KiB; text as appended pieces; cards cut (head/tail, diff regions, ≤ 3 diffs), else a big stub — cost if wrong: a huge card is read on the computer.
Ruling P5: resync by what the phone holds (generation + revs), not by sequence replay — cost if wrong: changed items are resent after a gap.
Ruling P6: pushes for needs-input and long-turn-done only; none while a phone has the session open or the window shows it; ≤ 1 per session per 30 s; listing phones still get them (in-app banner) — cost if wrong: one banner too many.
Ruling P7: answering on the notification itself is V2-2b (relay category / data-only messages, native plugin, background channel) — cost if wrong: two taps instead of one on a locked phone.
Ruling P8: no config message; Bypass permissions unreachable from a phone — cost if wrong: the mode is switched on the computer.
Ruling P9: new sessions only with ready agents in folders the desktop offers (ws:/path: keys); the new tab is not selected — cost if wrong: a root to set on the computer first.
Ruling P10: the AI session is its own route, not a terminal tab — cost if wrong: one tap (✦ or picker) to reach it.
Ruling P11: attaching to a restored, not-started tab starts its agent — cost if wrong: an agent starts when only looked at on the phone.
Ruling P12: undo, changes panel, images, voice, history and config stay V2-2b — cost if wrong: they stay on the computer for now.
````

### Task 2: `package:shellx_ai/remote.dart` — wire format, phone mirror, per-phone subscription [independent of V2-1b]

The desktop side and the phone side of spec D4–D6, both pure Dart.
- **`item_codec.dart`:** turns `TimelineItem`s and the session header into JSON records cut to phone size.
  - output: 4 KiB head + 4 KiB tail, terminal escapes removed;
  - diffs: region with 3 lines of context, ≤ 3 per card;
  - questions: in their ACP shape;
  - a card too big after cutting it harder: a `big` stub.
- **`AiSubscription`:** one per phone per session. It keeps what that phone was sent and makes the bodies:
  - a snapshot of the last 60 items (or what the phone holds, `AiHave`);
  - deltas by `rev`, with streaming text as appended pieces;
  - "load earlier" pages;
  - bodies ≤ 48 KiB, ~256 KiB unacknowledged at most.
- **`AiMirror`:** the phone's copy. It applies the bodies in order and answers false on a gap.

The listing types (`AiListing`, `AiSessionInfo`, …) live here too, so the phone and the hub share them.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/shellx_ai/lib/remote.dart`
- Create: `packages/shellx_ai/lib/src/remote/wire.dart`, `listing.dart`, `acp_out.dart`, `item_codec.dart`, `mirror.dart`, `subscription.dart`
- Test: `packages/shellx_ai/test/remote/remote_codec_test.dart`, `packages/shellx_ai/test/remote/remote_sync_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: V2-1a Task 5 (`AiSessionState`, `Timeline`, `TimelineItem` family, `AiNotice` family, `FileChange`); `shellx_acp` types (`ElicitationRequest`, `PermissionOption`, `PermissionKind`, `ToolKind`, `ToolCallStatus`, `ToolLocation`, `PlanEntry`, `PlanStatus`, `Usage`, `Redacted`).
- Produces (`package:shellx_ai/remote.dart`; no `dart:io`; every type's `toString()` is `'…'`):
  - wire:
    - constants `kAiWireVersion = 1`, `kAiBodyBytes = 48 * 1024`, `kAiWindowBytes = 256 * 1024`, `kAiAttachItems = 60`, `kAiPageItems = 60`, `kAiRecordBytes`;
    - `Uint8List encodeBody(Map<String, Object?>)`, `Map<String, Object?>? decodeBody(Uint8List?)` (never throws);
    - `int utf8Length(String)`.
  - listing:
    - `enum AiRemoteState { running, needsInput, idle, starting, stopped, needsAuth, failed, noFolder, agentOff }` with `wire` and `static parse(String?)` (unknown → `idle`);
    - `AiRemoteState aiRemoteStateOf(AiSessionState)`, `String? aiWaitingOf(AiSessionState)`.
  - listing classes (each with `toJson` and a `static fromJson` that answers null without its id/key):
    - `AiSessionInfo({required sid, agentId, agentName, title, state, cwd, since, waiting, acpSessionId})`;
    - `AiAgentChoice({required id, name, ready = true})`;
    - `AiFolderChoice({required key, name, path})`;
    - `AiListing({available = true, sessions, agents, folders, defaultAgent})` with `static const unavailable`, `int get needsInput`, `toJson()`, `static AiListing fromJson(Map, {required bool available})`.
  - `Map<String, Object?> elicitationToJson(ElicitationRequest)` (ACP `elicitation/create` shape).
  - `String? itemText(TimelineItem)`, `Map<String, Object?> encodeItem(TimelineItem, int index)`, `Map<String, Object?> encodeHead(AiSessionState, int Function(TimelineItem) indexOf)`.
  - mirror items:
    - `sealed class MirrorItem { int index, rev, turn; }`;
    - `MirrorText(…, MirrorTextKind kind, String text, {int? fullLength})` with `enum MirrorTextKind { user, agent, thought }`;
    - `MirrorTool(…, {required toolCallId, kind, title, status, end, startedAt, endedAt, permission, answerKind, answerName, locations, output, diffs, moreDiffs, input})`;
    - `MirrorAsk(…, {required id, required status, request})` with `open`;
    - `MirrorTurnEnd(…, {required outcome, required elapsed, stopReason, error, files, plus, minus})`;
    - `MirrorNotice(…, {required kind, args, resolved})`;
    - `MirrorUnknown(…, String? kind)` (also `big`).
  - mirror parts:
    - `enum MirrorPermissionState { waiting, answered, cancelled }`;
    - `MirrorOutput({head, tail, lines, cut})`, `MirrorDiff({path, newText, oldText, plus, minus, cut})`;
    - `MirrorTurn({index, startedAt, stopping, canForceStop})`;
    - `MirrorPermission({id, title, options, item, more})`, `MirrorAskRef({id, item, more})`, `MirrorQueued(id, text)`.
  - `MirrorHead({phase, agentId, agentName, title, cwd, turn, permission, ask, queue, usage, plan, modeId, modeName, unavailable})` with `running`, `canSend`, `static fromJson`.
  - `AiHave({generation, from, revs})` with `toJson`, `static fromJson`.
  - `final class AiMirror`:
    - fields: `generation`, `from`, `end`, `count`, `hasEarlier`, `head`, `items`, `changed`, `version`;
    - `MirrorItem? at(int)`, `AiHave get have`, `void clear()`, `bool apply(Map<String, Object?> body, {required bool snapshot})`.
  - `AiOutgoing(int seq, Uint8List body, {bool snapshot = false})`.
  - `final class AiSubscription`:
    - constructor: `AiSubscription(AiSessionState state, {required int generation, int attachItems, int pageItems, int windowBytes})`;
    - state: `unackedBytes`, `canSend`, `dirty`, `from`;
    - `List<AiOutgoing> attach(AiHave?)`, `List<AiOutgoing> delta()`, `List<AiOutgoing> earlier(int before)`, `void ack(int seq)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

`packages/shellx_ai/test/remote/remote_codec_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:shellx_acp/shellx_acp.dart';
import 'package:shellx_ai/remote.dart';
import 'package:shellx_ai/shellx_ai.dart';
import 'package:shellx_ai/src/remote/wire.dart'
    show clipText, headLines, plainText, tailLines, utf8Cut;
import 'package:test/test.dart';

final t0 = DateTime.utc(2026, 10, 14, 9);

AiSessionState fresh() =>
    AiSessionState(agentId: 'claude', agentName: 'Claude', cwd: '/p');

void update(AiSessionState s, Map<String, Object?> json) =>
    s.applyUpdate(SessionUpdate.fromJson(json), t0);

void say(AiSessionState s, String text) => update(s, {
  'sessionUpdate': 'agent_message_chunk',
  'content': {'type': 'text', 'text': text},
});

void tool(AiSessionState s, String id, Map<String, Object?> fields) =>
    update(s, {'sessionUpdate': 'tool_call', 'toolCallId': id, ...fields});

PermissionRequest perm(String toolCallId) => PermissionRequest.fromJson({
  'sessionId': 's',
  'toolCall': {'toolCallId': toolCallId, 'title': 'Edit main.ts'},
  'options': [
    {'optionId': 'allow', 'name': 'Yes', 'kind': 'allow_once'},
    {
      'optionId': 'always',
      'name': 'Yes, allow all edits',
      'kind': 'allow_always',
    },
    {'optionId': 'reject', 'name': 'No', 'kind': 'reject_once'},
  ],
});

/// Feeds every body to [mirror] in order, like the phone does.
bool feed(AiMirror mirror, List<AiOutgoing> out) {
  var ok = true;
  for (final o in out) {
    final body = decodeBody(o.body)!;
    expect(o.body.length, lessThanOrEqualTo(kAiBodyBytes));
    ok = mirror.apply(body, snapshot: o.snapshot) && ok;
  }
  return ok;
}

/// The mirror shows exactly what the desktop's timeline holds from [from].
void expectSame(AiMirror mirror, AiSessionState s) {
  final items = s.timeline.items;
  expect(mirror.end, items.length);
  for (var i = mirror.from; i < items.length; i++) {
    final m = mirror.at(i)!;
    expect(m.rev, items[i].rev, reason: 'rev $i');
    final text = itemText(items[i]);
    if (text != null) expect((m as MirrorText).text, text, reason: 'text $i');
  }
}

void main() {
  group('wire helpers', () {
    test('a UTF-8 cut never splits a character or a surrogate pair', () {
      const s = 'aé😀b';
      expect(utf8Length(s), 1 + 2 + 4 + 1);
      expect(utf8Cut(s, 3), 2); // "aé"
      expect(utf8Cut(s, 6), 2); // the emoji (4 bytes) does not fit
      expect(utf8Cut(s, 7), 4);
      expect(clipText('Sửa lỗi giỏ hàng', 8), 'Sửa…');
    });

    test('head and tail keep whole lines; escapes are stripped', () {
      final text = List.generate(100, (i) => 'line $i').join('\n');
      final head = headLines(text, 30);
      final tail = tailLines(text, 30);
      expect(head.split('\n').every((l) => l.startsWith('line ')), isTrue);
      expect(text.startsWith(head), isTrue);
      expect(text.endsWith(tail), isTrue);
      expect(tail.split('\n').first, startsWith('line '));
      expect(plainText('\x1b[32mok\x1b[0m done\x1b]0;title\x07'), 'ok done');
    });
  });

  group('listing', () {
    test('round-trips; an unknown state reads as idle', () {
      const listing = AiListing(
        sessions: [
          AiSessionInfo(
            sid: 'e.1',
            agentId: 'claude',
            agentName: 'Claude',
            title: 'Nút đăng xuất',
            state: AiRemoteState.needsInput,
            cwd: '/Users/me/shop',
            since: 5,
            waiting: 'Sửa src/main.ts',
            acpSessionId: 'acp-1',
          ),
        ],
        agents: [AiAgentChoice(id: 'claude', name: 'Claude')],
        folders: [
          AiFolderChoice(key: 'ws:a', name: 'shop', path: '/Users/me/shop'),
        ],
        defaultAgent: 'claude',
      );
      final back = AiListing.fromJson(
        jsonDecode(jsonEncode(listing.toJson())) as Map<String, Object?>,
        available: true,
      );
      expect(back.toJson(), listing.toJson());
      expect(back.needsInput, 1);
      final odd = AiSessionInfo.fromJson({'sid': 'e.2', 'state': 'dreaming'})!;
      expect(odd.state, AiRemoteState.idle);
      expect(AiSessionInfo.fromJson({'state': 'idle'}), isNull);
    });

    test('state of a session: a waiting question wins', () {
      final s = fresh();
      expect(aiRemoteStateOf(s), AiRemoteState.stopped);
      s.setPhase(AgentPhase.ready);
      expect(aiRemoteStateOf(s), AiRemoteState.idle);
      s.beginTurn(const [TextBlock('go')], t0);
      expect(aiRemoteStateOf(s), AiRemoteState.running);
      s.addPermission(perm('c1'), t0);
      expect(aiRemoteStateOf(s), AiRemoteState.needsInput);
      expect(aiWaitingOf(s), 'Edit main.ts');
    });

    test('toString never shows a title', () {
      const info = AiSessionInfo(
        sid: 'e.1',
        agentId: 'claude',
        agentName: 'Claude',
        title: 'secret',
        state: AiRemoteState.idle,
      );
      expect(
        '$info ${const AiListing(sessions: [info])}',
        isNot(contains('secret')),
      );
    });
  });

  group('questions travel in their ACP shape (D7)', () {
    test(
      'every field kind round-trips through ElicitationRequest.fromJson',
      () {
        final request =
            ElicitationRequest.fromJson({
                  'mode': 'form',
                  'message': 'Pick',
                  'requestedSchema': {
                    'type': 'object',
                    'title': 'T',
                    'properties': {
                      'name': {
                        'type': 'string',
                        'minLength': 1,
                        'maxLength': 9,
                        'default': 'a',
                      },
                      'n': {'type': 'integer', 'minimum': 1, 'maximum': 5},
                      'ok': {'type': 'boolean', 'default': true},
                      'one': {
                        'type': 'string',
                        'oneOf': [
                          {'const': 'x', 'title': 'X'},
                          {'const': 'y', 'title': 'Y'},
                        ],
                      },
                      'many': {
                        'type': 'array',
                        'items': {
                          'enum': ['p', 'q'],
                        },
                        'maxItems': 2,
                      },
                      'odd': {'type': 'object'},
                    },
                    'required': ['name', 'one'],
                  },
                })
                as ElicitationForm;
        final back =
            ElicitationRequest.fromJson(elicitationToJson(request))
                as ElicitationForm;
        expect(back.message, 'Pick');
        expect(back.title, 'T');
        expect(
          back.fields.map((f) => (f.key, f.required, f.runtimeType)).toList(),
          request.fields
              .map((f) => (f.key, f.required, f.runtimeType))
              .toList(),
        );
        expect(back.supported, isFalse); // 'odd' stays unsupported
        expect(
          back.accepts({
            'name': 'b',
            'one': 'x',
            'many': ['p'],
          }),
          isTrue,
        );
        expect(back.accepts({'name': 'b', 'one': 'z'}), isFalse);
        final url =
            ElicitationRequest.fromJson(
                  elicitationToJson(
                    const ElicitationUrl(
                      message: 'Sign in',
                      elicitationId: 'e1',
                      url: 'https://example.com',
                    ),
                  ),
                )
                as ElicitationUrl;
        expect((url.elicitationId, url.url), ('e1', 'https://example.com'));
      },
    );
  });

  group('items for the phone (§4.4)', () {
    test('a long tool output keeps its first and last lines, plain', () {
      final s = fresh()..beginTurn(const [TextBlock('run')], t0);
      final out = List.generate(
        5000,
        (i) => '\x1b[2mline $i\x1b[0m',
      ).join('\n');
      tool(s, 'c1', {
        'title': 'npm test',
        'kind': 'execute',
        'status': 'completed',
        'content': [
          {
            'type': 'content',
            'content': {'type': 'text', 'text': out},
          },
        ],
      });
      final r = encodeItem(s.timeline.items[1], 1);
      final o = r['out'] as Map<String, Object?>;
      expect(o['cut'], isTrue);
      expect(o['lines'], 5000);
      expect(o['head'] as String, startsWith('line 0\nline 1\n'));
      expect(o['tail'] as String, endsWith('line 4999'));
      expect('${o['head']}${o['tail']}', isNot(contains('\x1b')));
      expect(r['end'], 'completed');
    });

    test('a diff keeps the changed lines and 3 around them', () {
      final s = fresh()..beginTurn(const [TextBlock('edit')], t0);
      final old = List.generate(50, (i) => 'l$i').join('\n');
      final neu = old.replaceFirst('l20', 'L20').replaceFirst('l21', 'L21');
      tool(s, 'c1', {
        'title': 'Edit a.ts',
        'kind': 'edit',
        'content': [
          {'type': 'diff', 'path': '/p/a.ts', 'oldText': old, 'newText': neu},
          {
            'type': 'diff',
            'path': '/p/new.ts',
            'oldText': null,
            'newText': 'x\ny',
          },
        ],
      });
      final diffs = (encodeItem(s.timeline.items[1], 1)['diffs'] as List)
          .cast<Map<String, Object?>>();
      expect(diffs[0]['old'], 'l17\nl18\nl19\nl20\nl21\nl22\nl23\nl24');
      expect(diffs[0]['new'], 'l17\nl18\nl19\nL20\nL21\nl22\nl23\nl24');
      expect((diffs[0]['plus'], diffs[0]['minus']), (2, 2));
      expect(diffs[1]['created'], isTrue);
      expect((diffs[1]['plus'], diffs[1]['minus']), (2, 0));
    });

    test('a card too big even when cut harder becomes a big stub', () {
      final s = fresh()..beginTurn(const [TextBlock('edit')], t0);
      tool(s, 'c1', {
        'title': 'x' * 100,
        'kind': 'edit',
        'content': [
          for (var i = 0; i < 3; i++)
            {
              'type': 'diff',
              'path': '/p/$i',
              'oldText': null,
              'newText': '\x01' * 200000,
            },
        ],
      });
      final sub = AiSubscription(s, generation: 1);
      final mirror = AiMirror();
      expect(feed(mirror, sub.attach(null)), isTrue);
      final card = mirror.at(1)!;
      expect(card, anyOf(isA<MirrorTool>(), isA<MirrorUnknown>()));
    });

    test('turn end, notice and question records', () {
      final s = fresh()..setPhase(AgentPhase.ready);
      final turn = s.beginTurn(const [TextBlock('go')], t0);
      s.addAsk(
        ElicitationRequest.fromJson({
          'mode': 'form',
          'message': 'Which?',
          'requestedSchema': {
            'type': 'object',
            'properties': {
              'a': {'type': 'boolean'},
            },
          },
        }),
      );
      s.addNotice(const AgentExited(3));
      s.endTurn(
        outcome: TurnOutcome.finished,
        at: turn.startedAt.add(const Duration(seconds: 41)),
        changes: const [
          FileChange(
            path: 'a',
            kind: ChangeKind.modified,
            added: 40,
            removed: 5,
          ),
          FileChange(path: 'b', kind: ChangeKind.added, added: 4, removed: 0),
        ],
      );
      final sub = AiSubscription(s, generation: 1);
      final mirror = AiMirror();
      feed(mirror, sub.attach(null));
      final ask = mirror.at(1)! as MirrorAsk;
      expect((ask.status, ask.open), ('open', true));
      expect(ask.request, isA<ElicitationForm>());
      final notice = mirror.at(2)! as MirrorNotice;
      expect((notice.kind, notice.args['code']), ('exited', 3));
      final end = mirror.at(3)! as MirrorTurnEnd;
      expect(
        (end.outcome, end.elapsed.inSeconds, end.files, end.plus, end.minus),
        ('finished', 41, 2, 44, 5),
      );
    });
  });
}
````

`packages/shellx_ai/test/remote/remote_sync_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:shellx_acp/shellx_acp.dart';
import 'package:shellx_ai/remote.dart';
import 'package:shellx_ai/shellx_ai.dart';
import 'package:test/test.dart';

final t0 = DateTime.utc(2026, 10, 14, 9);

AiSessionState fresh() =>
    AiSessionState(agentId: 'claude', agentName: 'Claude', cwd: '/p');

void update(AiSessionState s, Map<String, Object?> json) =>
    s.applyUpdate(SessionUpdate.fromJson(json), t0);

void say(AiSessionState s, String text) => update(s, {
  'sessionUpdate': 'agent_message_chunk',
  'content': {'type': 'text', 'text': text},
});

void tool(AiSessionState s, String id, Map<String, Object?> fields) =>
    update(s, {'sessionUpdate': 'tool_call', 'toolCallId': id, ...fields});

PermissionRequest perm(String toolCallId) => PermissionRequest.fromJson({
  'sessionId': 's',
  'toolCall': {'toolCallId': toolCallId, 'title': 'Edit main.ts'},
  'options': [
    {'optionId': 'allow', 'name': 'Yes', 'kind': 'allow_once'},
    {
      'optionId': 'always',
      'name': 'Yes, allow all edits',
      'kind': 'allow_always',
    },
    {'optionId': 'reject', 'name': 'No', 'kind': 'reject_once'},
  ],
});

/// Feeds every body to [mirror] in order, like the phone does.
bool feed(AiMirror mirror, List<AiOutgoing> out) {
  var ok = true;
  for (final o in out) {
    final body = decodeBody(o.body)!;
    expect(o.body.length, lessThanOrEqualTo(kAiBodyBytes));
    ok = mirror.apply(body, snapshot: o.snapshot) && ok;
  }
  return ok;
}

/// The mirror shows exactly what the desktop's timeline holds from [from].
void expectSame(AiMirror mirror, AiSessionState s) {
  final items = s.timeline.items;
  expect(mirror.end, items.length);
  for (var i = mirror.from; i < items.length; i++) {
    final m = mirror.at(i)!;
    expect(m.rev, items[i].rev, reason: 'rev $i');
    final text = itemText(items[i]);
    if (text != null) expect((m as MirrorText).text, text, reason: 'text $i');
  }
}

void main() {
  group('subscription and mirror (D5)', () {
    test('attach, stream, permission: the mirror follows exactly', () {
      final s = fresh()..setPhase(AgentPhase.ready);
      s.beginTurn(const [TextBlock('Thêm nút đăng xuất')], t0);
      final sub = AiSubscription(s, generation: 7);
      final mirror = AiMirror();
      final first = sub.attach(null);
      expect(first.first.snapshot, isTrue);
      expect(feed(mirror, first), isTrue);
      expectSame(mirror, s);
      expect(mirror.head!.turn!.index, 1);

      say(s, 'Mình sẽ ');
      say(s, 'thêm nút.');
      final d = sub.delta();
      expect(d, hasLength(1));
      final record =
          (decodeBody(d.single.body)!['items'] as List).single as Map;
      expect((record['at'], record['add']), (0, 'Mình sẽ thêm nút.'));
      expect(feed(mirror, d), isTrue);
      say(s, ' Xong.');
      final d2 = sub.delta();
      final r2 = (decodeBody(d2.single.body)!['items'] as List).single as Map;
      expect((r2['at'], r2['add']), ('Mình sẽ thêm nút.'.length, ' Xong.'));
      feed(mirror, d2);
      expectSame(mirror, s);

      tool(s, 'c1', {
        'title': 'Edit main.ts',
        'kind': 'edit',
        'status': 'pending',
      });
      s.addPermission(perm('c1'), t0);
      feed(mirror, sub.delta());
      final p = mirror.head!.permission!;
      expect((p.title, p.item), ('Edit main.ts', 2));
      expect(p.options.map((o) => o.kind), [
        PermissionKind.allowOnce,
        PermissionKind.allowAlways,
        PermissionKind.rejectOnce,
      ]);
      expect(
        (mirror.at(2)! as MirrorTool).permission,
        MirrorPermissionState.waiting,
      );
      expect(sub.delta(), isEmpty); // nothing changed
    });

    test('a re-attach with what the phone holds brings only what changed', () {
      final s = fresh()..beginTurn(const [TextBlock('go')], t0);
      for (var i = 0; i < 10; i++) {
        tool(s, 'c$i', {'title': 'Read f$i', 'kind': 'read'});
      }
      final sub = AiSubscription(s, generation: 1);
      final mirror = AiMirror();
      feed(mirror, sub.attach(null));
      final have = AiHave.fromJson(
        jsonDecode(jsonEncode(mirror.have.toJson())) as Map<String, Object?>,
      );
      update(s, {
        'sessionUpdate': 'tool_call_update',
        'toolCallId': 'c3',
        'status': 'completed',
      });
      say(s, 'done');
      final again = sub.attach(have);
      final body = decodeBody(again.single.body)!;
      expect(body['reset'], isFalse);
      expect((body['items'] as List).map((r) => (r as Map)['i']), [4, 11]);
      expect(feed(mirror, again), isTrue);
      expectSame(mirror, s);
    });

    test('another generation, or too much new, starts over', () {
      final s = fresh()..beginTurn(const [TextBlock('go')], t0);
      final sub = AiSubscription(s, generation: 2);
      final mirror = AiMirror();
      feed(mirror, sub.attach(null));
      final stale = AiHave(generation: 1, from: 0, revs: mirror.have.revs);
      final again = decodeBody(sub.attach(stale).first.body)!;
      expect(again['reset'], isTrue);
      for (var i = 0; i < 200; i++) {
        tool(s, 'c$i', {'title': 'Read $i', 'kind': 'read'});
      }
      final far = decodeBody(sub.attach(mirror.have).first.body)!;
      expect((far['reset'], far['from']), (true, 201 - kAiAttachItems));
    });

    test('a 200 KB answer goes in pieces within the window; acks open it', () {
      final s = fresh()..beginTurn(const [TextBlock('write')], t0);
      final sub = AiSubscription(s, generation: 1, windowBytes: 64 * 1024);
      final mirror = AiMirror();
      feed(mirror, sub.attach(null));
      sub.ack(1);
      final para = 'Dòng tiếng Việt có dấu, "trích dẫn" và \\ gạch.\n';
      say(s, para * 4000);
      var rounds = 0;
      while (sub.dirty && rounds < 100) {
        final out = sub.delta();
        expect(sub.unackedBytes, lessThanOrEqualTo(64 * 1024 + kAiBodyBytes));
        expect(feed(mirror, out), isTrue);
        for (final o in out) {
          sub.ack(o.seq);
        }
        rounds++;
      }
      expect(rounds, greaterThan(3));
      expectSame(mirror, s);
    });

    test('load earlier: pages of whole items, continuing downwards', () {
      final s = fresh()..beginTurn(const [TextBlock('go')], t0);
      for (var i = 0; i < 150; i++) {
        tool(s, 'c$i', {'title': 'Read $i', 'kind': 'read'});
      }
      final sub = AiSubscription(s, generation: 1);
      final mirror = AiMirror();
      feed(mirror, sub.attach(null));
      expect((mirror.from, mirror.hasEarlier), (151 - kAiAttachItems, true));
      expect(feed(mirror, sub.earlier(mirror.from)), isTrue);
      expect(mirror.from, 151 - 2 * kAiAttachItems);
      expect(feed(mirror, sub.earlier(mirror.from)), isTrue);
      expect((mirror.from, mirror.hasEarlier), (0, false));
      expectSame(mirror, s);
      expect(sub.earlier(0), isEmpty);
    });

    test('a missing body is found: apply answers false', () {
      final s = fresh()..beginTurn(const [TextBlock('go')], t0);
      final sub = AiSubscription(s, generation: 1);
      final mirror = AiMirror();
      feed(mirror, sub.attach(null));
      say(s, 'one');
      sub.delta(); // lost on the way
      say(s, ' two');
      expect(feed(mirror, sub.delta()), isFalse);
    });

    test('toString of every remote type is …', () {
      final s = fresh()..beginTurn(const [TextBlock('secret')], t0);
      final sub = AiSubscription(s, generation: 1);
      final mirror = AiMirror();
      final out = sub.attach(null);
      feed(mirror, out);
      expect(
        {
          sub,
          mirror,
          out.first,
          mirror.head,
          mirror.at(0),
          mirror.have,
        }.map((o) => '$o').toSet(),
        {'…'},
      );
    });
  });
}
````

````dart
/// wire format, the desktop's per-phone [AiSubscription] and the phone's
/// [AiMirror]. Pure Dart: no file, process or socket access, no Flutter.
````

`packages/shellx_ai/lib/src/remote/wire.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:typed_data';

/// V2-2 §4: the version of the listing and mirror bodies. A body with a
/// higher `v` is still read: unknown keys and item kinds are skipped.
const int kAiWireVersion = 1;

/// The largest body (= shellx_core's `kAiMaxBodyBytes`; this package does
/// not depend on shellx_core, the hub's test pins the two together).
const int kAiBodyBytes = 48 * 1024;

/// Bytes a desktop may have in flight to one phone for one session before
/// it waits for an `ai.ack` (like a terminal's 256 KiB send window).
const int kAiWindowBytes = 256 * 1024;

/// Items a fresh attach brings; older ones come with "load earlier".
const int kAiAttachItems = 60;

/// Items per "load earlier" page.
const int kAiPageItems = 60;

/// The most text one item record carries; a longer text follows in more
/// records (`at` = where the piece starts).
const int kAiTextChunkBytes = 32 * 1024;

/// Tool output kept for the phone: this much from the start and this much
/// from the end, whole lines.
const int kAiOutputBytes = 4 * 1024;

/// A diff side kept for the phone (the changed region and 3 lines around).
const int kAiDiffSideBytes = 4 * 1024;

/// Diffs per tool card; context lines around a changed region.
const int kAiMaxDiffs = 3;
const int kAiDiffContext = 3;

/// The largest item record: a body holds at least one with its envelope.
const int kAiRecordBytes = kAiBodyBytes - 1024;

/// A tool call's input, as JSON text, when it is shown at all.
const int kAiInputBytes = 2 * 1024;

/// A question's form; a bigger one is answered on the computer.
const int kAiAskBytes = 16 * 1024;

/// UTF-8 JSON bytes of [value].
Uint8List encodeBody(Map<String, Object?> value) =>
    Uint8List.fromList(utf8.encode(jsonEncode(value)));

/// The JSON object in [bytes]; null when it is not one (never throws).
Map<String, Object?>? decodeBody(Uint8List? bytes) {
  if (bytes == null) return null;
  try {
    final value = jsonDecode(utf8.decode(bytes));
    return value is Map<String, Object?> ? value : null;
  } on Object {
    return null;
  }
}

/// UTF-8 length of [s] without encoding it.
int utf8Length(String s) {
  var n = 0;
  for (var i = 0; i < s.length; i++) {
    final c = s.codeUnitAt(i);
    if (c < 0x80) {
      n += 1;
    } else if (c < 0x800) {
      n += 2;
    } else if (c >= 0xD800 && c <= 0xDBFF && i + 1 < s.length) {
      final d = s.codeUnitAt(i + 1);
      if (d >= 0xDC00 && d <= 0xDFFF) {
        n += 4;
        i++;
      } else {
        n += 3;
      }
    } else {
      n += 3;
    }
  }
  return n;
}

/// The longest start of [s] (from [from]) within [maxBytes] of UTF-8; it
/// never splits a surrogate pair. Returns the end index (exclusive).
int utf8Cut(String s, int maxBytes, {int from = 0}) {
  var n = 0;
  var i = from;
  while (i < s.length) {
    final c = s.codeUnitAt(i);
    var size = 3;
    var units = 1;
    if (c < 0x80) {
      size = 1;
    } else if (c < 0x800) {
      size = 2;
    } else if (c >= 0xD800 && c <= 0xDBFF && i + 1 < s.length) {
      final d = s.codeUnitAt(i + 1);
      if (d >= 0xDC00 && d <= 0xDFFF) {
        size = 4;
        units = 2;
      }
    }
    if (n + size > maxBytes) break;
    n += size;
    i += units;
  }
  return i;
}

final _escapes = RegExp(
  r'\x1b(?:\[[0-?]*[ -/]*[@-~]|\][^\x07\x1b]*(?:\x07|\x1b\\)?|[@-Z\\-_])',
);

/// [s] without terminal escape sequences (colours, titles): tool output is
/// shown as plain text on the phone.
String plainText(String s) =>
    s.contains('\x1b') ? s.replaceAll(_escapes, '') : s;

/// [s] cut to [maxBytes] of UTF-8, with "…" when it was cut.
String clipText(String s, int maxBytes) {
  if (utf8Length(s) <= maxBytes) return s;
  return '${s.substring(0, utf8Cut(s, maxBytes - 3))}…';
}

/// The first whole lines of [s] within [maxBytes] (at least the start of
/// the first line).
String headLines(String s, int maxBytes) {
  final end = utf8Cut(s, maxBytes);
  if (end >= s.length) return s;
  final nl = s.lastIndexOf('\n', end);
  return s.substring(0, nl > 0 ? nl : end);
}

/// The last whole lines of [s] within [maxBytes].
String tailLines(String s, int maxBytes) {
  var start = s.length;
  var n = 0;
  while (start > 0) {
    final c = s.codeUnitAt(start - 1);
    // A surrogate pair counts 2 + 2 = its 4 UTF-8 bytes.
    final size = c < 0x80
        ? 1
        : (c < 0x800 ? 2 : (c >= 0xD800 && c <= 0xDFFF ? 2 : 3));
    if (n + size > maxBytes) break;
    n += size;
    start--;
  }
  if (start > 0 && start < s.length) {
    final c = s.codeUnitAt(start);
    if (c >= 0xDC00 && c <= 0xDFFF) start++; // never half a pair
  }
  if (start == 0) return s;
  final nl = s.indexOf('\n', start);
  return nl >= 0 && nl + 1 < s.length
      ? s.substring(nl + 1)
      : s.substring(start);
}

// Tolerant readers of a body: a key of the wrong type reads as absent.

String? jString(Map<String, Object?> m, String key) {
  final v = m[key];
  return v is String ? v : null;
}

int? jInt(Map<String, Object?> m, String key) {
  final v = m[key];
  if (v is int) return v;
  if (v is double && v.isFinite && v == v.truncateToDouble()) return v.toInt();
  return null;
}

bool jBool(Map<String, Object?> m, String key) => m[key] == true;

Map<String, Object?>? jMap(Map<String, Object?> m, String key) {
  final v = m[key];
  return v is Map<String, Object?> ? v : null;
}

Iterable<Map<String, Object?>> jMaps(Map<String, Object?> m, String key) {
  final v = m[key];
  return v is List ? v.whereType<Map<String, Object?>>() : const [];
}

List<int> jInts(Map<String, Object?> m, String key) {
  final v = m[key];
  return v is List ? [for (final e in v) e is int ? e : -1] : const [];
}
````

`packages/shellx_ai/lib/src/remote/listing.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:shellx_acp/shellx_acp.dart';

import '../model/session_state.dart';
import 'wire.dart';

/// A session's state in a phone's lists and header (V2-2 §4.3). The words
/// on the wire are [wire]; an unknown word reads as [idle].
enum AiRemoteState {
  running('running'),
  needsInput('needs_input'),
  idle('idle'),
  starting('starting'),
  stopped('stopped'),
  needsAuth('needs_auth'),
  failed('failed'),

  /// The AI tab has no project folder yet (V2-1 D5): chosen on the computer.
  noFolder('no_folder'),

  /// Its agent is switched off or removed in Settings › Agents.
  agentOff('agent_off');

  const AiRemoteState(this.wire);

  final String wire;

  static AiRemoteState parse(String? wire) =>
      values.firstWhere((s) => s.wire == wire, orElse: () => idle);
}

/// What [state] shows a phone: a waiting permission or question wins.
AiRemoteState aiRemoteStateOf(AiSessionState state) => switch (state.phase) {
  AgentPhase.stopped => AiRemoteState.stopped,
  AgentPhase.starting => AiRemoteState.starting,
  AgentPhase.needsAuth => AiRemoteState.needsAuth,
  AgentPhase.failed => AiRemoteState.failed,
  AgentPhase.ready ||
  AgentPhase.running when state.needsInput => AiRemoteState.needsInput,
  AgentPhase.ready => AiRemoteState.idle,
  AgentPhase.running => AiRemoteState.running,
};

/// What the agent waits for, in one line ("Sửa src/main.ts"); null when
/// nothing waits.
String? aiWaitingOf(AiSessionState state) {
  final permission = state.currentPermission;
  if (permission != null) return clipText(permission.title, 240);
  final ask = state.currentAsk;
  if (ask != null) return clipText(ask.request.message, 240);
  return null;
}

/// One AI session in a phone's list.
final class AiSessionInfo with Redacted {
  const AiSessionInfo({
    required this.sid,
    required this.agentId,
    required this.agentName,
    required this.title,
    required this.state,
    this.cwd,
    this.since,
    this.waiting,
    this.acpSessionId,
  });

  /// The desktop's handle for the session (opaque to the phone).
  final String sid;
  final String agentId;

  /// "Claude", "Codex"… (V2-1 D21: never "Claude Code").
  final String agentName;

  /// The tab title (V2-1 D2); empty for a session with none yet.
  final String title;
  final AiRemoteState state;
  final String? cwd;

  /// When the running turn started (ms since epoch), for "Đang chạy · 0:12".
  final int? since;

  /// What the agent waits for while [state] is [AiRemoteState.needsInput].
  final String? waiting;

  /// The agent's own session id: after a desktop restart the phone finds
  /// the same session again under its new [sid].
  final String? acpSessionId;

  Map<String, Object?> toJson() => {
    'sid': sid,
    'agent': agentId,
    'name': agentName,
    'title': title,
    'state': state.wire,
    if (cwd != null) 'cwd': cwd,
    if (since != null) 'since': since,
    if (waiting != null) 'waiting': waiting,
    if (acpSessionId != null) 'acp': acpSessionId,
  };

  /// Null without a `sid`.
  static AiSessionInfo? fromJson(Map<String, Object?> j) {
    final sid = jString(j, 'sid');
    if (sid == null || sid.isEmpty) return null;
    return AiSessionInfo(
      sid: sid,
      agentId: jString(j, 'agent') ?? '',
      agentName: jString(j, 'name') ?? '',
      title: jString(j, 'title') ?? '',
      state: AiRemoteState.parse(jString(j, 'state')),
      cwd: jString(j, 'cwd'),
      since: jInt(j, 'since'),
      waiting: jString(j, 'waiting'),
      acpSessionId: jString(j, 'acp'),
    );
  }
}

/// An agent a phone may start a session with.
final class AiAgentChoice with Redacted {
  const AiAgentChoice({
    required this.id,
    required this.name,
    this.ready = true,
  });

  final String id;
  final String name;

  /// False: installed but not usable now (needs install, Node…); shown
  /// greyed with "Cài trên máy tính".
  final bool ready;

  Map<String, Object?> toJson() => {'id': id, 'name': name, 'ready': ready};

  static AiAgentChoice? fromJson(Map<String, Object?> j) {
    final id = jString(j, 'id');
    if (id == null || id.isEmpty) return null;
    return AiAgentChoice(
      id: id,
      name: jString(j, 'name') ?? id,
      ready: j['ready'] != false,
    );
  }
}

/// A folder a phone may start a session in: a key the desktop made, never
/// a path the phone chose (V2-2 D9).
final class AiFolderChoice with Redacted {
  const AiFolderChoice({required this.key, required this.name, this.path});

  final String key;

  /// The workspace's name, else the folder's.
  final String name;
  final String? path;

  Map<String, Object?> toJson() => {
    'key': key,
    'name': name,
    if (path != null) 'path': path,
  };

  static AiFolderChoice? fromJson(Map<String, Object?> j) {
    final key = jString(j, 'key');
    if (key == null || key.isEmpty) return null;
    return AiFolderChoice(
      key: key,
      name: jString(j, 'name') ?? key,
      path: jString(j, 'path'),
    );
  }
}

/// A desktop's AI sessions, and what a new one may use (`ai.list.result`).
final class AiListing with Redacted {
  const AiListing({
    this.available = true,
    this.sessions = const [],
    this.agents = const [],
    this.folders = const [],
    this.defaultAgent,
  });

  /// No window serves AI (`available: false` on the envelope).
  static const unavailable = AiListing(available: false);

  /// From the envelope (`AiListResult.available`), not the body.
  final bool available;
  final List<AiSessionInfo> sessions;
  final List<AiAgentChoice> agents;
  final List<AiFolderChoice> folders;
  final String? defaultAgent;

  /// Sessions waiting for the user.
  int get needsInput =>
      sessions.where((s) => s.state == AiRemoteState.needsInput).length;

  Map<String, Object?> toJson() => {
    'v': kAiWireVersion,
    'sessions': [for (final s in sessions) s.toJson()],
    'agents': [for (final a in agents) a.toJson()],
    'folders': [for (final f in folders) f.toJson()],
    if (defaultAgent != null) 'default_agent': defaultAgent,
  };

  static AiListing fromJson(
    Map<String, Object?> j, {
    required bool available,
  }) => AiListing(
    available: available,
    sessions: [
      for (final s in jMaps(j, 'sessions')) ?AiSessionInfo.fromJson(s),
    ],
    agents: [for (final a in jMaps(j, 'agents')) ?AiAgentChoice.fromJson(a)],
    folders: [for (final f in jMaps(j, 'folders')) ?AiFolderChoice.fromJson(f)],
    defaultAgent: jString(j, 'default_agent'),
  );
}
````
