`packages/shellx_ai/lib/src/remote/acp_out.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:shellx_acp/shellx_acp.dart';

/// [request] back in the shape of `elicitation/create` params, so the phone
/// reads it with `ElicitationRequest.fromJson` and checks answers with the
/// same `accepts` as the desktop (V2-2 D7). Session and request ids are
/// left out: the desktop answers, not the phone.
Map<String, Object?> elicitationToJson(ElicitationRequest request) =>
    switch (request) {
      ElicitationForm(
        :final message,
        :final title,
        :final description,
        :final fields,
      ) =>
        {
          'mode': 'form',
          'message': message,
          'requestedSchema': {
            'type': 'object',
            'title': ?title,
            'description': ?description,
            'properties': {for (final f in fields) f.key: _field(f)},
            'required': [
              for (final f in fields)
                if (f.required) f.key,
            ],
          },
        },
      ElicitationUrl(:final message, :final elicitationId, :final url) => {
        'mode': 'url',
        'message': message,
        'elicitationId': elicitationId,
        'url': url,
      },
      ElicitationOther(:final message, :final mode) => {
        'mode': ?mode,
        'message': message,
      },
    };

Map<String, Object?> _field(ElicitField f) {
  final common = {
    if (f.title != null) 'title': f.title,
    if (f.description != null) 'description': f.description,
  };
  List<Map<String, Object?>> titled(List<ElicitOption> options) => [
    for (final o in options) {'const': o.value, 'title': o.title},
  ];
  return switch (f) {
    ElicitTextField(
      :final minLength,
      :final maxLength,
      :final format,
      :final defaultValue,
    ) =>
      {
        'type': 'string',
        ...common,
        'minLength': ?minLength,
        'maxLength': ?maxLength,
        'format': ?format,
        'default': ?defaultValue,
      },
    ElicitNumberField(
      :final integer,
      :final minimum,
      :final maximum,
      :final defaultValue,
    ) =>
      {
        'type': integer ? 'integer' : 'number',
        ...common,
        'minimum': ?minimum,
        'maximum': ?maximum,
        'default': ?defaultValue,
      },
    ElicitBoolField(:final defaultValue) => {
      'type': 'boolean',
      ...common,
      'default': ?defaultValue,
    },
    ElicitChoiceField(:final options, :final defaultValue) => {
      'type': 'string',
      ...common,
      'oneOf': titled(options),
      'default': ?defaultValue,
    },
    ElicitMultiChoiceField(
      :final options,
      :final minItems,
      :final maxItems,
      :final defaultValue,
    ) =>
      {
        'type': 'array',
        ...common,
        'items': {'anyOf': titled(options)},
        'minItems': ?minItems,
        'maxItems': ?maxItems,
        'default': ?defaultValue,
      },
    ElicitUnsupportedField(:final type) => {'type': ?type, ...common},
  };
}
````

`packages/shellx_ai/lib/src/remote/item_codec.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:math';

import 'package:shellx_acp/shellx_acp.dart';

import '../model/notices.dart';
import '../model/session_state.dart';
import '../model/timeline.dart';
import 'acp_out.dart';
import 'wire.dart';

/// The text of a text item (user prompt, agent text, thought); null for
/// any other item.
String? itemText(TimelineItem item) => switch (item) {
  UserItem() => item.text,
  StreamedTextItem() => item.text,
  _ => null,
};

/// A text record of item [index]: its text from char [from] (UTF-16 code
/// units), at most [maxBytes] of UTF-8. Returns the record and where the
/// piece ends (= the text's length when all of it went).
(Map<String, Object?>, int) encodeTextPiece(
  TimelineItem item,
  int index, {
  int from = 0,
  int maxBytes = kAiTextChunkBytes,
}) {
  final text = itemText(item) ?? '';
  final start = min(from, text.length);
  final end = utf8Cut(text, maxBytes, from: start);
  return (
    {
      'i': index,
      'r': item.rev,
      'k': switch (item) {
        UserItem() => 'user',
        ThoughtItem() => 'thought',
        _ => 'agent',
      },
      'turn': item.turn,
      'at': start,
      'add': text.substring(start, end),
    },
    end,
  );
}

/// A text piece of item [index] from char [from] as JSON, within
/// [kAiRecordBytes] once escaped (newlines and quotes grow in JSON).
(String, int) encodeTextRecord(TimelineItem item, int index, {int from = 0}) {
  var maxBytes = kAiTextChunkBytes;
  while (true) {
    final (piece, end) = encodeTextPiece(
      item,
      index,
      from: from,
      maxBytes: maxBytes,
    );
    final json = jsonEncode(piece);
    if (utf8Length(json) <= kAiRecordBytes || maxBytes <= 256) {
      return (json, end);
    }
    maxBytes ~/= 2;
  }
}

/// Item [index] as JSON within [kAiRecordBytes]: a tool card is cut harder
/// until it fits, else it becomes a `big` stub ("xem trên máy tính").
String encodeItemRecord(TimelineItem item, int index) {
  if (item is ToolItem) {
    for (final scale in const [1, 2, 4, 8]) {
      final json = jsonEncode({
        'i': index,
        'r': item.rev,
        'turn': item.turn,
        ..._tool(item, scale: scale),
      });
      if (utf8Length(json) <= kAiRecordBytes) return json;
    }
    return jsonEncode({
      'i': index,
      'r': item.rev,
      'turn': item.turn,
      'k': 'big',
    });
  }
  final json = jsonEncode(encodeItem(item, index));
  if (utf8Length(json) <= kAiRecordBytes) return json;
  return jsonEncode({'i': index, 'r': item.rev, 'turn': item.turn, 'k': 'big'});
}

/// Item [index] as the phone gets it (V2-2 §4.4): tool output and diffs are
/// cut to what a phone shows; a text item is its first piece (see
/// [encodeTextPiece]).
Map<String, Object?> encodeItem(TimelineItem item, int index) {
  final head = {'i': index, 'r': item.rev, 'turn': item.turn};
  return switch (item) {
    UserItem() || StreamedTextItem() => encodeTextPiece(item, index).$1,
    ToolItem() => {...head, ..._tool(item)},
    AskItem() => {...head, ..._ask(item)},
    TurnEndItem() => {...head, ..._turnEnd(item)},
    NoticeItem() => {
      ...head,
      'k': 'notice',
      'n': _notice(item.notice),
      if (item.resolved) 'resolved': true,
    },
  };
}

/// [scale] divides every cut (a card still too big is cut harder).
Map<String, Object?> _tool(ToolItem t, {int scale = 1}) {
  final f = t.fields;
  final answer = t.permissionAnswer;
  final texts = <String>[];
  final diffs = <ToolDiff>[];
  for (final c in f.content ?? const <ToolContent>[]) {
    switch (c) {
      case ToolContentBlock(:final block):
        final text = switch (block) {
          TextBlock(:final text) => text,
          ResourceLinkBlock(:final name) => name,
          ResourceBlock(:final text) => text ?? '',
          _ => '',
        };
        if (text.isNotEmpty) texts.add(plainText(text));
      case ToolDiff():
        diffs.add(c);
      case ToolTerminal() || ToolContentOther():
        break;
    }
  }
  final input = f.rawInput;
  String? inputText;
  if (input != null && (f.kind == null || f.kind == ToolKind.other)) {
    try {
      inputText = clipText(jsonEncode(input), kAiInputBytes ~/ scale);
    } on Object {
      inputText = null; // Not JSON: not shown.
    }
  }
  return {
    'k': 'tool',
    'id': t.toolCallId,
    if (f.kind != null) 'tk': f.kind!.wire,
    if (f.title != null) 'title': clipText(f.title!, 600),
    if (f.status != null) 'st': f.status!.wire,
    if (t.end != null) 'end': t.end!.name,
    't0': t.startedAt.millisecondsSinceEpoch,
    if (t.endedAt != null) 't1': t.endedAt!.millisecondsSinceEpoch,
    if (t.awaitingPermission)
      'perm': 'waiting'
    else if (answer != null)
      'perm': 'answered'
    else if (t.permissionCancelled)
      'perm': 'cancelled',
    if (answer != null)
      'ans': {'kind': answer.kind.wire, 'name': clipText(answer.name, 300)},
    if (f.locations case final locations? when locations.isNotEmpty)
      'loc': [
        for (final l in locations.take(5))
          {'path': clipText(l.path, 600), 'line': ?l.line},
      ],
    if (texts.isNotEmpty)
      'out': _output(texts.join('\n'), kAiOutputBytes ~/ scale),
    if (diffs.isNotEmpty)
      'diffs': [
        for (final d in diffs.take(kAiMaxDiffs))
          _diff(d, kAiDiffSideBytes ~/ scale),
      ],
    if (diffs.length > kAiMaxDiffs) 'more_diffs': diffs.length - kAiMaxDiffs,
    'input': ?inputText,
  };
}

Map<String, Object?> _output(String text, int side) {
  final lines = '\n'.allMatches(text).length + 1;
  if (utf8Length(text) <= 2 * side) {
    return {'head': text, 'lines': lines};
  }
  return {
    'head': headLines(text, side),
    'tail': tailLines(text, side),
    'lines': lines,
    'cut': true,
  };
}

/// The changed region of [d] with [kAiDiffContext] lines around it: the
/// lines both sides share at the start and at the end are dropped (V2-2
/// D6). A new file keeps its first lines.
Map<String, Object?> _diff(ToolDiff d, int side) {
  final newLines = const LineSplitter().convert(d.newText);
  final path = clipText(d.path, 600);
  final old = d.oldText;
  if (old == null) {
    final text = headLines(d.newText, side);
    return {
      'path': path,
      'new': text,
      'created': true,
      'plus': newLines.length,
      'minus': 0,
      if (text.length < d.newText.length) 'cut': true,
    };
  }
  final oldLines = const LineSplitter().convert(old);
  var start = 0;
  while (start < oldLines.length &&
      start < newLines.length &&
      oldLines[start] == newLines[start]) {
    start++;
  }
  var endOld = oldLines.length;
  var endNew = newLines.length;
  while (endOld > start &&
      endNew > start &&
      oldLines[endOld - 1] == newLines[endNew - 1]) {
    endOld--;
    endNew--;
  }
  final from = max(0, start - kAiDiffContext);
  final oldRegion = oldLines
      .sublist(from, min(oldLines.length, endOld + kAiDiffContext))
      .join('\n');
  final newRegion = newLines
      .sublist(from, min(newLines.length, endNew + kAiDiffContext))
      .join('\n');
  final oldCut = headLines(oldRegion, side);
  final newCut = headLines(newRegion, side);
  return {
    'path': path,
    'old': oldCut,
    'new': newCut,
    'plus': endNew - start,
    'minus': endOld - start,
    if (oldCut.length < oldRegion.length || newCut.length < newRegion.length)
      'cut': true,
  };
}

Map<String, Object?> _ask(AskItem a) {
  final response = a.response;
  final status = a.autoDeclined
      ? 'auto_declined'
      : switch (response) {
          ElicitAccept() => 'accepted',
          ElicitDecline() => 'declined',
          ElicitCancel() => 'cancelled',
          null => a.completed ? 'completed' : 'open',
        };
  final request = elicitationToJson(a.request);
  final fits = utf8Length(jsonEncode(request)) <= kAiAskBytes;
  return {
    'k': 'ask',
    'id': a.id,
    'st': status,
    if (fits) 'req': request else 'big': true,
  };
}

Map<String, Object?> _turnEnd(TurnEndItem e) {
  final changes = e.changes;
  return {
    'k': 'end',
    'outcome': e.outcome.name,
    'ms': e.elapsed.inMilliseconds,
    if (e.stopReason != null) 'stop': e.stopReason!.wire,
    if (e.error != null) 'error': clipText(e.error!, 1000),
    if (changes != null) ...{
      'files': changes.length,
      'plus': changes.fold<int>(0, (n, c) => n + (c.added ?? 0)),
      'minus': changes.fold<int>(0, (n, c) => n + (c.removed ?? 0)),
    },
  };
}

Map<String, Object?> _notice(AiNotice n) => switch (n) {
  LoginNeeded(:final methods) => {
    'k': 'login',
    'methods': [for (final m in methods.take(5)) clipText(m.name, 200)],
  },
  NodeMissing(:final required, :final found) => {
    'k': 'node',
    'required': required,
    'found': ?found,
  },
  AgentNotInstalled() => {'k': 'not_installed'},
  AgentMissing() => {'k': 'missing'},
  AgentExited(:final exitCode) => {'k': 'exited', 'code': exitCode},
  AgentOutputInvalid() => {'k': 'invalid'},
  StartFailed(:final reason) => {'k': 'start_failed', 'reason': reason.name},
  CannotResume() => {'k': 'cannot_resume'},
  FolderMissing() => {'k': 'folder_missing'},
  UndoUnavailable(:final reason, :final turnOnly) => {
    'k': 'undo_off',
    'reason': reason.name,
    if (turnOnly) 'turn_only': true,
  },
};

/// The session's header for the phone (V2-2 §4.5). [indexOf] gives a
/// timeline item's index (the card a permission or question belongs to).
Map<String, Object?> encodeHead(
  AiSessionState s,
  int Function(TimelineItem item) indexOf,
) {
  final turn = s.turn;
  final permission = s.currentPermission;
  final ask = s.currentAsk;
  final usage = s.usage;
  final mode = _mode(s);
  return {
    'phase': s.phase.name,
    if (s.label case final label?) 'title': clipText(label, 400),
    'agent': s.agentId,
    'name': s.agentName,
    'cwd': s.cwd,
    if (turn != null)
      'turn': {
        'n': turn.index,
        't0': turn.startedAt.millisecondsSinceEpoch,
        if (turn.cancelRequested) 'stopping': true,
        if (turn.canForceStop) 'force': true,
      },
    if (permission != null)
      'perm': {
        'id': permission.id,
        'item': indexOf(permission.item),
        'title': clipText(permission.title, 600),
        'options': [
          for (final o in permission.request.options)
            {
              'id': o.optionId,
              'name': clipText(o.name, 300),
              'kind': o.kind.wire,
            },
        ],
        if (s.permissions.length > 1) 'more': s.permissions.length - 1,
      },
    if (ask != null)
      'ask': {
        'id': ask.id,
        'item': indexOf(ask.item),
        if (s.asks.length > 1) 'more': s.asks.length - 1,
      },
    if (s.queue.isNotEmpty)
      'queue': [
        for (final q in s.queue) {'id': q.id, 'text': clipText(q.text, 400)},
      ],
    if (usage != null) 'usage': {'used': usage.used, 'size': usage.size},
    if (s.plan.isNotEmpty)
      'plan': [
        for (final e in s.plan.take(30))
          {'text': clipText(e.content, 600), 'st': e.status.wire},
      ],
    'mode': ?mode,
  };
}

/// The current mode, by id and name: the phone words Claude's known ids
/// itself (V2-1 D11) and shows other agents' names as sent.
Map<String, Object?>? _mode(AiSessionState s) {
  for (final o in s.configOptions ?? const <ConfigOption>[]) {
    if (o.category != 'mode') continue;
    final value = o.currentValue;
    for (final c in o.choices) {
      if (c.value == value) {
        return {'id': c.value, 'name': clipText(c.name, 200)};
      }
    }
  }
  final modes = s.modes;
  if (modes == null) return null;
  for (final m in modes.available) {
    if (m.id == modes.currentModeId) {
      return {'id': m.id, 'name': clipText(m.name, 200)};
    }
  }
  return null;
}

import 'package:shellx_acp/shellx_acp.dart';

import 'listing.dart';
import 'wire.dart';

/// One conversation item as the phone holds it (V2-2 D5): built from the
/// desktop's records, replaced (never changed) when the desktop sends a
/// newer [rev], so a view keys its widget on `(index, rev)`.
sealed class MirrorItem with Redacted {
  const MirrorItem(this.index, this.rev, this.turn);

  final int index;
  final int rev;
  final int turn;
}

enum MirrorTextKind { user, agent, thought }

final class MirrorText extends MirrorItem {
  const MirrorText(
    super.index,
    super.rev,
    super.turn,
    this.kind,
    this.text, {
    this.fullLength,
  });

  final MirrorTextKind kind;
  final String text;

  /// Set when an earlier page brought only the start of a long text: its
  /// whole length (the rest is read on the computer).
  final int? fullLength;
}

/// What happened to a tool call's permission request.
enum MirrorPermissionState { waiting, answered, cancelled }

/// Tool output: its first and last lines when it was long.

    this.tail = '',
    this.lines = 0,
    this.cut = false,
  });

  final String tail;
  final int lines;

  /// The middle was left on the computer ([lines] in all).

}

/// The changed region of one file (V2-2 D6); [oldText] null: a new file.

    required this.path,
    required this.newText,
    this.oldText,

    this.minus = 0,

}

  });

/// The session's header (V2-2 §4.5).

    required this.agentName,

  /// The session cannot run now ([AiRemoteState.noFolder] or
  /// [AiRemoteState.agentOff]): set up on the computer.
  final AiRemoteState? unavailable;

  bool get running => turn != null;

  /// A prompt can be sent or queued now (V2-1 D16).
  bool get canSend =>
      unavailable == null &&
      phase != 'needsAuth' &&
      phase != 'failed' &&
      queue.length < 5;

  static MirrorHead fromJson(Map<String, Object?> j) {
    final turn = jMap(j, 'turn');

    final ask = jMap(j, 'ask');
    final usage = jMap(j, 'usage');

/// What a phone holds of a session, sent with `ai.attach` so a re-attach
/// only brings what changed (V2-2 D5).
final class AiHave with Redacted {
  const AiHave({
    required this.generation,
    required this.from,
    required this.revs,
  });

  final int generation;
  final int from;

  /// The rev of each held item, from index [from] on.
  final List<int> revs;

  Map<String, Object?> toJson() => {
    'g': generation,
    'from': from,
    'revs': revs,
  };

  static AiHave? fromJson(Map<String, Object?>? j) {
    if (j == null) return null;
    final g = jInt(j, 'g');
    final from = jInt(j, 'from');
    if (g == null || from == null || from < 0) return null;
    return AiHave(generation: g, from: from, revs: jInts(j, 'revs'));
  }
}

/// The phone's copy of one AI session (V2-2 D5): the header and a window of
/// the conversation, `[from, end)`, fed by the desktop's bodies in order.
final class AiMirror with Redacted {
  int _generation = -1;
  int _from = 0;
  int _count = 0;
  bool _hasEarlier = false;
  MirrorHead? _head;
  final List<MirrorItem> _items = [];
  final Set<int> _changed = {};
  int _version = 0;

  int get generation => _generation;

  /// The first index held.
  int get from => _from;

  /// One past the last index held.
  int get end => _from + _items.length;

  /// Items on the desktop when the last body was made.
  int get count => _count;

  /// Older items exist on the desktop ("Tải thêm").
  bool get hasEarlier => _hasEarlier;
  MirrorHead? get head => _head;

  /// The held items, oldest first.
  List<MirrorItem> get items => List.unmodifiable(_items);

  /// Indices the last [apply] added or replaced.
  Set<int> get changed => Set.unmodifiable(_changed);

  /// Goes up on every [apply] that changed something.
  int get version => _version;

  MirrorItem? at(int index) {
    final i = index - _from;
    return i < 0 || i >= _items.length ? null : _items[i];
  }

  AiHave get have => AiHave(
    generation: _generation,
    from: _from,
    revs: [for (final item in _items) item.rev],
  );

  /// Forgets everything (the session closed, or a resync).
  void clear() {
    _generation = -1;
    _from = 0;
    _count = 0;
    _hasEarlier = false;
    _head = null;
    _items.clear();
    _changed.clear();
    _version++;
  }

  /// Applies one body ([snapshot]: the first after an attach). False when
  /// it does not fit what is held (a record past the end, a text piece
  /// that does not continue the text): the caller attaches again.
  bool apply(Map<String, Object?> body, {required bool snapshot}) {
    _changed.clear();
    if (snapshot) {
      final g = jInt(body, 'g') ?? 0;
      final from = jInt(body, 'from') ?? 0;
      if (jBool(body, 'reset') || g != _generation || from != _from) {
        _items.clear();
        _from = from;
      }
      _generation = g;
      _hasEarlier = jBool(body, 'more');
    }
    _count = jInt(body, 'n') ?? _count;
    final head = jMap(body, 'head');
    if (head != null) _head = MirrorHead.fromJson(head);
    final records = jMaps(body, 'items').toList();
    var ok = true;
    if (jBool(body, 'older')) {
      ok = _prepend(records);
      _hasEarlier = jBool(body, 'more');
    } else {
      for (final r in records) {
        if (!_put(r)) {
          ok = false;
          break;
        }
      }
    }
    _version++;
    return ok;
  }

  bool _prepend(List<Map<String, Object?>> records) {
    final page = <MirrorItem>[];
    for (final r in records) {
      final item = _parse(r, null);
      if (item == null) return false;
      page.add(item);
    }
    page.sort((a, b) => a.index.compareTo(b.index));
    for (var i = 0; i < page.length; i++) {
      if (page[i].index != _from - page.length + i) return false;
    }
    _items.insertAll(0, page);
    _from -= page.length;
    _changed.addAll(page.map((p) => p.index));
    return true;
  }

  bool _put(Map<String, Object?> r) {
    final index = jInt(r, 'i');
    if (index == null || index < _from) return index != null; // not held
    if (index > end) return false;
    final current = index < end ? _items[index - _from] : null;
    final item = _parse(r, current);
    if (item == null) return false;
    if (current == null) {
      _items.add(item);
    } else {
      _items[index - _from] = item;
    }
    _changed.add(index);
    return true;
  }

  /// Null when a text piece does not continue [current].
  static MirrorItem? _parse(Map<String, Object?> r, MirrorItem? current) {
    final index = jInt(r, 'i') ?? -1;
    final rev = jInt(r, 'r') ?? 0;
    final turn = jInt(r, 'turn') ?? 0;
    final kind = jString(r, 'k');
    DateTime? time(String key) {
      final ms = jInt(r, key);
      return ms == null ? null : DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(ms);
    }

    switch (kind) {
      case 'user' || 'agent' || 'thought':
        final at = jInt(r, 'at') ?? 0;
        final add = jString(r, 'add') ?? '';
        final before = current is MirrorText ? current.text : '';
        if (at != 0 && at != before.length) return null;
        return MirrorText(
          index,
          rev,
          turn,
          MirrorTextKind.values.byName(kind!),
          at == 0 ? add : before + add,
          fullLength: jInt(r, 'clip'),
        );
      case 'tool':
        final out = jMap(r, 'out');
        final ans = jMap(r, 'ans');
        return MirrorTool(
          index,
          rev,
          turn,
          toolCallId: jString(r, 'id') ?? '',
          kind: ToolKind.parse(jString(r, 'tk')),
          title: jString(r, 'title'),
          status: ToolCallStatus.parse(jString(r, 'st')),
          end: jString(r, 'end'),
          startedAt: time('t0'),
          endedAt: time('t1'),
          permission: switch (jString(r, 'perm')) {
            'waiting' => MirrorPermissionState.waiting,
            'answered' => MirrorPermissionState.answered,
            'cancelled' => MirrorPermissionState.cancelled,
            _ => null,
          },
          answerKind: ans == null
              ? null
              : PermissionKind.parse(jString(ans, 'kind')),
          answerName: ans == null ? null : jString(ans, 'name'),
          locations: [
            for (final l in jMaps(r, 'loc'))
              ToolLocation(jString(l, 'path') ?? '', jInt(l, 'line')),
          ],
          output: out == null
              ? null
              : MirrorOutput(
                  head: jString(out, 'head') ?? '',
                  tail: jString(out, 'tail') ?? '',
                  lines: jInt(out, 'lines') ?? 0,
                  cut: jBool(out, 'cut'),
                ),
          diffs: [
            for (final d in jMaps(r, 'diffs'))
              MirrorDiff(
                path: jString(d, 'path') ?? '',
                oldText: jBool(d, 'created') ? null : jString(d, 'old') ?? '',
                newText: jString(d, 'new') ?? '',
                plus: jInt(d, 'plus') ?? 0,
                minus: jInt(d, 'minus') ?? 0,
                cut: jBool(d, 'cut'),
              ),
          ],
          moreDiffs: jInt(r, 'more_diffs') ?? 0,
          input: jString(r, 'input'),
        );
      case 'ask':
        final req = jMap(r, 'req');
        return MirrorAsk(
          index,
          rev,
          turn,
          id: jInt(r, 'id') ?? -1,
          status: jString(r, 'st') ?? 'open',
          request: req == null ? null : ElicitationRequest.fromJson(req),
        );
      case 'end':
        return MirrorTurnEnd(
          index,
          rev,
          turn,
          outcome: jString(r, 'outcome') ?? 'finished',
          elapsed: Duration(milliseconds: jInt(r, 'ms') ?? 0),
          stopReason: jString(r, 'stop'),
          error: jString(r, 'error'),
          files: jInt(r, 'files'),
          plus: jInt(r, 'plus') ?? 0,
          minus: jInt(r, 'minus') ?? 0,
        );
      case 'notice':
        final n = jMap(r, 'n') ?? const {};
        return MirrorNotice(
          index,
          rev,
          turn,
          kind: jString(n, 'k') ?? '',
          args: n,
          resolved: jBool(r, 'resolved'),
        );
      default:
        return MirrorUnknown(index, rev, turn, kind);
    }
  }
}
````

`packages/shellx_ai/lib/src/remote/subscription.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:math';
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:shellx_acp/shellx_acp.dart';

import '../model/session_state.dart';
import '../model/timeline.dart';
import 'item_codec.dart';
import 'mirror.dart';
import 'wire.dart';

/// One mirror message to send: `ai.snapshot` when [snapshot], else
/// `ai.update`; [seq] goes up by one per message of a subscription.
final class AiOutgoing with Redacted {
  const AiOutgoing(this.seq, this.body, {this.snapshot = false});

  final int seq;
  final Uint8List body;
  final bool snapshot;
}

/// What one phone has been sent of one session, and what to send next
/// (V2-2 D5): item records keyed by index and `rev`, text as appended
/// pieces, the header when it changed. Every body stays within
/// [kAiBodyBytes]; about [windowBytes] wait for an `ai.ack` at most. Pure
/// bookkeeping: the hub decides when to call it. Kept across re-attaches
/// of the same phone, so [AiOutgoing.seq] never goes back.
final class AiSubscription with Redacted {
  AiSubscription(
    this.state, {
    required this.generation,
    this.attachItems = kAiAttachItems,
    this.pageItems = kAiPageItems,
    this.windowBytes = kAiWindowBytes,
  });

  final AiSessionState state;

  /// Tells this state apart from another one of the same session (a new
  /// runtime): an [AiHave] of another generation is not used.
  final int generation;
  final int attachItems;
  final int pageItems;
  final int windowBytes;

  var _seq = 0;
  var _from = 0;

  /// The rev sent for each index from [_from] on; -1: the phone's copy is
  /// outdated or a text is still partial.
  final _revs = <int>[];

  /// Text items: how much of the text the phone has (UTF-16 units).
  final _textSent = <int, int>{};
  String? _head;
  var _version = -1;
  var _rest = false;
  final _inflight = <(int, int)>[];

  /// Bytes sent and not yet acknowledged.
  int get unackedBytes => _inflight.fold(0, (n, e) => n + e.$2);

  /// The window has room.
  bool get canSend => unackedBytes < windowBytes;

  /// Something changed since the last bodies, or a part is still to go.
  bool get dirty => state.version != _version || _rest;

  /// The first index the phone holds.
  int get from => _from;

  /// The bodies after an attach: one `ai.snapshot`, then `ai.update`s
  /// while the window allows (the rest goes with [delta]). [have] is used
  /// when it is of this [generation], holds something and is recent enough
  /// (at most twice [attachItems] new items since).
  List<AiOutgoing> attach(AiHave? have) {
    final items = state.timeline.items;
    final n = items.length;
    _revs.clear();
    _textSent.clear();
    _head = null;
    final reuse =
        have != null &&
        have.generation == generation &&
        have.revs.isNotEmpty &&
        have.from + have.revs.length <= n &&
        n - (have.from + have.revs.length) <= 2 * attachItems;
    if (reuse) {
      _from = have.from;
      for (var k = 0; k < have.revs.length; k++) {
        final item = items[_from + k];
        if (have.revs[k] == item.rev) {
          _revs.add(item.rev);
          final text = itemText(item);
          if (text != null) _textSent[_from + k] = text.length;
        } else {
          _revs.add(-1);
        }
      }
    } else {
      _from = max(0, n - attachItems);
    }
    return _produce(
      snapshot: {
        'g': generation,
        'from': _from,
        'reset': !reuse,
        'more': _from > 0,
      },
    );
  }

  /// What changed since the last bodies, within the window (at least one
  /// body when anything did). Empty when nothing changed.
  List<AiOutgoing> delta() => dirty ? _produce() : const [];

  /// The page before index [before] (it must be [from]): whole items, the
  /// newest body first, each one continuing the held window downwards. A
  /// text longer than one piece comes cut, with `clip` = its full length.
  List<AiOutgoing> earlier(int before) {
    if (before != _from || _from == 0) return const [];
    final items = state.timeline.items;
    final start = max(0, _from - pageItems);
    final records = <(String, int)>[];
    final revs = <int>[];
    for (var i = _from - 1; i >= start; i--) {
      final item = items[i];
      final text = itemText(item);
      String record;
      if (text != null) {
        final (json, end) = encodeTextRecord(item, i);
        record = end < text.length
            ? '${json.substring(0, json.length - 1)},"clip":${text.length}}'
            : json;
        _textSent[i] = end;
      } else {
        record = encodeItemRecord(item, i);
      }
      records.add((record, utf8Length(record)));
      revs.add(item.rev);
    }
    final out = <AiOutgoing>[];
    var k = 0;
    while (k < records.length) {
      final batch = <String>[];
      var size = 64;
      while (k < records.length &&
          (batch.isEmpty || size + records[k].$2 + 1 <= kAiBodyBytes)) {
        batch.add(records[k].$1);
        size += records[k].$2 + 1;
        k++;
      }
      final lowest = _from - k;
      out.add(
        _out(
          '{"v":$kAiWireVersion,"older":true,"more":${lowest > 0},'
          '"items":[${batch.reversed.join(',')}]}',
        ),
      );
    }
    _revs.insertAll(0, revs.reversed);
    _from = start;
    return out;
  }

  /// Every message up to [seq] arrived.
  void ack(int seq) => _inflight.removeWhere((e) => e.$1 <= seq);

  List<AiOutgoing> _produce({Map<String, Object?>? snapshot}) {
    _version = state.version;
    _rest = false;
    final items = state.timeline.items;
    final n = items.length;
    final budget = max(kAiBodyBytes, windowBytes - unackedBytes);
    final records = <(String, int)>[];
    var produced = 0;
    for (var i = _from; i < n; i++) {
      final k = i - _from;
      final item = items[i];
      if (k < _revs.length && _revs[k] == item.rev) continue;
      if (produced >= budget) {
        _rest = true;
        break;
      }
      final text = itemText(item);
      String record;
      if (text != null) {
        var at = _textSent[i] ?? 0;
        if (at > text.length) at = 0; // never shorter: resend it whole
        final (json, end) = encodeTextRecord(item, i, from: at);
        record = json;
        _textSent[i] = end;
        _setRev(k, end >= text.length ? item.rev : -1);
        if (end < text.length) _rest = true;
      } else {
        record = encodeItemRecord(item, i);
        _setRev(k, item.rev);
      }
      final size = utf8Length(record);
      records.add((record, size));
      produced += size + 1;
    }
    final head = jsonEncode(encodeHead(state, _indexOf));
    final headChanged = head != _head;
    _head = head;
    if (snapshot == null && records.isEmpty && !headChanged) return const [];
    final out = <AiOutgoing>[];
    var k = 0;
    var first = true;
    do {
      final prefix = StringBuffer('{"v":$kAiWireVersion,"n":$n');
      if (first && snapshot != null) {
        for (final e in snapshot.entries) {
          prefix.write(',"${e.key}":${jsonEncode(e.value)}');
        }
      }
      if (first && (headChanged || snapshot != null)) {
        prefix.write(',"head":$head');
      }
      final batch = <String>[];
      var size = utf8Length(prefix.toString()) + 16;
      while (k < records.length &&
          (batch.isEmpty || size + records[k].$2 + 1 <= kAiBodyBytes)) {
        batch.add(records[k].$1);
        size += records[k].$2 + 1;
        k++;
      }
      out.add(
        _out(
          '$prefix,"items":[${batch.join(',')}]}',
          snapshot: first && snapshot != null,
        ),
      );
      first = false;
    } while (k < records.length);
    return out;
  }

  void _setRev(int k, int rev) {
    if (k < _revs.length) {
      _revs[k] = rev;
    } else {
      _revs.add(rev);
    }
  }

  int _indexOf(TimelineItem item) {
    final items = state.timeline.items;
    for (var i = items.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      if (identical(items[i], item)) return i;
    }
    return -1;
  }

  AiOutgoing _out(String body, {bool snapshot = false}) {
    final bytes = Uint8List.fromList(utf8.encode(body));
    final seq = ++_seq;
    _inflight.add((seq, bytes.length));
    return AiOutgoing(seq, bytes, snapshot: snapshot);
  }
}
````

### Task 3: `shellx_agent` — the AI endpoint, its slot, and the session router [independent of V2-1b]

The router hands every `AiMessage` to an `AiEndpoint` and its answers to the phone (spec D1). It adds `ai: 1` to `session.list {desktop: true}` answers when it has an endpoint (D3). It tells the endpoint when a phone leaves or every phone goes.

`AiEndpointSlot` is the endpoint every runtime's router gets. It forwards to a target set later (the window's hub, or the background service's forwarder). Until then it answers "unavailable": the list `available: false`, an attach `ai.closed {reason: unavailable}`, a request `ai.result {code: unavailable}`. Without an endpoint the router ignores `ai.*`, like an older agent.

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/shellx_agent/pubspec.yaml`, `packages/shellx_agent/lib/shellx_agent.dart`, `packages/shellx_agent/lib/src/session/session_router.dart`
- Create: `packages/shellx_agent/lib/src/ai/ai_endpoint.dart`
- Test: `packages/shellx_agent/test/ai/ai_router_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 (`AiMessage` family, `kAiProtocol`); Task 2 (`encodeBody`, `AiListing`); `OutboundMessage` (`session/agent_sessions.dart`).
- Produces (exported from `package:shellx_agent/shellx_agent.dart`):
  - `abstract interface class AiEndpoint { Stream<OutboundMessage> get outbound; void handle(String mobileId, AiMessage message); void phoneGone(String mobileId); void allPhonesGone(); }`
  - `final Uint8List emptyAiListingBody`
  - `AiMessage? aiUnavailableReply(AiMessage message, {String reason = 'unavailable'})`
  - `final class AiEndpointSlot implements AiEndpoint { AiEndpoint? target; }` (get and set)
  - `SessionRouter({required SessionManager shells, required DesktopSessionManager desktop, required bool Function() desktopSharing, AiEndpoint? ai})`

`packages/shellx_agent/test/ai/ai_router_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:fake_async/fake_async.dart';
import 'package:shellx_agent/shellx_agent.dart';
import 'package:shellx_core/shellx_core.dart';
import 'package:test/test.dart';

import '../support/fake_pty.dart';

const phone = 'b0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002';

/// Records every call; answers nothing.
final class _SpyEndpoint implements AiEndpoint {
  final _out = StreamController<OutboundMessage>.broadcast(sync: true);
  final handled = <(String, AiMessage)>[];
  final gone = <String>[];
  var allGone = 0;

  void say(String mobileId, AiMessage m) =>
      _out.add(OutboundMessage(mobileId, m));

  @override
  Stream<OutboundMessage> get outbound => _out.stream;
  @override
  void handle(String mobileId, AiMessage message) =>
      handled.add((mobileId, message));
  @override
  void phoneGone(String mobileId) => gone.add(mobileId);
  @override
  void allPhonesGone() => allGone++;
}

(SessionRouter, List<OutboundMessage>) _router({AiEndpoint? ai}) {
  var next = 10;
  int ids() => next++;
  final router = SessionRouter(
    shells: SessionManager(
      ptyFactory: FakePtyFactory(),
      shellResolver: const FixedShellResolver(),
      homeDirectory: '/Users/test',
      baseEnvironment: const {},
      nextId: ids,
    ),
    desktop: DesktopSessionManager(nextId: ids),
    desktopSharing: () => true,
    ai: ai,
  );
  final sent = <OutboundMessage>[];
  router.outbound.listen(sent.add);
  return (router, sent);
}

/// V2-2 D1, D3: the session router routes `ai.*`; the slot answers while
/// no window serves AI.
void main() {
  group('session router', () {
    test('ai: 1 only with an endpoint, only to a desktop: true list', () {
      fakeAsync((async) {
        final (withAi, sentA) = _router(ai: _SpyEndpoint());
        withAi.handle(phone, const SessionList(desktop: true));
        withAi.handle(phone, const SessionList());
        final (without, sentB) = _router();
        without.handle(phone, const SessionList(desktop: true));
        async.flushMicrotasks();
        final a = sentA.map((o) => o.message as SessionListResult).toList();
        expect(a.map((r) => r.ai), [kAiProtocol, null]);
        expect((sentB.single.message as SessionListResult).ai, isNull);
      });
    });

    test('ai.* goes to the endpoint, its answers to the phone; a phone '
        'leaving is told', () {
      final spy = _SpyEndpoint();
      final (router, sent) = _router(ai: spy);
      router.handle(phone, const AiList());
      spy.say(phone, const AiResult(rid: 1, ok: true));
      router.mobileDisconnected(phone);
      router.disconnectAllPhones();
      expect(spy.handled.single.$2, isA<AiList>());
      expect(sent.single.message, isA<AiResult>());
      expect(spy.gone, [phone]);
      expect(spy.allGone, 1);
    });

    test('without an endpoint ai.* is ignored, like an older agent', () {
      final (router, sent) = _router();
      router.handle(phone, const AiAttach(sid: 'x.1'));
      expect(sent, isEmpty);
    });
  });

  group('slot', () {
    test('answers unavailable until a hub is set, then forwards', () {
      final slot = AiEndpointSlot();
      final out = <OutboundMessage>[];
      slot.outbound.listen(out.add);
      slot.handle(phone, const AiList());
      slot.handle(phone, const AiAttach(sid: 'x.1'));
      slot.handle(phone, const AiPrompt(sid: 'x.1', rid: 4, text: 'hi'));
      slot.handle(phone, const AiAck(sid: 'x.1', seq: 1));
      expect(out.map((o) => o.message.type), [
        'ai.list.result',
        'ai.closed',
        'ai.result',
      ]);
      expect((out[0].message as AiListResult).available, isFalse);
      expect((out[2].message as AiResult).code, 'unavailable');
      final spy = _SpyEndpoint();
      slot.target = spy;
      slot.handle(phone, const AiList());
      spy.say(phone, const AiResult(rid: 9, ok: true));
      expect(spy.handled, hasLength(1));
      expect(out.last.message, const AiResult(rid: 9, ok: true));
    });
  });
}
````

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement**

`packages/shellx_agent/lib/src/ai/ai_endpoint.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:shellx_ai/remote.dart';
import 'package:shellx_core/shellx_core.dart';

import '../session/agent_sessions.dart';

/// V2-2 D1: where the `ai.*` messages of phones go, and where the answers
/// come from. The window's [AiPhoneHub] (the process with the AI tabs)
/// implements it; in the background service [MuxAiForwarder] passes them
/// to the attached window. The session router only routes.
abstract interface class AiEndpoint {
  /// Messages to phones, in order (a synchronous broadcast stream: a
  /// listener must not call back into the endpoint from its callback).
  Stream<OutboundMessage> get outbound;

  /// One `ai.*` message from phone [mobileId] (paired and trusted: only an
  /// established channel delivers messages).
  void handle(String mobileId, AiMessage message);

  /// The channel to [mobileId] is gone, or the phone was revoked.
  void phoneGone(String mobileId);

  /// Every phone is gone (the relay link stopped).
  void allPhonesGone();
}

/// The listing body when no window serves AI.
final emptyAiListingBody = encodeBody(const AiListing().toJson());

/// What a desktop answers when nobody serves AI (V2-2 D1): the list is
/// unavailable, an attach is closed with [reason], a request fails
/// `unavailable`. Null for messages that need no answer.
AiMessage? aiUnavailableReply(
  AiMessage message, {
  String reason = 'unavailable',
}) => switch (message) {
  AiList() => AiListResult(available: false, body: emptyAiListingBody),
  AiAttach(:final sid) => AiClosed(sid: sid, reason: reason),
  AiPrompt(:final rid) ||
  AiAnswer(:final rid) ||
  AiCancel(:final rid) ||
  AiForceStop(:final rid) ||
  AiUnqueue(:final rid) ||
  AiOpen(:final rid) => AiResult(rid: rid, ok: false, code: 'unavailable'),
  _ => null,
};

/// The router's [AiEndpoint] in a process whose window builds its hub
/// later (V2-2 D1): forwards to [target]; without one it answers
/// [aiUnavailableReply].
final class AiEndpointSlot implements AiEndpoint {
  final _out = StreamController<OutboundMessage>.broadcast(sync: true);
  AiEndpoint? _target;
  StreamSubscription<OutboundMessage>? _forward;

  AiEndpoint? get target => _target;

  /// Every phone hears of the change through its next request.
  set target(AiEndpoint? endpoint) {
    unawaited(_forward?.cancel());
    _target = endpoint;
    _forward = endpoint?.outbound.listen(_out.add);
  }

  @override
  Stream<OutboundMessage> get outbound => _out.stream;

  @override
  void handle(String mobileId, AiMessage message) {
    final target = _target;
    if (target != null) return target.handle(mobileId, message);
    final reply = aiUnavailableReply(message);
    if (reply != null) _out.add(OutboundMessage(mobileId, reply));
  }

  @override
  void phoneGone(String mobileId) => _target?.phoneGone(mobileId);

  @override
  void allPhonesGone() => _target?.allPhonesGone();
}
````

### Task 4: `shellx_agent` — the phone hub and AI pushes [independent of V2-1b]

`AiPhoneHub` is the window's side of AI on phones (spec D2, D5, D7, D11). It reads the window only through `AiSessionSource` (tests use plain states; Task 11 implements it over V2-1b).
- **Listing:** on `ai.list`, then again whenever it changed, at most every 250 ms.
- **Mirrors:** an attach starts a restored tab's agent (D15) and sends the snapshot. Each change is mirrored at most every 100 ms per session, within each phone's window; acks open the window.
- **Requests (D7):** each is checked against what is pending **now**.
  - Permissions must name the waiting request and one of its options.
  - Questions must pass `form.accepts`.
  - Force stop only once the desktop offers it.
  - Prompts are refused when empty, over 32 KiB, or the queue is full.
  - Each request gets one `ai.result`; a phone over its budget gets `busy`.
- **Pushes (D11):** a needs-input push (≤ 1 per session per 30 s) and a long-turn-done push. None while a phone has that session open, or while the window shows it.

`AgentNotifications.aiNotice` words and sends the push with `data {kind: ai, ai_session}`.

**Files:**
- Create: `packages/shellx_agent/lib/src/ai/ai_phone_hub.dart`
- Modify: `packages/shellx_agent/lib/src/notify/agent_notifications.dart`, `packages/shellx_agent/lib/src/notify/notification_text.dart`, `packages/shellx_agent/lib/shellx_agent.dart`
- Create: `packages/shellx_agent/test/support/ai_rig.dart`
- Test: `packages/shellx_agent/test/ai/ai_phone_hub_test.dart`, `packages/shellx_agent/test/notify/ai_notice_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes:
  - Tasks 1–3;
  - V2-1a model: `AiSessionState` (`permissions`, `asks`, `queue`, `removeQueued`, `turn`, `timeline`, `currentPermission`, `currentAsk`, `label`, `sessionId`, `addListener`, `removeListener`), `TurnEndItem`, `TurnOutcome`;
  - ACP: `ElicitationForm.accepts`, `ElicitAccept`, `ElicitDecline`, `ElicitCancel`, `TextBlock`.
- Produces (exported from `package:shellx_agent/shellx_agent.dart`):
  - `final class AiSessionEntry({required int paneId, required String agentId, required String agentName, required String title, String? cwd, String? acpSessionId, AiSessionState? state, AiRemoteState? unavailable})`
  - `abstract interface class AiControl { AiSessionState get state; Future<bool> send(List<ContentBlock>); void cancel(); Future<void> forceStop(); void answerPermission(int id, String optionId); void answerAsk(int id, ElicitationResponse); }`
  - `abstract interface class AiSessionSource`:
    - `Stream<void> get changes`, `List<AiSessionEntry> get sessions`;
    - `List<AiAgentChoice> get agents`, `List<AiFolderChoice> get folders`, `String? get defaultAgent`;
    - `AiControl? control(int paneId, {bool start = false})`, `Future<int?> open(String agentId, String folderKey)`;
    - `bool seenOnDesktop(int paneId)`, `Duration? get doneThreshold`.
  - pushes:
    - `enum AiPushKind { needsInput, done }`;
    - `final class AiPush({required AiPushKind kind, required String agentName, required String sid, String? detail, int? turn, Duration? elapsed})` with `Map<String, Object?> toFields()` and `static AiPush? fromFields(Map<String, Object?>)`;
    - `typedef AiPushSink = void Function(AiPush push)`.
  - `final class AiPhoneHub implements AiEndpoint`:
    - constructor: `AiPhoneHub({required AiSessionSource source, AiPushSink? push, DateTime Function()? now, String? epoch, Duration flushEvery = 100 ms, Duration listEvery = 250 ms, Duration pushEvery = 30 s})`;
    - members: `final String epoch`, `String sidOf(int paneId)`, `void dispose()`.
  - `void AgentNotifications.aiNotice(AiPush push)`
  - `String NotificationText.aiNeedsInput(String? detail)`, `String NotificationText.aiDone(int turn, Duration elapsed)`
  - test support (`test/support/ai_rig.dart`): `aiT0`, `FakeAiControl`, `FakeAiSource` (`add(paneId, {runs, unavailable, title})`, `remove`, `controls`, `started`, `seen`, `doneThreshold`, `opened`, `openedWith`), `HubRig({source, pushes})` (`hub`, `sent`, `now`, `to(phone)`, `last<T>(phone)`, `mirror(phone, sid)`).

`packages/shellx_agent/test/support/ai_rig.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:shellx_acp/shellx_acp.dart';
import 'package:shellx_agent/shellx_agent.dart';
import 'package:shellx_ai/remote.dart';
import 'package:shellx_ai/shellx_ai.dart';
import 'package:shellx_core/shellx_core.dart';

final aiT0 = DateTime.utc(2026, 10, 14, 9);

/// What a runtime does with a phone's request, on a plain state: records
/// it and changes the state the way `AiRuntime` would.
final class FakeAiControl implements AiControl {
  FakeAiControl(this.state);

  @override
  final AiSessionState state;
  final sent = <String>[];
  final permissions = <(int, String)>[];
  final asks = <(int, ElicitationResponse)>[];
  var cancels = 0;
  var forceStops = 0;
  bool accept = true;

  @override
  Future<bool> send(List<ContentBlock> prompt) async {
    if (!accept) return false;
    sent.add(blockText(prompt));
    if (state.turn == null) {
      state.beginTurn(prompt, aiT0);
    } else {
      state.enqueue(prompt);
    }
    return true;
  }

  @override
  void cancel() {
    cancels++;
    state.turn?.cancelRequested = true;
    state.touch();
  }

  @override
  Future<void> forceStop() async => forceStops++;

  @override
  void answerPermission(int id, String optionId) {
    permissions.add((id, optionId));
    state.resolvePermission(id, PermissionSelected(optionId));
  }

  @override
  void answerAsk(int id, ElicitationResponse response) {
    asks.add((id, response));
    state.resolveAsk(id, response);
  }
}

/// A window with AI sessions on plain states.
final class FakeAiSource implements AiSessionSource {
  final _changes = StreamController<void>.broadcast(sync: true);
  final entries = <AiSessionEntry>[];
  final controls = <int, FakeAiControl>{};
  final started = <int>[];
  final seen = <int>{};
  @override
  Duration? doneThreshold = const Duration(seconds: 30);
  int? opened;
  final openedWith = <(String, String)>[];

  /// Adds AI pane [paneId] with a ready state (or none, [runs] false).
  AiSessionState add(
    int paneId, {
    bool runs = true,
    AiRemoteState? unavailable,
    String title = 'Phiên AI mới',
  }) {
    final state = AiSessionState(
      agentId: 'claude',
      agentName: 'Claude',
      cwd: '/p',
    )..setPhase(AgentPhase.ready);
    entries.add(
      AiSessionEntry(
        paneId: paneId,
        agentId: 'claude',
        agentName: 'Claude',
        title: title,
        cwd: '/p',
        state: runs ? state : null,
        unavailable: unavailable,
      ),
    );
    if (runs) controls[paneId] = FakeAiControl(state);
    _changes.add(null);
    return state;
  }

  void remove(int paneId) {
    entries.removeWhere((e) => e.paneId == paneId);
    controls.remove(paneId);
    _changes.add(null);
  }

  @override
  Stream<void> get changes => _changes.stream;

  @override
  List<AiSessionEntry> get sessions => List.of(entries);

  @override
  List<AiAgentChoice> get agents => const [
    AiAgentChoice(id: 'claude', name: 'Claude'),
  ];

  @override
  List<AiFolderChoice> get folders => const [
    AiFolderChoice(key: 'ws:a', name: 'shop', path: '/p'),
  ];

  @override
  String? get defaultAgent => 'claude';

  @override
  AiControl? control(int paneId, {bool start = false}) {
    if (start) started.add(paneId);
    return controls[paneId];
  }

  @override
  Future<int?> open(String agentId, String folderKey) async {
    openedWith.add((agentId, folderKey));
    final id = opened;
    if (id != null) add(id);
    return id;
  }

  @override
  bool seenOnDesktop(int paneId) => seen.contains(paneId);
}

/// A hub over [source] whose messages are kept per phone.
final class HubRig {
  HubRig({FakeAiSource? source, this.pushes})
    : source = source ?? FakeAiSource() {
    hub = AiPhoneHub(
      source: this.source,
      push: pushes?.add,
      now: () => now,
      epoch: 'ep',
    );
    hub.outbound.listen(sent.add);
  }

  final FakeAiSource source;
  final List<AiPush>? pushes;
  late final AiPhoneHub hub;
  final sent = <OutboundMessage>[];
  DateTime now = aiT0;

  List<AiMessage> to(String phone) => [
    for (final o in sent)
      if (o.mobileId == phone) o.message as AiMessage,
  ];

  T last<T extends AiMessage>(String phone) => to(phone).whereType<T>().last;

  /// The mirror of [sid] built from every body [phone] got.
  AiMirror mirror(String phone, String sid) {
    final mirror = AiMirror();
    for (final m in to(phone)) {
      switch (m) {
        case AiSnapshot(sid: final s, :final body) when s == sid:
          mirror.apply(decodeBody(body)!, snapshot: true);
        case AiUpdate(sid: final s, :final body) when s == sid:
          mirror.apply(decodeBody(body)!, snapshot: false);
        default:
          break;
      }
    }
    return mirror;
  }
}
````

`packages/shellx_agent/test/ai/ai_phone_hub_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:fake_async/fake_async.dart';
import 'package:shellx_acp/shellx_acp.dart';
import 'package:shellx_agent/shellx_agent.dart';
import 'package:shellx_ai/remote.dart';
import 'package:shellx_ai/shellx_ai.dart';
import 'package:shellx_core/shellx_core.dart';
import 'package:test/test.dart';

import '../support/ai_rig.dart';

const phone = 'b0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002';
const tablet = 'b0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000003';

PermissionRequest perm(String toolCallId) => PermissionRequest.fromJson({
  'sessionId': 's',
  'toolCall': {'toolCallId': toolCallId, 'title': 'Sửa src/main.ts'},
  'options': [
    {'optionId': 'allow', 'name': 'Yes', 'kind': 'allow_once'},
    {'optionId': 'reject', 'name': 'No', 'kind': 'reject_once'},
  ],
});

void say(AiSessionState s, String text) => s.applyUpdate(
  SessionUpdate.fromJson({
    'sessionUpdate': 'agent_message_chunk',
    'content': {'type': 'text', 'text': text},
  }),
  aiT0,
);

AiListing listing(AiListResult r) =>
    AiListing.fromJson(decodeBody(r.body)!, available: r.available);

Uint8List json(Object v) => Uint8List.fromList(utf8.encode(jsonEncode(v)));

/// V2-2 D1, D2, D5, D10, D11: the window's side of AI on phones.
void main() {
  test('the body limit is the protocol limit', () {
    expect(kAiBodyBytes, kAiMaxBodyBytes);
  });

  group('listing', () {
    test('lists sessions with their state; resent only when it changed', () {
      fakeAsync((async) {
        final rig = HubRig();
        final s = rig.source.add(-1, title: 'Fix cart');
        rig.source.add(-2, runs: false, unavailable: AiRemoteState.noFolder);
        rig.hub.handle(phone, const AiList());
        final first = listing(rig.last<AiListResult>(phone));
        expect(first.available, isTrue);
        expect(first.sessions.map((i) => (i.sid, i.state)), [
          ('ep.-1', AiRemoteState.idle),
          ('ep.-2', AiRemoteState.noFolder),
        ]);
        expect(first.folders.single.key, 'ws:a');

        s.beginTurn(const [TextBlock('go')], aiT0);
        for (var i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
          say(s, 'x');
        }
        async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 300));
        final lists = rig.to(phone).whereType<AiListResult>().toList();
        expect(lists, hasLength(2)); // one coalesced change, not 21
        expect(listing(lists.last).sessions.first.state, AiRemoteState.running);
        async.elapse(const Duration(seconds: 1));
        expect(rig.to(phone).whereType<AiListResult>(), hasLength(2));
      });
    });
  });

  group('mirror', () {
    test('attach starts the agent and sends a snapshot; streaming is '
        'coalesced to one update per 100 ms', () {
      fakeAsync((async) {
        final rig = HubRig();
        final s = rig.source.add(-1);
        rig.hub.handle(phone, const AiAttach(sid: 'ep.-1'));
        expect(rig.source.started, [-1]);
        expect(rig.to(phone).single, isA<AiSnapshot>());
        s.beginTurn(const [TextBlock('Viết hàm')], aiT0);
        async.flushTimers();
        final before = rig.to(phone).length;
        for (var i = 0; i < 50; i++) {
          say(s, 'chunk $i ');
          async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 4)); // 200 ms in all
        }
        async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 200));
        final updates = rig.to(phone).length - before;
        expect(updates, inInclusiveRange(2, 4));
        final mirror = rig.mirror(phone, 'ep.-1');
        expect((mirror.items.last as MirrorText).text, startsWith('chunk 0 '));
        expect((mirror.items.last as MirrorText).text, endsWith('chunk 49 '));
      });
    });

    test('the window stops updates until the phone acks', () {
      fakeAsync((async) {
        final rig = HubRig();
        final s = rig.source.add(-1)..beginTurn(const [TextBlock('go')], aiT0);
        rig.hub.handle(phone, const AiAttach(sid: 'ep.-1'));
        for (var i = 0; i < 40; i++) {
          say(s, '${'Dòng dài có dấu. ' * 800}\n');
          async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 150));
        }
        final unacked = rig.to(phone).whereType<AiUpdate>().toList();
        final bytes = unacked.fold<int>(0, (n, u) => n + u.body.length);
        expect(bytes, lessThan(kAiWindowBytes + 2 * kAiBodyBytes));
        final count = unacked.length;
        rig.hub.handle(phone, AiAck(sid: 'ep.-1', seq: unacked.last.seq));
        async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 150));
        expect(rig.to(phone).whereType<AiUpdate>().length, greaterThan(count));
      });
    });

    test('an unknown session, or one of another run, is closed', () {
      final rig = HubRig()..source.add(-1);
      rig.hub.handle(phone, const AiAttach(sid: 'ep.-9'));
      rig.hub.handle(phone, const AiAttach(sid: 'old.-1'));
      expect(rig.to(phone).map((m) => (m as AiClosed).reason), [
        'gone',
        'gone',
      ]);
    });

    test('a session that cannot run says why', () {
      final rig = HubRig()
        ..source.add(-2, runs: false, unavailable: AiRemoteState.agentOff);
      rig.hub.handle(phone, const AiAttach(sid: 'ep.-2'));
      final m = rig.mirror(phone, 'ep.-2');
      expect(m.head!.unavailable, AiRemoteState.agentOff);
    });

    test('a tab that closes closes on the phone', () {
      final rig = HubRig()..source.add(-1);
      rig.hub.handle(phone, const AiAttach(sid: 'ep.-1'));
      rig.source.remove(-1);
      expect(rig.last<AiClosed>(phone).reason, 'gone');
    });
  });

  group('requests: explicit, checked against what is pending now (D10)', () {
    test('a prompt is sent; empty, too big or a full queue is refused', () {
      fakeAsync((async) {
        final rig = HubRig();
        final s = rig.source.add(-1);
        final control = rig.source.controls[-1]!;
        rig.hub.handle(
          phone,
          const AiPrompt(sid: 'ep.-1', rid: 1, text: 'Sửa lỗi'),
        );
        rig.hub.handle(phone, const AiPrompt(sid: 'ep.-1', rid: 2, text: '  '));
        rig.hub.handle(
          phone,
          AiPrompt(
            sid: 'ep.-1',
            rid: 3,
            text: 'ắ' * (kAiMaxPromptBytes ~/ 3 + 1),
          ),
        );
        for (var i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
          rig.hub.handle(
            phone,
            AiPrompt(sid: 'ep.-1', rid: 10 + i, text: 'q$i'),
          );
        }
        async.flushMicrotasks();
        final results = {
          for (final r in rig.to(phone).whereType<AiResult>()) r.rid: r.code,
        };
        expect(results[1], isNull);
        expect(results[2], 'refused');
        expect(results[3], 'too_large');
        expect(results[15], 'queue_full');
        expect(control.sent.first, 'Sửa lỗi');
        expect(s.queue, hasLength(AiSessionState.maxQueue));
      });
    });

    test('a permission answer must name the pending request and one of its '
        'options; nothing is ever answered without one', () {
      fakeAsync((async) {
        final rig = HubRig();
        final s = rig.source.add(-1)..beginTurn(const [TextBlock('go')], aiT0);
        final control = rig.source.controls[-1]!;
        rig.hub.handle(phone, const AiList());
        rig.hub.handle(phone, const AiAttach(sid: 'ep.-1'));
        final p = s.addPermission(perm('c1'), aiT0);
        async.elapse(const Duration(seconds: 5));
        expect(control.permissions, isEmpty); // never on its own
        rig.hub.handle(
          phone,
          AiAnswer(sid: 'ep.-1', rid: 1, permission: p.id + 7, option: 'allow'),
        );
        rig.hub.handle(
          phone,
          AiAnswer(sid: 'ep.-1', rid: 2, permission: p.id, option: 'yolo'),
        );
        rig.hub.handle(
          phone,
          AiAnswer(sid: 'ep.-1', rid: 3, permission: p.id, option: 'allow'),
        );
        rig.hub.handle(
          tablet,
          AiAnswer(sid: 'ep.-1', rid: 4, permission: p.id, option: 'reject'),
        );
        final codes = {
          for (final r in rig.sent.map((o) => o.message).whereType<AiResult>())
            r.rid: r.code,
        };
        expect(codes, {1: 'stale', 2: 'refused', 3: null, 4: 'stale'});
        expect(control.permissions, [(p.id, 'allow')]);
      });
    });

    test('a question: accept only with values the form accepts', () {
      final rig = HubRig();
      final s = rig.source.add(-1)..beginTurn(const [TextBlock('go')], aiT0);
      final control = rig.source.controls[-1]!;
      final a = s.addAsk(
        ElicitationRequest.fromJson({
          'mode': 'form',
          'message': 'Which?',
          'requestedSchema': {
            'type': 'object',
            'properties': {
              'pick': {
                'type': 'string',
                'enum': ['a', 'b'],
              },
            },
            'required': ['pick'],
          },
        }),
      )!;
      rig.hub.handle(
        phone,
        AiAnswer(
          sid: 'ep.-1',
          rid: 1,
          ask: a.id,
          action: 'accept',
          body: json({'pick': 'z'}),
        ),
      );
      rig.hub.handle(
        phone,
        AiAnswer(
          sid: 'ep.-1',
          rid: 2,
          ask: a.id,
          action: 'accept',
          body: json({'pick': 'b'}),
        ),
      );
      final codes = [
        for (final r in rig.to(phone).whereType<AiResult>()) r.code,
      ];
      expect(codes, ['refused', null]);
      expect((control.asks.single.$2 as ElicitAccept).content, {'pick': 'b'});
    });

    test('stop needs a turn; force stop only once the desktop offers it', () {
      final rig = HubRig();
      final s = rig.source.add(-1);
      final control = rig.source.controls[-1]!;
      rig.hub.handle(phone, const AiCancel(sid: 'ep.-1', rid: 1));
      s.beginTurn(const [TextBlock('go')], aiT0);
      rig.hub.handle(phone, const AiForceStop(sid: 'ep.-1', rid: 2));
      rig.hub.handle(phone, const AiCancel(sid: 'ep.-1', rid: 3));
      s.turn!.canForceStop = true;
      rig.hub.handle(phone, const AiForceStop(sid: 'ep.-1', rid: 4));
      final codes = [
        for (final r in rig.to(phone).whereType<AiResult>()) r.code,
      ];
      expect(codes, ['stale', 'refused', null, null]);
      expect((control.cancels, control.forceStops), (1, 1));
    });

    test('open answers the new session; a refused one says so', () async {
      final rig = HubRig();
      rig.source.opened = -5;
      rig.hub.handle(
        phone,
        const AiOpen(rid: 1, agent: 'claude', folder: 'ws:a'),
      );
      rig.source.opened = null;
      rig.hub.handle(
        phone,
        const AiOpen(rid: 2, agent: 'claude', folder: 'nope'),
      );
      await Future<void>.delayed(Duration.zero);
      final results = rig.to(phone).whereType<AiResult>().toList();
      expect((results[0].ok, results[0].sid), (true, 'ep.-5'));
      expect((results[1].ok, results[1].code), (false, 'refused'));
    });

    test('a phone that floods gets busy, then is heard again', () {
      fakeAsync((async) {
        final rig = HubRig()..source.add(-1);
        for (var i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
          rig.hub.handle(phone, AiCancel(sid: 'ep.-1', rid: i));
        }
        final busy = rig
            .to(phone)
            .whereType<AiResult>()
            .where((r) => r.code == 'busy');
        expect(busy.length, greaterThan(30));
        rig.now = rig.now.add(const Duration(seconds: 3));
        rig.hub.handle(phone, const AiCancel(sid: 'ep.-1', rid: 500));
        expect(rig.last<AiResult>(phone).code, 'stale');
      });
    });
  });

  group('push (D11)', () {
    test('needs-input pushes once per 30 s, only when no phone has the '
        'session open and the window does not show it', () {
      fakeAsync((async) {
        final pushes = <AiPush>[];
        final rig = HubRig(pushes: pushes);
        final s = rig.source.add(-1)..beginTurn(const [TextBlock('go')], aiT0);
        final p1 = s.addPermission(perm('c1'), aiT0);
        expect(pushes.single.kind, AiPushKind.needsInput);
        expect(pushes.single.detail, 'Sửa src/main.ts');
        expect(pushes.single.sid, 'ep.-1');
        s.resolvePermission(p1.id, const PermissionSelected('allow'));
        s.addPermission(perm('c2'), aiT0);
        expect(pushes, hasLength(1)); // within 30 s
        rig.now = rig.now.add(const Duration(seconds: 31));
        rig.hub.handle(phone, const AiAttach(sid: 'ep.-1'));
        s.addPermission(perm('c3'), aiT0); // queued behind c2: no new one
        rig.hub.phoneGone(phone);
        rig.source.seen.add(-1);
        final p = s.permissions.first;
        s.resolvePermission(p.id, const PermissionSelected('allow'));
        expect(pushes, hasLength(1)); // seen on the computer
        rig.source.seen.clear();
        s.resolvePermission(
          s.permissions.first.id,
          const PermissionSelected('allow'),
        );
        s.addPermission(perm('c4'), aiT0);
        expect(pushes, hasLength(2));
      });
    });

    test('a phone that only lists still gets the push (shown in the app)', () {
      final pushes = <AiPush>[];
      final rig = HubRig(pushes: pushes);
      final s = rig.source.add(-1)..beginTurn(const [TextBlock('go')], aiT0);
      rig.hub.handle(phone, const AiList());
      s.addPermission(perm('c1'), aiT0);
      expect(pushes, hasLength(1));
    });

    test(
      'a long turn that ends pushes done; a short or stopped one does not',
      () {
        final pushes = <AiPush>[];
        final rig = HubRig(pushes: pushes);
        final s = rig.source.add(-1);
        s.beginTurn(const [TextBlock('a')], aiT0);
        s.endTurn(
          outcome: TurnOutcome.finished,
          at: aiT0.add(const Duration(seconds: 5)),
        );
        s.beginTurn(const [TextBlock('b')], aiT0);
        s.endTurn(
          outcome: TurnOutcome.cancelled,
          at: aiT0.add(const Duration(minutes: 5)),
        );
        s.beginTurn(const [TextBlock('c')], aiT0);
        s.endTurn(
          outcome: TurnOutcome.finished,
          at: aiT0.add(const Duration(minutes: 4)),
        );
        expect(pushes.map((p) => (p.kind, p.turn)), [(AiPushKind.done, 3)]);
        expect(pushes.single.elapsed, const Duration(minutes: 4));
      },
    );

    test('push fields round-trip for the background service', () {
      const push = AiPush(
        kind: AiPushKind.done,
        agentName: 'Claude',
        sid: 'ep.-1',
        turn: 3,
        elapsed: Duration(seconds: 61),
      );
      final back = AiPush.fromFields(push.toFields())!;
      expect(
        (back.kind, back.agentName, back.sid, back.turn, back.elapsed),
        (push.kind, push.agentName, push.sid, push.turn, push.elapsed),
      );
      expect('$push', '…');
    });
  });
}
````

import 'dart:convert';

import 'package:fake_async/fake_async.dart';
import 'package:http/http.dart' as http;
import 'package:http/testing.dart';
import 'package:shellx_agent/shellx_agent.dart';
import 'package:shellx_core/shellx_core.dart';
import 'package:test/test.dart';

const phone = 'b0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002';

/// V2-2 D11: the words and data of AI pushes.
void main() {
  group('push words (D11)', () {
    test('needs input and done, both languages', () {
      final vi = NotificationText('vi_VN');
      final en = NotificationText('en');
      expect(
        vi.aiNeedsInput('Sửa src/main.ts'),
        'Cần bạn trả lời: Sửa src/main.ts',
      );
      expect(vi.aiNeedsInput(null), 'Cần bạn trả lời');
      expect(
        en.aiNeedsInput('Run\nnpm test'),
        'Needs your answer: Run npm test',
      );
      expect(
        vi.aiDone(3, const Duration(minutes: 4, seconds: 2)),
        'Xong lượt 3 · 4 phút 2 giây',
      );
      expect(
        en.aiDone(3, const Duration(seconds: 45)),
        'Finished turn 3 · 45 s',
      );
    });

    test('aiNotice sends title, body and the session to open', () {
      fakeAsync((async) {
        final bodies = <Map<String, dynamic>>[];
        final api = ShellxApi(
          baseUri: Uri.parse('https://relay.test'),
          httpClient: MockClient((request) async {
            bodies.add(jsonDecode(request.body) as Map<String, dynamic>);
            return http.Response(
              '{"sent":1}',
              202,
              headers: {'content-type': 'application/json'},
            );
          }),
        )..token = 't';
        final n = AgentNotifications(
          NotificationsConfig(
            api: api,
            threshold: () => null,
            machineName: () => 'MacBook',
            locale: () => 'vi_VN',
          ),
        );
        n.aiNotice(
          const AiPush(
            kind: AiPushKind.needsInput,
            agentName: 'Claude',
            sid: 'ep.-1',
            detail: 'Sửa src/main.ts',
          ),
        );
        async.flushMicrotasks();
        expect(bodies.single, {
          'title': 'MacBook · Claude',
          'body': 'Cần bạn trả lời: Sửa src/main.ts',
          'data': {'kind': 'ai', 'ai_session': 'ep.-1'},
        });
      });
    });
  });
}
