`packages/shellx_agent/lib/src/ai/ai_phone_hub.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:convert';
import 'dart:math';

import 'package:shellx_acp/shellx_acp.dart';
import 'package:shellx_ai/remote.dart';
import 'package:shellx_ai/shellx_ai.dart';
import 'package:shellx_core/shellx_core.dart';

import '../session/agent_sessions.dart';
import 'ai_endpoint.dart';

/// One AI session of the window, as the hub needs it (V2-2 D2).
final class AiSessionEntry with Redacted {
  const AiSessionEntry({
    required this.paneId,
    required this.agentId,
    required this.agentName,
    required this.title,
    this.cwd,
    this.acpSessionId,
    this.state,
    this.unavailable,
  });

  /// The AI pane (V2-1 D3); the hub's session id is made from it.
  final int paneId;
  final String agentId;
  final String agentName;
  final String title;
  final String? cwd;
  final String? acpSessionId;

  /// The runtime's state; null while no runtime was made (a restored tab
  /// not shown yet) or when it cannot run ([unavailable]).
  final AiSessionState? state;

  /// [AiRemoteState.noFolder] or [AiRemoteState.agentOff]; null otherwise.
  final AiRemoteState? unavailable;
}

/// What a phone may do to a running session (V2-1a `AiRuntime`).
abstract interface class AiControl {
  AiSessionState get state;

  /// Sends or queues (V2-1 D16); false when it cannot be taken.
  Future<bool> send(List<ContentBlock> prompt);
  void cancel();
  Future<void> forceStop();
  void answerPermission(int id, String optionId);
  void answerAsk(int id, ElicitationResponse response);
}

/// The window, as the hub sees it (V2-2 D2). The desktop app implements
/// it over its tabs and runtimes; tests over plain states.
abstract interface class AiSessionSource {
  /// Fires when [sessions], [agents] or [folders] may have changed (a tab
  /// opened or closed, a runtime made, a folder chosen).
  Stream<void> get changes;

  /// The AI sessions, in sidebar order.
  List<AiSessionEntry> get sessions;

  /// The agents a phone may start, and where.
  List<AiAgentChoice> get agents;
  List<AiFolderChoice> get folders;
  String? get defaultAgent;

  /// The runtime of AI pane [paneId], made on first use; with [start] its
  /// agent starts if it has not (a phone wants to see the session). Null
  /// when it cannot run.
  AiControl? control(int paneId, {bool start = false});

  /// A new AI tab with agent [agentId] in folder [folderKey] (from
  /// [folders]), not selected, its agent started; its pane id, or null
  /// when either is not offered.
  Future<int?> open(String agentId, String folderKey);

  /// The user is looking at pane [paneId] on the computer now (window
  /// focused, pane focused): no push for it (V2-2 D11).
  bool seenOnDesktop(int paneId);

  /// A4's long-command threshold: a turn at least this long that ends
  /// unseen is pushed as done; null: off.
  Duration? get doneThreshold;
}

enum AiPushKind { needsInput, done }

/// A push the hub asks for (V2-2 D11); worded by the agent side's
/// `AgentNotifications.aiNotice`.
final class AiPush with Redacted {
  const AiPush({
    required this.kind,
    required this.agentName,
    required this.sid,
    this.detail,
    this.turn,
    this.elapsed,
  });

  final AiPushKind kind;
  final String agentName;
  final String sid;

  /// What the agent waits for ("Sửa src/main.ts").
  final String? detail;
  final int? turn;
  final Duration? elapsed;

  /// For `ai.push` (window → background service).
  Map<String, Object?> toFields() => {
    'kind': kind.name,
    'name': agentName,
    'sid': sid,
    'detail': ?detail,
    'turn': ?turn,
    if (elapsed != null) 'ms': elapsed!.inMilliseconds,
  };

  static AiPush? fromFields(Map<String, Object?> f) {
    final kind = AiPushKind.values
        .where((k) => k.name == f['kind'])
        .firstOrNull;
    final name = f['name'];
    final sid = f['sid'];
    if (kind == null || name is! String || sid is! String) return null;
    final detail = f['detail'];
    final turn = f['turn'];
    final ms = f['ms'];
    return AiPush(
      kind: kind,
      agentName: name,
      sid: sid,
      detail: detail is String ? detail : null,
      turn: turn is int ? turn : null,
      elapsed: ms is int ? Duration(milliseconds: ms) : null,
    );
  }
}

typedef AiPushSink = void Function(AiPush push);

final class _Phone {
  bool listing = false;
  String? sentListing;
  final subs = <String, AiSubscription>{};
  double tokens = _AiBudget.burst;
  DateTime refilled = DateTime.fromMillisecondsSinceEpoch(0);
}

abstract final class _AiBudget {
  /// Requests a phone may make at once, and per second after that.
  static const double burst = 60;
  static const double perSecond = 30;
}

final class _Watch {
  _Watch(this.state, this.listener, this.generation, this.items);
  final AiSessionState state;
  final void Function() listener;
  final int generation;
  int? attention;
  int items;
  DateTime? pushedAt;
}

/// The window's side of AI on phones (V2-2 D1, D2, D5, D11): lists the AI
/// sessions, mirrors the ones a phone attaches (at most one update per
/// [flushEvery] each, within each phone's window), runs the phone's
/// requests — only explicit ones, checked against what is pending now —
/// and asks for a push when a session needs the user and no phone has it
/// open and the window does not show it. Never logs anything a session holds.
final class AiPhoneHub implements AiEndpoint {
  AiPhoneHub({
    required AiSessionSource source,
    AiPushSink? push,
    DateTime Function()? now,
    String? epoch,
    this.flushEvery = const Duration(milliseconds: 100),
    this.listEvery = const Duration(milliseconds: 250),
    this.pushEvery = const Duration(seconds: 30),
  }) : _source = source,
       _push = push,
       _now = now ?? DateTime.now,
       epoch = epoch ?? _newEpoch() {
    _changes = source.changes.listen((_) => _rescan());
    _rescan();
  }

  final AiSessionSource _source;
  final AiPushSink? _push;
  final DateTime Function() _now;

  /// Starts every session id of this hub: ids of another run are unknown.
  final String epoch;
  final Duration flushEvery;
  final Duration listEvery;

  /// At most one "needs you" push per session in this time.
  final Duration pushEvery;

  final _out = StreamController<OutboundMessage>.broadcast(sync: true);
  final _phones = <String, _Phone>{};
  final _watched = <int, _Watch>{};
  final _generations = Expando<int>();
  var _nextGeneration = 1;
  final _flushTimers = <String, Timer>{};
  final _lastFlush = <String, DateTime>{};
  Timer? _listTimer;
  late final StreamSubscription<void> _changes;
  var _disposed = false;

  static String _newEpoch() {
    final r = Random.secure();
    return List.generate(6, (_) => r.nextInt(36).toRadixString(36)).join();
  }

  /// The session id of AI pane [paneId] for phones.
  String sidOf(int paneId) => '$epoch.$paneId';

  int? _paneOf(String sid) {
    final dot = sid.lastIndexOf('.');
    if (dot <= 0 || sid.substring(0, dot) != epoch) return null;
    return int.tryParse(sid.substring(dot + 1));
  }

  @override
  Stream<OutboundMessage> get outbound => _out.stream;

  @override
  void handle(String mobileId, AiMessage message) {
    if (_disposed) return;
    final phone = _phones.putIfAbsent(mobileId, _Phone.new);
    if (message is! AiAck && message is! AiDetach && !_take(phone)) {
      final rid = _ridOf(message);
      if (rid != null) {
        _send(mobileId, AiResult(rid: rid, ok: false, code: 'busy'));
      }
      return;
    }
    switch (message) {
      case AiList():
        phone.listing = true;
        phone.sentListing = null;
        _sendListing(mobileId, phone);
      case AiAttach(:final sid, :final body):
        _attach(mobileId, phone, sid, AiHave.fromJson(decodeBody(body)));
      case AiDetach(:final sid):
        phone.subs.remove(sid);
      case AiAck(:final sid, :final seq):
        final sub = phone.subs[sid];
        if (sub == null) return;
        sub.ack(seq);
        if (sub.dirty && sub.canSend) _scheduleFlush(sid);
      case AiEarlier(:final sid, :final before):
        final sub = phone.subs[sid];
        if (sub == null) return;
        for (final o in sub.earlier(before)) {
          _send(mobileId, AiUpdate(sid: sid, seq: o.seq, body: o.body));
        }
      case AiPrompt(:final sid, :final rid, :final text):
        unawaited(_prompt(mobileId, sid, rid, text));
      case AiAnswer():
        _answer(mobileId, message);
      case AiCancel(:final sid, :final rid):
        _command(mobileId, sid, rid, (c) {
          if (c.state.turn == null) return 'stale';
          c.cancel();
          return null;
        });
      case AiForceStop(:final sid, :final rid):
        _command(mobileId, sid, rid, (c) {
          if (c.state.turn?.canForceStop != true) return 'refused';
          unawaited(c.forceStop());
          return null;
        });
      case AiUnqueue(:final sid, :final rid, :final queued):
        _command(mobileId, sid, rid, (c) {
          if (!c.state.queue.any((q) => q.id == queued)) return 'stale';
          c.state.removeQueued(queued);
          return null;
        });
      case AiOpen(:final rid, :final agent, :final folder):
        unawaited(_open(mobileId, rid, agent, folder));
      case AiListResult() ||
          AiSnapshot() ||
          AiUpdate() ||
          AiClosed() ||
          AiResult():
        break; // desktop → phone types are never taken from a phone
    }
  }

  @override
  void phoneGone(String mobileId) => _phones.remove(mobileId);

  @override
  void allPhonesGone() => _phones.clear();

  /// Stops listening; the window is going.
  void dispose() {
    _disposed = true;
    unawaited(_changes.cancel());
    for (final t in _flushTimers.values) {
      t.cancel();
    }
    _flushTimers.clear();
    _listTimer?.cancel();
    for (final w in _watched.values) {
      w.state.removeListener(w.listener);
    }
    _watched.clear();
    _phones.clear();
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------ sessions --

  void _rescan() {
    if (_disposed) return;
    final entries = _source.sessions;
    final live = <int>{};
    for (final e in entries) {
      final state = e.state;
      if (state == null) continue;
      live.add(e.paneId);
      final watch = _watched[e.paneId];
      if (watch != null && identical(watch.state, state)) continue;
      if (watch != null) {
        watch.state.removeListener(watch.listener);
        _resubscribe(e.paneId, state);
      }
      void listener() => _onState(e.paneId);
      state.addListener(listener);
      _watched[e.paneId] = _Watch(
        state,
        listener,
        _generation(state),
        state.timeline.items.length,
      )..attention = state.currentPermission?.id ?? state.currentAsk?.id;
    }
    for (final paneId in [..._watched.keys]) {
      if (live.contains(paneId)) continue;
      final w = _watched.remove(paneId)!;
      w.state.removeListener(w.listener);
    }
    // Sessions that went away close on every phone that mirrors them.
    final present = {for (final e in entries) sidOf(e.paneId)};
    for (final MapEntry(key: mobileId, value: phone) in _phones.entries) {
      for (final sid in [...phone.subs.keys]) {
        if (present.contains(sid)) continue;
        phone.subs.remove(sid);
        _send(mobileId, AiClosed(sid: sid, reason: 'gone'));
      }
    }
    _scheduleListing();
  }

  int _generation(AiSessionState state) =>
      _generations[state] ??= _nextGeneration++;

  /// The session got a new state (a new runtime): phones start over.
  void _resubscribe(int paneId, AiSessionState state) {
    final sid = sidOf(paneId);
    for (final MapEntry(key: mobileId, value: phone) in _phones.entries) {
      if (!phone.subs.containsKey(sid)) continue;
      final sub = AiSubscription(state, generation: _generation(state));
      phone.subs[sid] = sub;
      _sendAll(mobileId, sid, sub.attach(null));
    }
  }

  void _onState(int paneId) {
    final w = _watched[paneId];
    if (w == null) return;
    final sid = sidOf(paneId);
    _scheduleFlush(sid);
    _scheduleListing();
    final s = w.state;
    final attention = s.currentPermission?.id ?? s.currentAsk?.id;
    if (attention != null && attention != w.attention) {
      _maybePush(paneId, w, AiPushKind.needsInput, detail: aiWaitingOf(s));
    }
    w.attention = attention;
    final items = s.timeline.items;
    for (var i = w.items; i < items.length; i++) {
      final item = items[i];
      if (item is! TurnEndItem || item.outcome == TurnOutcome.cancelled) {
        continue;
      }
      final threshold = _source.doneThreshold;
      if (threshold == null || item.elapsed < threshold) continue;
      _maybePush(
        paneId,
        w,
        AiPushKind.done,
        turn: item.turn,
        elapsed: item.elapsed,
      );
    }
    w.items = items.length;
  }

  void _maybePush(
    int paneId,
    _Watch w,
    AiPushKind kind, {
    String? detail,
    int? turn,
    Duration? elapsed,
  }) {
    final push = _push;
    if (push == null) return;
    final sid = sidOf(paneId);
    // A phone that has the session open shows it already (V2-2 D11). A
    // phone that only lists still gets the push: the app shows it as an
    // in-app banner while open, a notification otherwise.
    if (_phones.values.any((p) => p.subs.containsKey(sid))) return;
    if (_source.seenOnDesktop(paneId)) return;
    final now = _now();
    final last = w.pushedAt;
    if (kind == AiPushKind.needsInput &&
        last != null &&
        now.difference(last) < pushEvery) {
      return;
    }
    w.pushedAt = now;
    push(
      AiPush(
        kind: kind,
        agentName: w.state.agentName,
        sid: sid,
        detail: detail,
        turn: turn,
        elapsed: elapsed,
      ),
    );
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------- listing --

  void _scheduleListing() {
    if (_listTimer != null || !_phones.values.any((p) => p.listing)) return;
    _listTimer = Timer(listEvery, () {
      _listTimer = null;
      for (final MapEntry(key: id, value: phone) in [..._phones.entries]) {
        if (phone.listing) _sendListing(id, phone);
      }
    });
  }

  void _sendListing(String mobileId, _Phone phone) {
    final listing = AiListing(
      sessions: [
        for (final e in _source.sessions)
          AiSessionInfo(
            sid: sidOf(e.paneId),
            agentId: e.agentId,
            agentName: e.agentName,
            title: e.state?.label ?? e.title,
            state:
                e.unavailable ??
                (e.state == null
                    ? AiRemoteState.stopped
                    : aiRemoteStateOf(e.state!)),
            cwd: e.cwd,
            since: e.state?.turn?.startedAt.millisecondsSinceEpoch,
            waiting: e.state == null ? null : aiWaitingOf(e.state!),
            acpSessionId: e.state?.sessionId ?? e.acpSessionId,
          ),
      ],
      agents: _source.agents,
      folders: _source.folders,
      defaultAgent: _source.defaultAgent,
    );
    final json = jsonEncode(listing.toJson());
    if (json == phone.sentListing) return;
    phone.sentListing = json;
    _send(
      mobileId,
      AiListResult(available: true, body: encodeBody(listing.toJson())),
    );
  }

  // -------------------------------------------------------------- mirror --

  void _attach(String mobileId, _Phone phone, String sid, AiHave? have) {
    final paneId = _paneOf(sid);
    final entry = paneId == null
        ? null
        : _source.sessions.where((e) => e.paneId == paneId).firstOrNull;
    if (paneId == null || entry == null) {
      phone.subs.remove(sid);
      _send(mobileId, AiClosed(sid: sid, reason: 'gone'));
      return;
    }
    final state = _source.control(paneId, start: true)?.state ?? entry.state;
    if (state == null) {
      // It cannot run yet: the phone shows why (set up on the computer).
      phone.subs.remove(sid);
      _send(
        mobileId,
        AiSnapshot(
          sid: sid,
          seq: 1,
          body: encodeBody({
            'v': kAiWireVersion,
            'g': 0,
            'from': 0,
            'n': 0,
            'reset': true,
            'head': {
              'phase': 'stopped',
              'agent': entry.agentId,
              'name': entry.agentName,
              'title': entry.title,
              'cwd': ?entry.cwd,
              'unavailable': (entry.unavailable ?? AiRemoteState.noFolder).wire,
            },
            'items': const <Object?>[],
          }),
        ),
      );
      return;
    }
    _rescan(); // a runtime may just have been made
    var sub = phone.subs[sid];
    if (sub == null || !identical(sub.state, state)) {
      sub = AiSubscription(state, generation: _generation(state));
      phone.subs[sid] = sub;
    }
    _sendAll(mobileId, sid, sub.attach(have));
  }

  void _sendAll(String mobileId, String sid, List<AiOutgoing> out) {
    for (final o in out) {
      _send(
        mobileId,
        o.snapshot
            ? AiSnapshot(sid: sid, seq: o.seq, body: o.body)
            : AiUpdate(sid: sid, seq: o.seq, body: o.body),
      );
    }
  }

  void _scheduleFlush(String sid) {
    if (_flushTimers.containsKey(sid)) return;
    if (!_phones.values.any((p) => p.subs.containsKey(sid))) return;
    final last = _lastFlush[sid];
    var wait = last == null
        ? Duration.zero
        : flushEvery - _now().difference(last);
    if (wait.isNegative) wait = Duration.zero;
    _flushTimers[sid] = Timer(wait, () {
      _flushTimers.remove(sid);
      _flush(sid);
    });
  }

  void _flush(String sid) {
    if (_disposed) return;
    _lastFlush[sid] = _now();
    for (final MapEntry(key: mobileId, value: phone) in [..._phones.entries]) {
      final sub = phone.subs[sid];
      if (sub == null || !sub.canSend) continue;
      _sendAll(mobileId, sid, sub.delta());
    }
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------ requests --

  /// [run] answers a refusal code, or null when done.
  void _command(
    String mobileId,
    String sid,
    int rid,
    String? Function(AiControl control) run,
  ) {
    final control = _controlOf(sid);
    final code = control == null ? 'not_found' : run(control);
    _send(mobileId, AiResult(rid: rid, ok: code == null, code: code));
  }

  AiControl? _controlOf(String sid) {
    final paneId = _paneOf(sid);
    if (paneId == null) return null;
    if (!_source.sessions.any((e) => e.paneId == paneId)) return null;
    return _source.control(paneId);
  }

  Future<void> _prompt(
    String mobileId,
    String sid,
    int rid,
    String text,
  ) async {
    final control = _controlOf(sid);
    String? code;
    if (control == null) {
      code = 'not_found';
    } else if (text.trim().isEmpty) {
      code = 'refused';
    } else if (utf8Length(text) > kAiMaxPromptBytes) {
      code = 'too_large';
    } else if (control.state.queue.length >= AiSessionState.maxQueue) {
      code = 'queue_full';
    } else if (!await control.send([TextBlock(text)])) {
      code = 'refused';
    }
    _send(mobileId, AiResult(rid: rid, ok: code == null, code: code));
  }

  void _answer(String mobileId, AiAnswer a) {
    _command(mobileId, a.sid, a.rid, (c) {
      final s = c.state;
      final permission = a.permission;
      if (permission != null) {
        final p = s.permissions.where((p) => p.id == permission).firstOrNull;
        if (p == null) return 'stale';
        final option = a.option;
        if (option == null ||
            !p.request.options.any((o) => o.optionId == option)) {
          return 'refused';
        }
        c.answerPermission(permission, option);
        return null;
      }
      final askId = a.ask;
      if (askId == null) return 'refused';
      final ask = s.asks.where((q) => q.id == askId).firstOrNull;
      if (ask == null) return 'stale';
      switch (a.action) {
        case 'decline':
          c.answerAsk(askId, const ElicitDecline());
        case 'cancel':
          c.answerAsk(askId, const ElicitCancel());
        case 'accept':
          final form = ask.request;
          final content = decodeBody(a.body);
          if (form is! ElicitationForm || content == null) return 'refused';
          final values = <String, Object>{
            for (final e in content.entries)
              if (e.value != null) e.key: e.value!,
          };
          if (!form.accepts(values)) return 'refused';
          c.answerAsk(askId, ElicitAccept(values));
        default:
          return 'refused';
      }
      return null;
    });
  }

  Future<void> _open(
    String mobileId,
    int rid,
    String agent,
    String folder,
  ) async {
    int? paneId;
    try {
      paneId = await _source.open(agent, folder);
    } on Object {
      paneId = null;
    }
    if (paneId != null) _rescan();
    _send(
      mobileId,
      AiResult(
        rid: rid,
        ok: paneId != null,
        code: paneId == null ? 'refused' : null,
        sid: paneId == null ? null : sidOf(paneId),
      ),
    );
  }

  // --------------------------------------------------------------- utils --

  bool _take(_Phone phone) {
    final now = _now();
    final seconds = now.difference(phone.refilled).inMicroseconds / 1e6;
    phone
      ..tokens = min(
        _AiBudget.burst,
        phone.tokens + seconds * _AiBudget.perSecond,
      )
      ..refilled = now;
    if (phone.tokens < 1) return false;
    phone.tokens -= 1;
    return true;
  }

  static int? _ridOf(AiMessage m) => switch (m) {
    AiPrompt(:final rid) ||
    AiAnswer(:final rid) ||
    AiCancel(:final rid) ||
    AiForceStop(:final rid) ||
    AiUnqueue(:final rid) ||
    AiOpen(:final rid) => rid,
    _ => null,
  };

  void _send(String mobileId, AiMessage message) {
    if (_disposed) return;
    _out.add(OutboundMessage(mobileId, message));
  }
}
````

### Task 5: `shellx_agent` — AI through the background service (mux) [independent of V2-1b]

With the background service on, the relay link and the router are in the daemon, while the AI tabs are in the window (spec D1).
- **Daemon side, `MuxAiForwarder`:**
  - passes phones' `ai.*` to the window that sent `ai.serve`, as `ai.in {m, b}`;
  - takes its `ai.out {m, b}` back, but only desktop → phone types that fit one sealed batch, and only from the serving window;
  - answers "unavailable" when no window serves AI;
  - when that window goes: the phones that listed hear `available: false`, the open mirrors close with `desktop_closed`;
  - lists again, for the new window, every phone that listed;
  - `ai.push` from the window becomes the service's own push.
- **Window side, `MuxAiBinding`:** feeds the hub from the link and sends `ai.serve` once listening.
- **`MuxServer`:** gets an `ai` field like `cli`. Without it, the window's `ai.*` messages are ignored, as by an older service (none has a `q`, so no error is ever answered).

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/shellx_agent/lib/src/mux/mux_messages.dart`, `packages/shellx_agent/lib/src/mux/mux_server.dart`, `packages/shellx_agent/lib/shellx_agent.dart`
- Create: `packages/shellx_agent/lib/src/mux/mux_ai.dart`
- Test: `packages/shellx_agent/test/mux/mux_ai_test.dart`, `packages/shellx_agent/test/mux/mux_ai_server_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 1–4; `MuxClient`, `MuxMessage`, `MemoryMuxTransport`, `clientHandshake`, `MuxServer`.
- Produces (exported from `package:shellx_agent/shellx_agent.dart`):
  - `MuxType.aiIn = 'ai.in'`, `aiGone = 'ai.gone'`, `aiOut = 'ai.out'`, `aiServe = 'ai.serve'`, `aiPush = 'ai.push'`
  - `final class MuxAiForwarder implements AiEndpoint { MuxAiForwarder({void Function(AiPush push)? onPush}); bool get serving; void serve(Object owner, void Function(MuxMessage) send); void unserve(Object owner); void fromUi(Object owner, MuxMessage m); }`
  - `final class MuxAiBinding { MuxAiBinding(MuxClient client, AiEndpoint hub); Future<void> dispose(); }`
  - `AiPushSink muxAiPushSink(MuxClient client)`
  - `MuxServer.ai` (`MuxAiForwarder?`, assignable)

`packages/shellx_agent/test/mux/mux_ai_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:shellx_agent/shellx_agent.dart';
import 'package:shellx_core/shellx_core.dart';
import 'package:test/test.dart';

import '../support/ai_rig.dart';

const phone = 'b0000000-0000-4000-8000-000000000002';

/// V2-2 D1: the background service forwards `ai.*` to the window.
void main() {
  group('background service (mux)', () {
    test('no window: unavailable; a window serves: forwarded both ways; '
        'it quits: lists unavailable, mirrors closed; a new one re-lists', () {
      final pushes = <AiPush>[];
      final forwarder = MuxAiForwarder(onPush: pushes.add);
      final out = <OutboundMessage>[];
      forwarder.outbound.listen(out.add);
      forwarder.handle(phone, const AiList());
      expect((out.single.message as AiListResult).available, isFalse);

      final toUi = <MuxMessage>[];
      final window = Object();
      forwarder.serve(window, toUi.add);
      expect(toUi.single.type, MuxType.aiIn); // the phone that listed
      forwarder.handle(phone, const AiAttach(sid: 'e.-1'));
      expect(toUi.last.fields['m'], phone);

      MuxMessage outMsg(AppMessage m) => MuxMessage(
        MuxType.aiOut,
        fields: {'m': phone, 'b': encodeCbor(m.toMap())},
      );
      forwarder.fromUi(window, outMsg(const AiResult(rid: 1, ok: true)));
      forwarder.fromUi(
        window,
        outMsg(const AiPrompt(sid: 'e.-1', rid: 2, text: 'x')),
      );
      forwarder.fromUi(window, outMsg(const SessionReset(id: 1)));
      forwarder.fromUi(Object(), outMsg(const AiResult(rid: 3, ok: true)));
      expect(out.map((o) => o.message.type), ['ai.list.result', 'ai.result']);

      forwarder.fromUi(
        window,
        MuxMessage(
          MuxType.aiPush,
          fields: const AiPush(
            kind: AiPushKind.needsInput,
            agentName: 'Claude',
            sid: 'e.-1',
          ).toFields(),
        ),
      );
      expect(pushes.single.sid, 'e.-1');

      forwarder.unserve(window);
      expect(out.skip(2).map((o) => o.message.type), [
        'ai.list.result',
        'ai.closed',
      ]);
      expect((out[3].message as AiClosed).reason, 'desktop_closed');

      final again = <MuxMessage>[];
      forwarder.serve(Object(), again.add);
      expect(again.single.type, MuxType.aiIn);
      forwarder.phoneGone(phone);
      expect(again.last.type, MuxType.aiGone);
    });

    test('the window side: ai.in reaches the hub, its answers go back as '
        'ai.out, and it serves at once', () async {
      final (a, b) = MemoryMuxTransport.pair();
      final client = MuxClient(a);
      final fromWindow = <MuxMessage>[];
      b.messages.listen(fromWindow.add);
      final rig = HubRig()..source.add(-1);
      final binding = MuxAiBinding(client, rig.hub);
      await Future<void>.delayed(Duration.zero);
      expect(fromWindow.single.type, MuxType.aiServe);
      b.send(
        MuxMessage(
          MuxType.aiIn,
          fields: {'m': phone, 'b': encodeCbor(const AiList().toMap())},
        ),
      );
      await Future<void>.delayed(Duration.zero);
      final reply = fromWindow.last;
      expect((reply.type, reply.fields['m']), (MuxType.aiOut, phone));
      final message = AppMessage.fromMap(
        decodeCbor(reply.fields['b']! as dynamic) as Map<Object?, Object?>,
      );
      expect((message as AiListResult).available, isTrue);
      await binding.dispose();
      await client.close();
    });
  });
}
````

`packages/shellx_agent/test/mux/mux_ai_server_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:shellx_agent/shellx_agent.dart';
import 'package:shellx_core/shellx_core.dart';
import 'package:test/test.dart';

import '../support/mux_fixtures.dart';
import '../support/session_rig.dart';

const _self = MuxPeerInfo(
  protocol: kMuxProtocol,
  app: '2.0.0',
  instance: 'inst1',
  pid: 42,
);

Future<(MuxConnection, List<MuxMessage>)> _ui(TestListener l) async {
  final (client, daemonSide) = MemoryMuxTransport.pair();
  l.push(daemonSide);
  final peer = await clientHandshake(
    client,
    secret: secret,
    app: '2.0.0',
    role: 'ui',
  );
  final inbox = [...peer.early];
  client.messages.listen(inbox.add);
  return (client, inbox);
}

/// V2-2 D1: the background service and the window over the real mux link.
void main() {
  late SessionRig rig;
  late TestListener listener;
  late MuxServer server;

  setUp(() {
    rig = SessionRig();
    listener = TestListener();
  });

  tearDown(() async => server.close());

  test('a window that serves gets the phones\' ai.* and answers them; when '
      'it goes, phones hear the window closed', () async {
    final forwarder = MuxAiForwarder();
    final out = <OutboundMessage>[];
    forwarder.outbound.listen(out.add);
    server =
        MuxServer(
            sessions: rig.manager,
            secret: secret,
            self: _self,
            onQuit: () async {},
          )
          ..ai = forwarder
          ..serve(listener);
    final (ui, inbox) = await _ui(listener);
    ui.send(const MuxMessage(MuxType.aiServe));
    await pumpEventQueue();
    expect(forwarder.serving, isTrue);
    forwarder.handle(phone, const AiList());
    forwarder.handle(phone, const AiAttach(sid: 'e.-1'));
    await pumpEventQueue();
    expect(inbox.where((m) => m.type == MuxType.aiIn), hasLength(2));
    ui.send(
      MuxMessage(
        MuxType.aiOut,
        fields: {
          'm': phone,
          'b': encodeCbor(const AiResult(rid: 1, ok: true).toMap()),
        },
      ),
    );
    await pumpEventQueue();
    expect(out.single.message, const AiResult(rid: 1, ok: true));
    await ui.close();
    await pumpEventQueue();
    expect(forwarder.serving, isFalse);
    expect(out.skip(1).map((o) => o.message.type), [
      'ai.list.result',
      'ai.closed',
    ]);
  });

  test('an older service (no forwarder) ignores the window\'s ai.* '
      'messages and keeps answering', () async {
    server = MuxServer(
      sessions: rig.manager,
      secret: secret,
      self: _self,
      onQuit: () async {},
    )..serve(listener);
    final (ui, inbox) = await _ui(listener);
    ui.send(const MuxMessage(MuxType.aiServe));
    ui.send(
      MuxMessage(
        MuxType.aiOut,
        fields: {
          'm': phone,
          'b': encodeCbor({'t': 'ai.list'}),
        },
      ),
    );
    final reply = ui.messages.firstWhere((m) => m.q == 1);
    ui.send(const MuxMessage(MuxType.sessionsList, q: 1));
    expect((await reply).type, MuxType.result);
    expect(inbox.where((m) => m.type == MuxType.error), isEmpty);
  });
}
````

`packages/shellx_agent/lib/src/mux/mux_ai.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:shellx_core/shellx_core.dart';

import '../ai/ai_endpoint.dart';
import '../ai/ai_phone_hub.dart';
import '../session/agent_sessions.dart';
import 'mux_messages.dart';
import 'remote_local_sessions.dart';

MuxMessage _carry(String type, String mobileId, AiMessage message) =>
    MuxMessage(type, fields: {'m': mobileId, 'b': encodeCbor(message.toMap())});

/// The `ai.*` message in [m]'s `b`; null when it is not one this version
/// reads.
AiMessage? _carried(MuxMessage m) {
  final bytes = m.get<Uint8List>('b');
  if (bytes == null) return null;
  try {
    final map = decodeCbor(bytes);
    if (map is! Map<Object?, Object?>) return null;
    final message = AppMessage.fromMap(map);
    return message is AiMessage ? message : null;
  } on FormatException {
    return null;
  }
}

/// V2-2 D1, background service side: phones' `ai.*` messages go to the
/// attached window that serves AI (`ai.in`), its answers come back
/// (`ai.out`). Without such a window phones get [aiUnavailableReply]; when
/// it goes, every phone that listed hears `available: false` and every
/// mirror closes with `desktop_closed`. When a window starts serving, the
/// phones that listed are listed again for it.
final class MuxAiForwarder implements AiEndpoint {
  MuxAiForwarder({this.onPush});

  /// `ai.push` from the window: the service's own notifications send it.
  final void Function(AiPush push)? onPush;

  final _out = StreamController<OutboundMessage>.broadcast(sync: true);
  Object? _owner;
  void Function(MuxMessage message)? _ui;
  final _listing = <String>{};
  final _attached = <String, Set<String>>{};

  /// A window serves AI now.
  bool get serving => _ui != null;

  @override
  Stream<OutboundMessage> get outbound => _out.stream;

  /// The window behind [owner] serves AI from now on (`ai.serve`).
  void serve(Object owner, void Function(MuxMessage message) send) {
    _owner = owner;
    _ui = send;
    for (final mobileId in _listing) {
      send(_carry(MuxType.aiIn, mobileId, const AiList()));
    }
  }

  /// [owner]'s window went away.
  void unserve(Object owner) {
    if (!identical(_owner, owner)) return;
    _owner = null;
    _ui = null;
    for (final mobileId in _listing) {
      _emit(mobileId, AiListResult(available: false, body: emptyAiListingBody));
    }
    for (final MapEntry(key: mobileId, value: sids) in _attached.entries) {
      for (final sid in sids) {
        _emit(mobileId, AiClosed(sid: sid, reason: 'desktop_closed'));
      }
    }
    _attached.clear();
  }

  /// `ai.out` or `ai.push` from [owner]'s window. Only desktop → phone
  /// types that fit one sealed batch are passed on; anything else is
  /// dropped, never sent (a batch too big would drop the phone's channel).
  void fromUi(Object owner, MuxMessage m) {
    if (!identical(_owner, owner)) return;
    switch (m.type) {
      case MuxType.aiOut:
        final mobileId = m.get<String>('m');
        final message = _carried(m);
        if (mobileId == null || message == null) return;
        if (message is! AiListResult &&
            message is! AiSnapshot &&
            message is! AiUpdate &&
            message is! AiResult &&
            message is! AiClosed) {
          return;
        }
        if (encodeBatch([message]).length > kMaxSealedBatchBytes) return;
        if (message is AiClosed) _attached[mobileId]?.remove(message.sid);
        _emit(mobileId, message);
      case MuxType.aiPush:
        final push = AiPush.fromFields(m.fields);
        if (push != null) onPush?.call(push);
    }
  }

  @override
  void handle(String mobileId, AiMessage message) {
    switch (message) {
      case AiList():
        _listing.add(mobileId);
      case AiAttach(:final sid):
        (_attached[mobileId] ??= {}).add(sid);
      case AiDetach(:final sid):
        _attached[mobileId]?.remove(sid);
      default:
        break;
    }
    final ui = _ui;
    if (ui != null) {
      ui(_carry(MuxType.aiIn, mobileId, message));
      return;
    }
    final reply = aiUnavailableReply(message);
    if (reply != null) _emit(mobileId, reply);
  }

  @override
  void phoneGone(String mobileId) {
    _listing.remove(mobileId);
    _attached.remove(mobileId);
    _ui?.call(MuxMessage(MuxType.aiGone, fields: {'m': mobileId}));
  }

  @override
  void allPhonesGone() {
    _listing.clear();
    _attached.clear();
    _ui?.call(const MuxMessage(MuxType.aiGone));
  }

  void _emit(String mobileId, AiMessage message) =>
      _out.add(OutboundMessage(mobileId, message));
}

/// V2-2 D1, window side, in background-service mode: the service's `ai.in`
/// and `ai.gone` go to [hub]; the hub's messages go back as `ai.out`. Tells
/// the service it serves AI (`ai.serve`) once listening.
final class MuxAiBinding {
  MuxAiBinding(this.client, this.hub) {
    _events = client.events.listen(_onEvent);
    _out = hub.outbound.listen(
      (o) => client.send(MuxType.aiOut, {
        'm': o.mobileId,
        'b': encodeCbor(o.message.toMap()),
      }),
    );
    client.send(MuxType.aiServe);
  }

  final MuxClient client;
  final AiEndpoint hub;
  late final StreamSubscription<MuxMessage> _events;
  late final StreamSubscription<OutboundMessage> _out;

  void _onEvent(MuxMessage m) {
    switch (m.type) {
      case MuxType.aiIn:
        final mobileId = m.get<String>('m');
        final message = _carried(m);
        if (mobileId != null && message != null) hub.handle(mobileId, message);
      case MuxType.aiGone:
        final mobileId = m.get<String>('m');
        if (mobileId == null) {
          hub.allPhonesGone();
        } else {
          hub.phoneGone(mobileId);
        }
    }
  }

  /// The link is gone or replaced: the phones it carried are gone too (at
  /// once, before a new link's phones arrive).
  Future<void> dispose() {
    hub.allPhonesGone();
    return Future.wait([_events.cancel(), _out.cancel()]);
  }
}

/// [AiPushSink] of a window in background-service mode: the service sends
/// the push with its own account (`ai.push`).
AiPushSink muxAiPushSink(MuxClient client) =>
    (push) => client.send(MuxType.aiPush, push.toFields());
````

### Task 6: `shellx_client` — `MachineAi`: listing, mirrors, requests, resume [independent of V2-1b]

The phone's side (spec D5, D7).
- **`MachineClient.ai`:**
  - learns `supported` from `session.list.result.ai`;
  - keeps the listing current while watched;
  - keeps one `AiSessionLink` (with its `AiMirror`) per open session;
  - acknowledges at most every 50 ms per session;
  - sends requests with a `rid`, which resolve on `ai.result`, fail with `AiRequestException(code)`, or time out.
- **Channel loss:** pending requests fail and are never resent; links go back to `attaching`.
- **Every new channel:** `ai.list` again, and every open link re-attaches with what it holds.
- **Gap or unreadable body:** the link re-attaches.
- **Resume:** `_resumeOnce` now also brings a lost channel back when only AI wants it (no terminal tab open).

The end-to-end tests run a real `AgentService`, `SessionRouter` and hub on the fake relay and the real encrypted channel. They include the mandatory compatibility tests both ways.

**Files:**
- Modify: `packages/shellx_client/pubspec.yaml`, `packages/shellx_client/lib/shellx_client.dart`, `packages/shellx_client/lib/src/machine_client.dart`, `packages/shellx_client/test/support/harness.dart`
- Create: `packages/shellx_client/lib/src/machine_ai.dart`
- Test: `packages/shellx_client/test/ai_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 1–4; `MachineClient` internals (`_send`, `connect`, `_timeout`, `_dispatch`, handshake install, `_channelLost`, `shutdown`, `_resumeOnce`).
- Produces (exported from `package:shellx_client/shellx_client.dart`):
  - `final class AiRequestException implements Exception { const AiRequestException(String code); final String code; }`
  - `enum AiLinkState { attaching, live, closed }`
  - `final class AiSessionLink { String sid; AiMirror mirror; AiLinkState state; String? closedReason; Stream<void> changes; void detach(); void loadEarlier(); }`
  - `final class MachineAi`:
    - state: `bool? supported`, `AiListing? listing`, `Stream<AiListing> listings`, `bool wantsChannel`;
    - `Future<void> watch()`, `AiSessionLink attach(String sid)`;
    - requests: `Future<void> prompt(String sid, String text)`, `answerPermission(String sid, int permission, String option)`, `answerAsk(String sid, int ask, ElicitationResponse response)`, `cancel(String sid)`, `forceStop(String sid)`, `unqueue(String sid, int queued)`;
    - `Future<String> open(String agent, String folder)`.
  - `MachineClient.ai` (`late final MachineAi`).
  - test support: `Harness(FakeAsync async, TestKeys keys, {bool paired = true, AiEndpoint? ai})`.

````dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:fake_async/fake_async.dart';

final class _Source implements AiSessionSource {
  final state = AiSessionState(
    agentId: 'claude',

  )..setPhase(AgentPhase.ready);
  late final runtime = _Control(state);
  final _changes = StreamController<void>.broadcast(sync: true);

  @override
  Stream<void> get changes => _changes.stream;
  @override
  List<AiSessionEntry> get sessions => [

      paneId: -1,
      agentId: 'claude',
      agentName: 'Claude',

      cwd: '/p',
      state: state,
    ),
  ];
  @override
  List<AiAgentChoice> get agents => const [
    AiAgentChoice(id: 'claude', name: 'Claude'),
  ];
  @override
  List<AiFolderChoice> get folders => const [];
  @override

PermissionRequest _perm() => PermissionRequest.fromJson({
  'sessionId': 's',
  'toolCall': {'toolCallId': 'c1', 'title': 'Sửa src/main.ts'},
  'options': [
    {'optionId': 'allow', 'name': 'Yes', 'kind': 'allow_once'},
    {'optionId': 'reject', 'name': 'No', 'kind': 'reject_once'},

});

void _say(AiSessionState s, String text) => s.applyUpdate(
  SessionUpdate.fromJson({
    'sessionUpdate': 'agent_message_chunk',
    'content': {'type': 'text', 'text': text},
  }),
  _t0,
);

/// V2-2 end to end: a real phone client and a real agent over the fake
/// relay and the real encrypted channel.
void main() {
  late TestKeys keys;
  setUpAll(() async => keys = await TestKeys.generate());

  test('list, attach, stream, prompt and answer from the phone', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final source = _Source();
      final hub = AiPhoneHub(source: source, epoch: 'ep');
      final h = Harness(async, keys, ai: hub);
      final ai = h.phoneMachine.ai;
      h.run(h.phoneMachine.listSessions(includeDesktop: true));
      expect(ai.supported, isTrue);
      h.run(ai.watch());
      settle(async);
      expect(ai.listing!.sessions.single.sid, 'ep.-1');

      final link = ai.attach('ep.-1');
      settle(async);
      expect(link.state, AiLinkState.live);
      h.run(ai.prompt('ep.-1', 'Thêm nút đăng xuất'));
      expect(source.runtime.prompts, ['Thêm nút đăng xuất']);
      for (var i = 0; i < 30; i++) {
        _say(source.state, 'phần $i. ');
        async.elapse(const Duration(milliseconds: 20));
      }
      settle(async, const Duration(milliseconds: 300));
      final text = (link.mirror.items.last as MirrorText).text;
      expect(text, startsWith('phần 0. '));
      expect(text, endsWith('phần 29. '));

      final p = source.state.addPermission(_perm(), _t0);
      settle(async, const Duration(milliseconds: 300));
      expect(link.mirror.head!.permission!.id, p.id);
      h.run(ai.answerPermission('ep.-1', p.id, 'allow'));
      expect(source.runtime.answers, [(p.id, 'allow')]);
      expect(
        () =>
            h.run(h.tabletMachine.ai.answerPermission('ep.-1', p.id, 'reject')),
        throwsA(
          isA<AiRequestException>().having((e) => e.code, 'code', 'stale'),
        ),
      );
      settle(async, const Duration(milliseconds: 300));
      expect(link.mirror.head!.permission, isNull);
    });
  });

  test('a lost channel resumes with what the phone holds', () {
    fakeAsync((async) {
      final source = _Source();
      final hub = AiPhoneHub(source: source, epoch: 'ep');
      final h = Harness(async, keys, ai: hub);
      final ai = h.phoneMachine.ai;
      source.state.beginTurn(const [TextBlock('go')], _t0);
      for (var i = 0; i < 20; i++) {
        _say(source.state, 'a$i ');
        source.state.applyUpdate(
          SessionUpdate.fromJson({
            'sessionUpdate': 'tool_call',
            'toolCallId': 't$i',
            'title': 'Read $i',
            'kind': 'read',
          }),
          _t0,
        );
      }
      final link = ai.attach('ep.-1');
      settle(async);
      expect(link.mirror.end, source.state.timeline.items.length);
      h.disconnectPhone();
      expect(link.state, AiLinkState.attaching);
      _say(source.state, 'after');
      h.reconnectPhone();
      settle(async, const Duration(seconds: 1));
      expect(link.state, AiLinkState.live);
      expect(link.mirror.end, source.state.timeline.items.length);
      expect((link.mirror.items.last as MirrorText).text, 'after');
    });
  });

  group('compatibility (mandatory)', () {
    test(
      'new phone, old desktop: no ai flag, nothing sent, terminals fine',
      () {
        fakeAsync((async) {
          final h = Harness(async, keys); // an agent without AI for phones
          final machine = h.phoneMachine;
          h.run(machine.listSessions(includeDesktop: true));
          expect(machine.ai.supported, isFalse);
          // Even when asked anyway, the old agent ignores it; the channel
          // stays and terminals keep working.
          expect(
            () => h.run(machine.ai.prompt('x.1', 'hi')),
            throwsA(isA<MachineConnectionException>()),
          );
          final tab = h.run(machine.openSession(cols: 80, rows: 24));
          final recorder = TabRecorder(tab);
          h.pty.emit('still here\r\n');
          settle(async);
          expect(recorder.text, contains('still here'));
        });
      },
    );

    test('old phone, new desktop: a phone that never asks gets no ai.*', () {
      fakeAsync((async) {
        final source = _Source();
        final hub = AiPhoneHub(source: source, epoch: 'ep');
        final toPhones = <OutboundMessage>[];
        hub.outbound.listen(toPhones.add);
        final h = Harness(async, keys, ai: hub);
        final tab = h.run(h.phoneMachine.openSession(cols: 80, rows: 24));
        final recorder = TabRecorder(tab);
        source.state.beginTurn(const [TextBlock('go')], _t0);
        _say(source.state, 'streaming on the desktop');
        source.state.addPermission(_perm(), _t0);
        h.pty.emit('ok\r\n');
        settle(async, const Duration(seconds: 1));
        expect(toPhones, isEmpty);
        expect(recorder.text, contains('ok'));
      });
    });
  });
}
````

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement**

`packages/shellx_client/lib/src/machine_ai.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:async';
import 'dart:typed_data';

import 'package:meta/meta.dart';
import 'package:shellx_acp/shellx_acp.dart';
import 'package:shellx_ai/remote.dart';
import 'package:shellx_core/shellx_core.dart';

import 'client_types.dart';

/// A request of the AI session failed on the computer (`ai.result` with
/// [code]: `stale`, `refused`, `queue_full`, `too_large`, `busy`,
/// `not_found`, `unavailable`, or a newer word).
final class AiRequestException implements Exception {
  const AiRequestException(this.code);
  final String code;

  @override
  String toString() => 'AiRequestException($code)';
}

enum AiLinkState {
  /// Waiting for the snapshot (first attach, or after a channel loss).
  attaching,
  live,

  /// The computer closed it ([AiSessionLink.closedReason]) or it was
  /// detached.
  closed,
}

/// This phone's mirror of one AI session (V2-2 D5). Kept across channel
/// losses: on a new channel it re-attaches with what it holds, so only
/// what changed comes back.
final class AiSessionLink {
  AiSessionLink._(this.sid, this._owner);

  final String sid;
  final MachineAi _owner;
  final mirror = AiMirror();
  final _changes = StreamController<void>.broadcast(sync: true);
  AiLinkState _state = AiLinkState.attaching;
  String? _closedReason;
  var _awaitingSnapshot = true;
  var _lastSeq = 0;
  Timer? _ack;

  AiLinkState get state => _state;

  /// `gone` (the tab closed), `desktop_closed`, `unavailable`, or `detached`.
  String? get closedReason => _closedReason;

  /// After every change of [mirror] or [state].
  Stream<void> get changes => _changes.stream;

  /// Stops mirroring (the screen left).
  void detach() => _owner._detach(this);

  /// "Tải thêm": the page before the held window.
  void loadEarlier() {
    if (_state != AiLinkState.live || !mirror.hasEarlier) return;
    _owner._send([AiEarlier(sid: sid, before: mirror.from)]);
  }

  void _set(AiLinkState state, [String? reason]) {
    _state = state;
    if (reason != null) _closedReason = reason;
    _changes.add(null);
  }

  void _body(int seq, Uint8List body, {required bool snapshot}) {
    _lastSeq = seq;
    _scheduleAck();
    if (_state == AiLinkState.closed) return;
    if (snapshot) {
      _awaitingSnapshot = false;
    } else if (_awaitingSnapshot) {
      return; // made for what the phone held before its re-attach
    }
    final decoded = decodeBody(body);
    if (decoded == null || !mirror.apply(decoded, snapshot: snapshot)) {
      _awaitingSnapshot = true;
      _owner._attachNow(this); // a gap: start again from what it holds
      return;
    }
    if (_state != AiLinkState.live) _state = AiLinkState.live;
    _changes.add(null);
  }

  /// One ack per 50 ms at most: each `_send` is a relay frame.
  void _scheduleAck() {
    _ack ??= Timer(const Duration(milliseconds: 50), () {
      _ack = null;
      if (_state != AiLinkState.closed) {
        _owner._send([AiAck(sid: sid, seq: _lastSeq)]);
      }
    });
  }

  void _dispose() {
    _ack?.cancel();
    unawaited(_changes.close());
  }
}

/// The AI sessions of one machine (V2-2): its listing and the sessions
/// this phone mirrors. Part of [MachineClient]; every request connects
/// first and fails like the client's own requests.
final class MachineAi {
  @internal
  MachineAi({
    required bool Function(List<AppMessage> messages) send,
    required Future<void> Function() connect,
    required Duration timeout,
  }) : _sendRaw = send,
       _connect = connect,
       _timeout = timeout;

  final bool Function(List<AppMessage>) _sendRaw;
  final Future<void> Function() _connect;
  final Duration _timeout;
  bool? _supported;
  AiListing? _listing;
  final _listings = StreamController<AiListing>.broadcast(sync: true);
  var _watching = false;
  final _links = <String, AiSessionLink>{};
  final _pending = <int, Completer<AiResult>>{};
  var _nextRid = 1;

  /// A listing is watched or a mirror is open: a lost channel must come
  /// back even without a terminal tab.
  bool get wantsChannel =>
      _watching || _links.values.any((l) => l.state != AiLinkState.closed);

  /// Whether the computer has AI sessions for phones (`ai` in
  /// `session.list.result`); null before the first desktop list.
  bool? get supported => _supported;

  /// The last listing; null before the first one.
  AiListing? get listing => _listing;

  /// Every listing as it comes (the first answer, then each change).
  Stream<AiListing> get listings => _listings.stream;

  /// Keeps the listing current: asks now (connecting first when there is
  /// no channel) and again on every new channel.
  Future<void> watch() async {
    _watching = true;
    if (!_send([const AiList()])) await _connect(); // channelReady asks
  }

  /// Mirrors session [sid] (the same link while it stays open).
  AiSessionLink attach(String sid) {
    final existing = _links[sid];
    if (existing != null && existing.state != AiLinkState.closed) {
      return existing;
    }
    final link = AiSessionLink._(sid, this);
    _links[sid] = link;
    // Without a channel, the next one attaches it (channelReady).
    if (!_attachNow(link)) {
      unawaited(_connect().catchError((Object _) {}));
    }
    return link;
  }

  Future<void> prompt(String sid, String text) {
    if (utf8Length(text) > kAiMaxPromptBytes) {
      return Future.error(const AiRequestException('too_large'));
    }
    return _request((rid) => AiPrompt(sid: sid, rid: rid, text: text));
  }

  Future<void> answerPermission(String sid, int permission, String option) =>
      _request(
        (rid) => AiAnswer(
          sid: sid,
          rid: rid,
          permission: permission,
          option: option,
        ),
      );

  Future<void> answerAsk(String sid, int ask, ElicitationResponse response) =>
      _request(
        (rid) => AiAnswer(
          sid: sid,
          rid: rid,
          ask: ask,
          action: switch (response) {
            ElicitAccept() => 'accept',
            ElicitDecline() => 'decline',
            ElicitCancel() => 'cancel',
          },
          body: switch (response) {
            ElicitAccept(:final content) => encodeBody(content ?? const {}),
            _ => null,
          },
        ),
      );

  Future<void> cancel(String sid) =>
      _request((rid) => AiCancel(sid: sid, rid: rid));

  Future<void> forceStop(String sid) =>
      _request((rid) => AiForceStop(sid: sid, rid: rid));

  Future<void> unqueue(String sid, int queued) =>
      _request((rid) => AiUnqueue(sid: sid, rid: rid, queued: queued));

  /// A new session; its sid.
  Future<String> open(String agent, String folder) async {
    final result = await _request(
      (rid) => AiOpen(rid: rid, agent: agent, folder: folder),
    );
    final sid = result.sid;
    if (sid == null) throw const AiRequestException('refused');
    return sid;
  }

  // -------------------------------------------------- from MachineClient --

  @internal
  void noteListResult(SessionListResult result) {
    final ai = result.ai;
    _supported = ai != null && ai >= 1;
  }

  @internal
  void handle(AiMessage message) {
    switch (message) {
      case AiListResult(:final available, :final body):
        _supported = true;
        final json = decodeBody(body);
        final listing = json == null
            ? AiListing(available: available)
            : AiListing.fromJson(json, available: available);
        _listing = listing;
        _listings.add(listing);
      case AiSnapshot(:final sid, :final seq, :final body):
        _links[sid]?._body(seq, body, snapshot: true);
      case AiUpdate(:final sid, :final seq, :final body):
        _links[sid]?._body(seq, body, snapshot: false);
      case AiClosed(:final sid, :final reason):
        _links[sid]?._set(AiLinkState.closed, reason);
      case AiResult(:final rid):
        _pending.remove(rid)?.complete(message);
      default:
        break; // phone → desktop types never come from the desktop
    }
  }

  /// A new channel: the listing and every open mirror resume on it.
  @internal
  void channelReady() {
    if (_watching) _send([const AiList()]);
    for (final link in _links.values) {
      if (link.state != AiLinkState.closed) _attachNow(link);
    }
  }

  @internal
  void channelLost() {
    for (final link in _links.values) {
      if (link.state == AiLinkState.live) link._set(AiLinkState.attaching);
      link._awaitingSnapshot = true;
    }
    const lost = MachineConnectionException(
      MachineConnectionFailure.agentOffline,
    );
    final pending = [..._pending.values];
    _pending.clear();
    for (final c in pending) {
      c.completeError(lost);
    }
  }

  @internal
  void dispose() {
    channelLost();
    for (final link in _links.values) {
      link._dispose();
    }
    _links.clear();
    unawaited(_listings.close());
  }

  // ------------------------------------------------------------ private --

  /// False when there is no channel.
  bool _attachNow(AiSessionLink link) {
    link._awaitingSnapshot = true;
    final have = link.mirror.generation < 0 ? null : link.mirror.have;
    return _send([
      AiAttach(
        sid: link.sid,
        body: have == null ? null : encodeBody(have.toJson()),
      ),
    ]);
  }

  void _detach(AiSessionLink link) {
    if (!identical(_links[link.sid], link)) return;
    _links.remove(link.sid);
    if (link.state != AiLinkState.closed) {
      _send([AiDetach(sid: link.sid)]);
      link._set(AiLinkState.closed, 'detached');
    }
    link._dispose();
  }

  bool _send(List<AppMessage> messages) => _sendRaw(messages);

  Future<AiResult> _request(AiMessage Function(int rid) make) async {
    await _connect();
    final rid = _nextRid++;
    final done = Completer<AiResult>();
    _pending[rid] = done;
    if (!_send([make(rid)])) {
      _pending.remove(rid);
      throw const MachineConnectionException(
        MachineConnectionFailure.agentOffline,
      );
    }
    final result = await done.future.timeout(
      _timeout,
      onTimeout: () {
        _pending.remove(rid);
        throw const MachineConnectionException(
          MachineConnectionFailure.timeout,
        );
      },
    );
    if (!result.ok) throw AiRequestException(result.code ?? 'refused');
    return result;
  }
}
````

### Task 7: Desktop — the AI slot in every runtime; the forwarder in the background service [independent of V2-1b]

`SessionCore` (one per process, outliving every runtime) gets an `AiEndpointSlot`, and every `CoreAgentRuntime` builds its router with it (spec D1).
- **In-process:** the window plugs its hub into the slot in Task 11. Until then phones hear "unavailable", never silence.
- **Background service:** `daemon_main.dart` installs a `MuxAiForwarder` as both `server.ai` and the slot's target. Its pushes go out with the service's own notifications.

This task touches only `src/agent/` and `daemon_main.dart`, which V2-1b does not change.

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/desktop/lib/src/agent/session_core.dart`, `apps/desktop/lib/src/agent/agent_runtime.dart`, `apps/desktop/lib/daemon_main.dart`
- Test: `apps/desktop/test/agent/ai_slot_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 3–5 (`AiEndpointSlot`, `MuxAiForwarder`, `MuxServer.ai`, `AgentNotifications.aiNotice`).
- Produces: `SessionCore.ai` (`final AiEndpointSlot`); every runtime's `SessionRouter` uses it; in the daemon, `MuxAiForwarder` is `server.ai` and `core.ai.target`.

`apps/desktop/test/agent/ai_slot_test.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shellx_agent/shellx_agent.dart';
import 'package:shellx_core/shellx_core.dart';
import 'package:shellx_desktop/src/agent/session_core.dart';

import '../support/fake_pty.dart';

const _phone = '33333333-3333-4333-8333-333333333333';

/// V2-2 D1: every runtime's router sends `ai.*` to the process's slot; it
/// answers "unavailable" until the window (or the background service's
/// forwarder) takes it.
void main() {
  SessionCore core() => SessionCore(
    ptyFactory: FakePtyFactory(),
    shellResolver: const FixedShellResolver(),
    homeDirectory: '/Users/phu',
    baseEnvironment: const {'HOME': '/Users/phu'},
    api: ShellxApi(baseUri: Uri.parse('https://relay.test')),
  );

  test(
    'no target: the phone hears unavailable; a target gets the messages',
    () {
      final c = core();
      final out = <OutboundMessage>[];
      c.ai.outbound.listen(out.add);
      c.ai.handle(_phone, const AiList());
      expect((out.single.message as AiListResult).available, isFalse);

      final forwarder = MuxAiForwarder();
      final toWindow = <MuxMessage>[];
      forwarder.serve(Object(), toWindow.add);
      c.ai.target = forwarder;
      c.ai.handle(_phone, const AiAttach(sid: 'e.-1'));
      expect(toWindow.single.type, MuxType.aiIn);
    },
  );
}
````

### Task 8: Mobile foundation — backend interfaces, fakes, strings, words [independent of V2-1b]

The app sees `shellx_client`'s AI types through interfaces, so tests use fakes (like `TabHandle`):
- `MachineSession.ai` (`AiMachine`);
- `AiSessionHandle`.

The test fakes are realistic: `FakeAiSession` feeds its mirror with bodies made by a real desktop-side `AiSubscription` over a real `AiSessionState`.

Also in this task:
- every string of spec §5.5, in Vietnamese and English;
- `AiWords`: the phone's wording of states, tool cards (V2-1 §4.2), permission options (V2-1 D10), Claude's modes (V2-1 D11), notices (V2-1 §4.5) and failures;
- two pure helpers: diff lines and markdown blocks;
- the mobile dependencies: `shellx_acp`, `shellx_ai`, and `flutter_markdown_plus 1.0.12`, pinned like the desktop's.

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/mobile/pubspec.yaml`, `apps/mobile/lib/core/backend/backend.dart`, `apps/mobile/lib/core/backend/client_backend.dart`, `apps/mobile/test/support/fakes.dart`
- Modify: `apps/mobile/lib/l10n/intl_en.arb`, `apps/mobile/lib/l10n/intl_vi.arb`; generated: `apps/mobile/lib/shared/src/l10n.dart`, `apps/mobile/lib/shared/src/intl/messages_en.dart`, `apps/mobile/lib/shared/src/intl/messages_vi.dart`
- Create: `apps/mobile/lib/features/ai/domain/ai_words.dart`, `apps/mobile/lib/features/ai/domain/ai_text.dart`
- Test: `apps/mobile/test/features/ai/ai_words_test.dart`, `apps/mobile/test/features/ai/ai_text_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 6 (`MachineAi`, `AiSessionLink`, `AiLinkState`, `AiRequestException`); Task 2 (`AiMirror`, `Mirror*`, `AiListing`, `AiSubscription`, `decodeBody`, `AiRemoteState`).
- Produces:
  - `abstract interface class AiSessionHandle { String get sid; AiMirror get mirror; AiLinkState get state; String? get closedReason; Stream<void> get changes; void detach(); void loadEarlier(); }`
  - `abstract interface class AiMachine`:
    - state: `bool? get supported`, `AiListing? get listing`, `Stream<AiListing> get listings`;
    - `Future<void> watch()`, `AiSessionHandle attach(String sid)`;
    - requests: `prompt`, `answerPermission`, `answerAsk`, `cancel`, `forceStop`, `unqueue` (same signatures as `MachineAi`);
    - `Future<String> open(String agent, String folder)`.
  - `MachineSession.ai` (`AiMachine get ai`); the production adapter wraps `MachineClient.ai`.
  - strings: the `ai*` keys of Step 2 (e.g. `aiPermAllowOnce` "Cho phép", `aiRowSubtitle(agent, state)`, `aiTurnFiles(int count)`).
  - `abstract final class AiWords`:
    - lists: `state(S, AiRemoteState)`, `headState(MirrorHead)`;
    - session view: `clock(Duration)`, `option(S, PermissionOption)`, `mode(S, MirrorHead)`;
    - cards: `fileName(String)`, `toolName(String)`, `toolTitle(S, MirrorTool)`, `notice(S, MirrorNotice, String agent)`;
    - `failure(S, String code, {bool answer = false})` (nullable); `kAiMaxPromptKb = 32`.
  - helpers: `typedef DiffLine = ({String mark, String text})`, `List<DiffLine> diffLines(String? oldText, String newText)`, `List<String> markdownBlocks(String text)`.
  - test fakes (`test/support/fakes.dart`):
    - `FakeAiSession(sid, {AiSessionState? desktop})` with `desktop`, `sync()`, `close(reason)`, `lose()`, `detached`, `earlierCalls`;
    - `FakeAiMachine` with `supportedValue`, `listingValue`, `emitListing`, `session(sid)`, `failWith`, `gate`, `openedSid`, `prompts`, `permissionAnswers`, `askAnswers`, `cancels`, `forceStops`, `unqueued`, `opened`, `watchCalls`;
    - `FakeMachineSession.ai` (`FakeAiMachine`).

````dart
  /// closes its session on the machine: at once, or — without a channel —
  /// right after the next channel is installed. A desktop tab
  /// ([SessionKind.desktop]) only leaves the terminal (`session.detach`): the
  /// window keeps running on the computer. An ended tab is only removed here.
  Future<void> closeTab(int id);
}
````

````dart
  /// Removes the tab and ends its [TabHandle.events]. An attached tab also
  /// closes its session on the machine: at once, or — without a channel —
  /// right after the next channel is installed. A desktop tab
  /// ([SessionKind.desktop]) only leaves the terminal (`session.detach`): the
  /// window keeps running on the computer. An ended tab is only removed here.
  Future<void> closeTab(int id);
}

/// App-side view of one mirrored AI session ([AiSessionLink] in `shellx_client`).
abstract interface class AiSessionHandle {
  String get sid;

  /// What the phone holds of the session; changes come on [changes].
  AiMirror get mirror;
  AiLinkState get state;

  /// `gone`, `desktop_closed`, `unavailable`, `detached`, or a newer word.
  String? get closedReason;
  Stream<void> get changes;

  /// Stops mirroring (the screen closed).
  void detach();

  /// "Tải thêm": older items.
  void loadEarlier();
}

/// App-side view of a machine's AI sessions ([MachineAi] in `shellx_client`). Every request
/// connects first; it throws [AiRequestException] (the computer said no, with its code) or
/// [MachineConnectionException].
abstract interface class AiMachine {
  /// Null until the first session list; false for a computer without AI for phones.
  bool? get supported;
  AiListing? get listing;
  Stream<AiListing> get listings;

  /// Keeps [listing] current while the app is connected.
  Future<void> watch();
  AiSessionHandle attach(String sid);
  Future<void> prompt(String sid, String text);
  Future<void> answerPermission(String sid, int permission, String option);
  Future<void> answerAsk(String sid, int ask, ElicitationResponse response);
  Future<void> cancel(String sid);
  Future<void> forceStop(String sid);
  Future<void> unqueue(String sid, int queued);

  /// A new session; its sid.
  Future<String> open(String agent, String folder);
}

/// Everything the signed-in UI needs from `shellx_core` + `shellx_client`.
````

````dart
final class _AiMachineAdapter implements AiMachine {
  _AiMachineAdapter(this._ai);

  final MachineAi _ai;
  final Map<AiSessionLink, _AiSessionAdapter> _links = Map.identity();

  @override
  bool? get supported => _ai.supported;

  @override
  AiListing? get listing => _ai.listing;

  @override
  Stream<AiListing> get listings => _ai.listings;

  @override
  Future<void> watch() => _ai.watch();

  @override
  AiSessionHandle attach(String sid) {
    final link = _ai.attach(sid);
    _links.removeWhere((key, _) => key.state == AiLinkState.closed && !identical(key, link));
    return _links.putIfAbsent(link, () => _AiSessionAdapter(link));
  }

  @override
  Future<void> prompt(String sid, String text) => _ai.prompt(sid, text);

  @override
  Future<void> answerPermission(String sid, int permission, String option) =>
      _ai.answerPermission(sid, permission, option);

  @override
  Future<void> answerAsk(String sid, int ask, ElicitationResponse response) => _ai.answerAsk(sid, ask, response);

  @override
  Future<void> cancel(String sid) => _ai.cancel(sid);

  @override
  Future<void> forceStop(String sid) => _ai.forceStop(sid);

  @override
  Future<void> unqueue(String sid, int queued) => _ai.unqueue(sid, queued);

  @override
  Future<String> open(String agent, String folder) => _ai.open(agent, folder);
}

final class _AiSessionAdapter implements AiSessionHandle {
  _AiSessionAdapter(this._link);

  final AiSessionLink _link;

  @override
  String get sid => _link.sid;

  @override
  AiMirror get mirror => _link.mirror;

  @override
  AiLinkState get state => _link.state;

  @override
  String? get closedReason => _link.closedReason;

  @override
  Stream<void> get changes => _link.changes;

  @override
  void detach() => _link.detach();

  @override
  void loadEarlier() => _link.loadEarlier();
}
````

````dart
/// A mirrored AI session for tests (V2-2). Fed like the real one: a desktop-side
/// [AiSubscription] over [desktop] makes the bodies, [sync] applies them as the phone would.
class FakeAiSession implements AiSessionHandle {
  FakeAiSession(this.sid, {AiSessionState? desktop})
    : desktop =
          desktop ?? (AiSessionState(agentId: 'claude', agentName: 'Claude', cwd: '/p')..setPhase(AgentPhase.ready));

  @override
  final String sid;

  /// The session as the computer holds it; tests change it like the agent would.
  final AiSessionState desktop;
  late final AiSubscription _sub = AiSubscription(desktop, generation: 1);
  var _attached = false;

  @override
  final AiMirror mirror = AiMirror();
  AiLinkState stateValue = AiLinkState.attaching;
  String? closedReasonValue;
  final StreamController<void> _changes = StreamController<void>.broadcast(sync: true);
  bool detached = false;
  int earlierCalls = 0;

  /// Brings the phone's mirror up to date with [desktop] (the snapshot first).
  void sync() {
    final out = _attached ? _sub.delta() : _sub.attach(null);
    _attached = true;
    for (final o in out) {
      mirror.apply(decodeBody(o.body)!, snapshot: o.snapshot);
      _sub.ack(o.seq);
    }
    stateValue = AiLinkState.live;
    _changes.add(null);
  }

  /// The computer closed it ([reason]: `gone`, `desktop_closed`, …).
  void close(String reason) {
    stateValue = AiLinkState.closed;
    closedReasonValue = reason;
    _changes.add(null);
  }

  /// The channel dropped: the mirror stays, the link re-attaches.
  void lose() {
    stateValue = AiLinkState.attaching;
    _changes.add(null);
  }

  @override
  AiLinkState get state => stateValue;

  @override
  String? get closedReason => closedReasonValue;

  @override
  Stream<void> get changes => _changes.stream;

  @override
  void detach() => detached = true;

  @override
  void loadEarlier() {
    earlierCalls++;
    for (final o in _sub.earlier(mirror.from)) {
      mirror.apply(decodeBody(o.body)!, snapshot: false);
      _sub.ack(o.seq);
    }
    _changes.add(null);
  }
}

/// Scriptable [AiMachine] (V2-2): records every request; [failWith] makes them fail with that
/// `ai.result` code.
class FakeAiMachine implements AiMachine {
  bool? supportedValue;
  AiListing? listingValue;
  final StreamController<AiListing> _listings = StreamController<AiListing>.broadcast(sync: true);
  final Map<String, FakeAiSession> sessions = {};
  final List<(String, String)> prompts = [];
  final List<(String, int, String)> permissionAnswers = [];
  final List<(String, int, ElicitationResponse)> askAnswers = [];
  final List<String> cancels = [];
  final List<String> forceStops = [];
  final List<(String, int)> unqueued = [];
  final List<(String, String)> opened = [];
  String? failWith;
  String openedSid = 'e.-9';
  int watchCalls = 0;

  /// Requests wait for this when set (an answer still travelling).
  Completer<void>? gate;

  void emitListing(AiListing listing) {
    listingValue = listing;
    _listings.add(listing);
  }

  FakeAiSession session(String sid) => sessions.putIfAbsent(sid, () => FakeAiSession(sid));

  Future<void> _request() async {
    await gate?.future;
    final code = failWith;
    if (code != null) throw AiRequestException(code);
  }

  @override
  bool? get supported => supportedValue;

  @override
  AiListing? get listing => listingValue;

  @override
  Stream<AiListing> get listings => _listings.stream;

  @override
  Future<void> watch() async => watchCalls++;

  @override
  AiSessionHandle attach(String sid) => session(sid);

  @override
  Future<void> prompt(String sid, String text) async {
    prompts.add((sid, text));
    await _request();
  }

  @override
  Future<void> answerPermission(String sid, int permission, String option) async {
    permissionAnswers.add((sid, permission, option));
    await _request();
  }

  @override
  Future<void> answerAsk(String sid, int ask, ElicitationResponse response) async {
    askAnswers.add((sid, ask, response));
    await _request();
  }

  @override
  Future<void> cancel(String sid) async {
    cancels.add(sid);
    await _request();
  }

  @override
  Future<void> forceStop(String sid) async {
    forceStops.add(sid);
    await _request();
  }

  @override
  Future<void> unqueue(String sid, int queued) async {
    unqueued.add((sid, queued));
    await _request();
  }

  @override
  Future<String> open(String agent, String folder) async {
    opened.add((agent, folder));
    await _request();
    return openedSid;
  }
}
````

````bash
 ("aiAttentionTitle", "{agent} needs your answer", "{agent} cần bạn trả lời"),

import json, sys, re
def placeholders(text):
    names=[]
    # ICU plural: {count, plural, ...}
    for m in re.finditer(r"\{(\w+)(,|\})", text):
        n=m.group(1)
        if n not in names: names.append(n)
    return names
INTS = {'count', 'n', 'percent', 'done', 'total', 'max', 'code', 'version'}
def patch(path, lang):
    """Appends the keys before the closing brace; the existing lines stay byte for byte."""
    text = open(path, encoding='utf-8').read().rstrip()
    assert text.endswith('}'), path
    body = text[:-1].rstrip()
    existing = json.loads(text)
    lines = []
    for key, en, vi in STRINGS:
        assert key not in existing, key
        value = en if lang == 'en' else vi
        lines.append('  ' + json.dumps(key) + ': ' + json.dumps(value, ensure_ascii=False))
        names = placeholders(value)
        if lang == 'en' and names:
            meta = {'placeholders': {n: {'type': 'int' if n in INTS else 'String'} for n in names}}
            lines.append('  ' + json.dumps('@' + key) + ': ' + json.dumps(meta, ensure_ascii=False))
    out = body + ',\n' + ',\n'.join(lines) + '\n}\n'
    json.loads(out)  # still valid JSON
    open(path, 'w', encoding='utf-8').write(out)
if __name__ == '__main__':
    root = sys.argv[1]
    patch(root + '/lib/l10n/intl_en.arb', 'en')
    patch(root + '/lib/l10n/intl_vi.arb', 'vi')
EOF
PATH=~/fvm/versions/3.41.4/bin:$PATH dart run intl_utils:generate
````

````dart
/// V2-2 §5: the phone says what the desktop says (V2-1 §4.2, D10, D11), in Vietnamese and English.
/// The strings follow the last loaded locale (intl_utils): load right before use.
void main() {
  test('tool cards: a verb and what it acts on', () async {
    final vi = await S.load(const Locale('vi'));
    expect(AiWords.toolTitle(vi, _tool(ToolKind.read, title: 'Read File', path: '/p/src/main.ts')), 'Đọc main.ts');
    expect(
      AiWords.toolTitle(
        vi,
        _tool(
          ToolKind.edit,
          diff: const MirrorDiff(path: 'C:\\p\\a.ts', newText: 'x'),
        ),
      ),
      'Tạo a.ts',
    );
    expect(
      AiWords.toolTitle(
        vi,
        _tool(
          ToolKind.edit,
          diff: const MirrorDiff(path: '/p/a.ts', oldText: 'a', newText: 'b'),
        ),
      ),
      'Sửa a.ts',
    );
    expect(AiWords.toolTitle(vi, _tool(ToolKind.execute, title: 'npm test')), 'Chạy npm test');
    final en = await S.load(const Locale('en'));
    expect(AiWords.toolTitle(en, _tool(ToolKind.other, title: 'mcp__github__create_issue')), 'github · create_issue');
  });

  test('permission options by kind; the agent\'s words for an unknown kind', () async {
    final vi = await S.load(const Locale('vi'));
    PermissionOption o(PermissionKind kind) => PermissionOption(optionId: 'x', name: 'Agent words', kind: kind);
    expect(AiWords.option(vi, o(PermissionKind.allowOnce)), 'Cho phép');
    expect(AiWords.option(vi, o(PermissionKind.allowAlways)), 'Luôn cho phép');
    expect(AiWords.option(vi, o(PermissionKind.rejectOnce)), 'Từ chối');
    expect(AiWords.option(vi, o(PermissionKind.rejectAlways)), 'Luôn từ chối');
    expect(AiWords.option(vi, o(PermissionKind.other)), 'Agent words');
  });

  test('modes, states, clock and failures', () async {
    final vi = await S.load(const Locale('vi'));
    const head = MirrorHead(phase: 'running', agentId: 'claude', agentName: 'Claude', modeId: 'acceptEdits');
    expect(AiWords.mode(vi, head), 'Tự sửa tệp');
    expect(AiWords.headState(head), AiRemoteState.running);
    expect(AiWords.state(vi, AiRemoteState.needsInput), 'Cần bạn trả lời');
    expect(AiWords.clock(const Duration(seconds: 72)), '1:12');
    expect(AiWords.clock(const Duration(hours: 1, minutes: 2, seconds: 3)), '1:02:03');
    expect(AiWords.failure(vi, 'stale', answer: true), 'Đã được trả lời ở thiết bị khác.');
    expect(AiWords.failure(vi, 'stale'), isNull);
    expect(AiWords.failure(vi, 'too_large'), 'Quá dài để gửi từ điện thoại (tối đa 32 KB).');
  });
}
````
