`apps/mobile/lib/features/ai/domain/ai_words.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:shellx_acp/shellx_acp.dart';
import 'package:shellx_ai/remote.dart';
import 'package:shellx_mobile/shared/shared.dart';

/// V2-2 §5: the words of AI sessions on the phone (the desktop's V2-1 §4
/// wording, said the same way).
abstract final class AiWords {
  /// A session's state in a list or header.
  static String state(S s, AiRemoteState state) => switch (state) {
    AiRemoteState.running => s.aiStateRunning,
    AiRemoteState.needsInput => s.aiStateNeedsInput,
    AiRemoteState.idle => s.aiStateIdle,
    AiRemoteState.starting => s.aiStateStarting,
    AiRemoteState.stopped => s.aiStateStopped,
    AiRemoteState.needsAuth => s.aiStateNeedsAuth,
    AiRemoteState.failed => s.aiStateFailed,
    AiRemoteState.noFolder => s.aiStateNoFolder,
    AiRemoteState.agentOff => s.aiStateAgentOff,
  };

  /// The header's state: what the desktop's runtime phase and the pending
  /// requests say (the same rule as `aiRemoteStateOf`).
  static AiRemoteState headState(MirrorHead head) {
    if (head.unavailable != null) return head.unavailable!;
    if (head.permission != null || head.ask != null) return AiRemoteState.needsInput;
    return switch (head.phase) {
      'running' => AiRemoteState.running,
      'starting' => AiRemoteState.starting,
      'needsAuth' => AiRemoteState.needsAuth,
      'failed' => AiRemoteState.failed,
      'ready' => AiRemoteState.idle,
      _ => AiRemoteState.stopped,
    };
  }

  /// "0:12", "3:05", "1:02:03".
  static String clock(Duration d) {
    final total = d.isNegative ? 0 : d.inSeconds;
    final h = total ~/ 3600;
    final m = (total % 3600) ~/ 60;
    final sec = (total % 60).toString().padLeft(2, '0');
    return h > 0 ? '$h:${m.toString().padLeft(2, '0')}:$sec' : '$m:$sec';
  }

  /// V2-1 D10: the main label of a permission option by kind; the agent's
  /// own words when the kind is unknown.
  static String option(S s, PermissionOption o) => switch (o.kind) {
    PermissionKind.allowOnce => s.aiPermAllowOnce,
    PermissionKind.allowAlways => s.aiPermAllowAlways,
    PermissionKind.rejectOnce => s.aiPermRejectOnce,
    PermissionKind.rejectAlways => s.aiPermRejectAlways,
    PermissionKind.other => o.name,
  };

  /// V2-1 D11: Claude's known mode ids in the app's words; others as sent.
  static String? mode(S s, MirrorHead head) => switch (head.modeId) {
    'default' => s.aiModeDefault,
    'acceptEdits' => s.aiModeAcceptEdits,
    'plan' => s.aiModePlan,
    'auto' => s.aiModeAuto,
    'bypassPermissions' => s.aiModeBypass,
    _ => head.modeName,
  };

  /// The last part of [path] ("src/main.ts" → "main.ts"), both separators.
  static String fileName(String path) {
    final parts = path.split(RegExp(r'[\\/]'))..removeWhere((p) => p.isEmpty);
    return parts.isEmpty ? path : parts.last;
  }

  /// `mcp__server__tool` → "server · tool" (V2-1 §4.2).
  static String toolName(String title) {
    final m = RegExp(r'^mcp__([^_]+(?:_[^_]+)*)__(.+)$').firstMatch(title);
    return m == null ? title : '${m.group(1)} · ${m.group(2)}';
  }

  /// A tool card's one line (V2-1 §4.2): a verb and what it acts on.
  static String toolTitle(S s, MirrorTool t) {
    final location = t.locations.isEmpty ? null : t.locations.first.path;
    final diff = t.diffs.isEmpty ? null : t.diffs.first;
    final title = t.title ?? '';
    final target = location != null
        ? fileName(location)
        : diff != null
        ? fileName(diff.path)
        : title;
    return switch (t.kind) {
      ToolKind.read => s.aiToolRead(target),
      ToolKind.edit when diff != null && diff.oldText == null => s.aiToolCreate(target),
      ToolKind.edit => s.aiToolEdit(target),
      ToolKind.delete => s.aiToolDelete(target),
      ToolKind.move => s.aiToolMove(target),
      ToolKind.search => s.aiToolSearch(title),
      ToolKind.fetch => s.aiToolFetch(title),
      ToolKind.execute => s.aiToolExecute(title),
      ToolKind.think => s.aiThought,
      ToolKind.switchMode => s.aiToolSwitchMode,
      ToolKind.other || null => toolName(title),
    };
  }

  /// A notice card's text (V2-1 §4.5).
  static String notice(S s, MirrorNotice n, String agent) {
    int arg(String key) => n.args[key] is int ? n.args[key]! as int : 0;
    return switch (n.kind) {
      'login' => s.aiNoticeLogin(agent),
      'node' => s.aiNoticeNode(agent, arg('required')),
      'not_installed' => s.aiNoticeNotInstalled(agent),
      'missing' => s.aiNoticeMissing(agent),
      'exited' => s.aiNoticeExited(agent, arg('code')),
      'invalid' => s.aiNoticeInvalid(agent),
      'start_failed' => s.aiNoticeStartFailed(agent),
      'cannot_resume' => s.aiNoticeCannotResume,
      'folder_missing' => s.aiNoticeFolderMissing,
      'undo_off' => s.aiNoticeUndoOff,
      _ => s.aiNoticeOther,
    };
  }

  /// What a failed request shows (`ai.result` codes, V2-2 §4.6); null when
  /// nothing needs saying (a stop that came after the turn ended: the
  /// header already shows it). [answer]: the request was an answer.
  static String? failure(S s, String code, {bool answer = false}) => switch (code) {
    'stale' => answer ? s.aiAnsweredElsewhere : null,
    'queue_full' => s.aiQueueFull,
    'too_large' => s.aiTooLong(kAiMaxPromptKb),
    'offline' => s.aiReconnecting,
    'unavailable' => s.aiUnavailable,
    'not_found' || 'gone' => s.aiClosedGone,
    _ => s.aiRequestFailed(code),
  };

  /// The prompt limit in KB, for [S.aiTooLong].
  static const int kAiMaxPromptKb = 32;
}
````

`apps/mobile/lib/features/ai/domain/ai_text.dart`:

````dart
/// One line of a phone diff (V2-2 D6): ' ' context, '-' removed, '+' added.
typedef DiffLine = ({String mark, String text});

/// The lines of a diff region the desktop sent: the lines both sides share
/// at the start and at the end are context, the middle is removed then
/// added (the desktop already cut the region to its changed lines and 3
/// around them). [oldText] null: a new file, every line added.
List<DiffLine> diffLines(String? oldText, String newText) {
  final after = newText.isEmpty ? <String>[] : newText.split('\n');
  if (oldText == null) return [for (final l in after) (mark: '+', text: l)];
  final before = oldText.isEmpty ? <String>[] : oldText.split('\n');
  var start = 0;
  while (start < before.length && start < after.length && before[start] == after[start]) {
    start++;
  }
  var endOld = before.length;
  var endNew = after.length;
  while (endOld > start && endNew > start && before[endOld - 1] == after[endNew - 1]) {
    endOld--;
    endNew--;
  }
  return [
    for (final l in before.sublist(0, start)) (mark: ' ', text: l),
    for (final l in before.sublist(start, endOld)) (mark: '-', text: l),
    for (final l in after.sublist(start, endNew)) (mark: '+', text: l),
    for (final l in after.sublist(endNew)) (mark: ' ', text: l),
  ];
}

/// [text] split into markdown blocks at blank lines outside code fences, so
/// a streaming answer re-renders only its last block (V2-1 D17, V2-2 §5).
List<String> markdownBlocks(String text) {
  final blocks = <String>[];
  final current = StringBuffer();
  var fence = false;
  for (final line in text.split('\n')) {
    final trimmed = line.trimLeft();
    if (trimmed.startsWith('```') || trimmed.startsWith('~~~')) fence = !fence;
    if (!fence && line.trim().isEmpty) {
      if (current.isNotEmpty) {
        blocks.add(current.toString());
        current.clear();
      }
      continue;
    }
    if (current.isNotEmpty) current.write('\n');
    current.write(line);
  }
  if (current.isNotEmpty) blocks.add(current.toString());
  return blocks;
}
````

### Task 9: Mobile — the AI session page [independent of V2-1b]

The page of spec §5.2–5.4, `AiSessionRoute(agentId, machineName, sid)`:
- **Header:** title, then status "Claude · Đang chạy · 0:12 · Hỏi trước · Ngữ cảnh 16%".
- **Banners:** the connection banner; the computer unreachable (`errorAgentOffline`, instead of a spinner); "reconnecting" over a kept mirror; closed.
- **A session that cannot run (D14):** its state ("Chọn thư mục trên máy tính") in the middle, and no dock.
- **Timeline (`AiTimeline`):**
  - a `CustomScrollView` whose `center` is the first item of the first snapshot, so new items below and earlier pages above never move what is read;
  - items keyed by `(index, rev)`;
  - it follows the bottom only while the user is there; "Mới nhất" otherwise; "Tải thêm" at the top.
- **Items:**
  - user bubble;
  - markdown answer (blocks, never images, http/https links on tap);
  - folded thought;
  - tool card (verb + target + state; tap for diff/output/input; open while it waits for permission; "Bạn chọn: …");
  - question line, turn summary, notice, `big` stub.
- **Dock:**
  - plan line;
  - permission panel: big buttons in the agent's order, a haptic, waiting while the answer travels, "Xem chi tiết" scrolls to the card;
  - question form, submittable only when `accepts`;
  - queue with ×;
  - composer: Send / Queue / Stop / Force stop; the text stays when sending fails.

**`AiSessionController`** attaches on creation and detaches on dispose. Each request returns its failure code, and the page words it.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/mobile/lib/features/ai/presentation/controllers/ai_session_controller.dart`
- Create: `apps/mobile/lib/features/ai/presentation/widgets/ai_markdown.dart`, `ai_items.dart`, `ai_dock.dart`, `ai_timeline.dart`
- Create: `apps/mobile/lib/features/ai/presentation/pages/ai_session_page.dart`
- Modify: `apps/mobile/lib/core/router/app_router.dart`; generated: `apps/mobile/lib/core/router/app_router.gr.dart`, `apps/mobile/lib/core/dependency/dependency.config.dart`
- Test: `apps/mobile/test/features/ai/ai_session_page_test.dart`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 8 (`AiMachine`, `AiSessionHandle`, `AiWords`, `diffLines`, `markdownBlocks`, fakes, strings); `SessionHolder`, `PopupHandler`, `ExternalLinks`, `ConnectionBanner`, `NoticeBanner`, `AppButton`.
- Produces:
  - `@injectable class AiSessionController extends Controller`:
    - constructor: `AiSessionController(@factoryParam String agentId, @factoryParam String sid, SessionHolder session)`;
    - signals: `version`, `closed`, `machineState`, `sending`, `answering` (`int?`), `stopping`, `now`;
    - getters: `backend`, `mirror`, `head`, `linkState`, `closedReason`, `canSend`;
    - requests (each `Future<String?>`, null = done): `send(String)`, `answerPermission(MirrorPermission, PermissionOption)`, `answerAsk(int, ElicitationResponse)`, `stop()`, `forceStop()`, `unqueue(int)`;
    - `void loadEarlier()`, `void dispose()`.
  - widgets: `AiMarkdown`, `AiItemView`, `AiToolCard`, `AiDiffView`, `AiPermissionPanel`, `AiAskPanel`, `AiQueueRow`, `AiComposer`, `AiPlanStrip`, `AiTimeline`.
  - `@RoutePage() AiSessionPage({required agentId, required machineName, required sid})` and the generated `AiSessionRoute` / `AiSessionRouteArgs`.
  - keys:
    - page and header: `ai.back`, `ai.title`, `ai.status`, `ai.closed`, `ai.offline`, `ai.unavailable`;
    - timeline: `ai.timeline`, `ai.loadEarlier`, `ai.latest`;
    - permission: `ai.permission`, `ai.option.<optionId>`, `ai.permission.details`;
    - question: `ai.ask`, `ai.ask.submit`, `ai.ask.decline`;
    - dock: `ai.composer`, `ai.send`, `ai.stop`, `ai.forceStop`, `ai.unqueue.<id>`, `ai.plan`.

````dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter_test/flutter_test.dart';
import 'package:shellx_acp/shellx_acp.dart';
import 'package:shellx_ai/shellx_ai.dart';
import 'package:shellx_client/shellx_client.dart';
import 'package:shellx_mobile/core/core.dart' hide test;
import 'package:shellx_mobile/features/ai/presentation/controllers/ai_session_controller.dart';

import '../../support/app_harness.dart';
import '../../support/fake_links.dart';
import '../../support/fake_popup_handler.dart';
import '../../support/fakes.dart';

const sid = 'e.-1';

void say(AiSessionState s, String text) => s.applyUpdate(
  SessionUpdate.fromJson({
    'sessionUpdate': 'agent_message_chunk',
    'content': {'type': 'text', 'text': text},
  }),
  DateTime.now(),
);

void tool(AiSessionState s, String id, Map<String, Object?> fields) =>
    s.applyUpdate(SessionUpdate.fromJson({'sessionUpdate': 'tool_call', 'toolCallId': id, ...fields}), DateTime.now());

PermissionRequest perm() => PermissionRequest.fromJson({
  'sessionId': 's',
  'toolCall': {'toolCallId': 'c1', 'title': 'Edit src/main.ts', 'kind': 'edit'},
  'options': [
    {'optionId': 'allow', 'name': 'Yes', 'kind': 'allow_once'},
    {'optionId': 'always', 'name': 'Yes, allow all edits during this session', 'kind': 'allow_always'},
    {'optionId': 'reject', 'name': 'No', 'kind': 'reject_once'},
  ],
});

/// V2-2 §5.2–5.3: the AI session page.
void main() {
  late FakeBackend backend;
  late FakeMachineSession machine;
  late FakeSessionHolder session;
  late FakePopupHandler popups;

  setUp(() {
    backend = FakeBackend(pinnedList: [pinned(agentA)]);
    machine = backend.addMachine(agentA);
    session = FakeSessionHolder(backend);
    popups = FakePopupHandler();
    getIt
      ..registerSingleton<PopupHandler>(popups)
      ..registerSingleton<SessionHolder>(session)
      ..registerSingleton<ExternalLinks>(FakeExternalLinks())
      ..registerFactoryParam<AiSessionController, String, String>(
        (agentId, sid) => AiSessionController(agentId, sid, session),
      );
  });

  tearDown(() async => getIt.reset());

  /// Opens the page of [sid]; the desktop state is `machine.ai.session(sid).desktop`.
  Future<FakeAiSession> open(WidgetTester tester, {List<Object?>? results}) async {
    tester.view.physicalSize = const Size(1170, 2532);
    tester.view.devicePixelRatio = 3;
    addTearDown(tester.view.reset);
    final ai = machine.ai.session(sid);
    await tester.pumpWidget(
      routedApp(
        open: () => AiSessionRoute(agentId: agentA, machineName: 'MacBook', sid: sid),
        routes: [AutoRoute(page: AiSessionRoute.page)],
        results: results,
      ),
    );
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    // Not openPushed: the page shows a spinner until the snapshot arrives.
    await tester.tap(find.text('open'));
    await tester.pump();
    await tester.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 500));
    ai.sync();
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    return ai;
  }

  testWidgets('the conversation as the desktop holds it; the status in the header', (tester) async {
    final ai = machine.ai.session(sid);
    ai.desktop.beginTurn(const [TextBlock('Thêm nút đăng xuất')], DateTime.now());
    say(ai.desktop, 'Mình sẽ thêm **nút** và ![ảnh](https://example.com/x.png) xong.');
    tool(ai.desktop, 'c1', {
      'title': 'Read main.ts',
      'kind': 'read',
      'status': 'completed',
      'locations': [
        {'path': '/p/src/main.ts'},
      ],
    });
    await open(tester);
    final timeline = find.byKey(const Key('ai.timeline'));
    expect(find.descendant(of: timeline, matching: find.text('Thêm nút đăng xuất')), findsOneWidget);
    expect(tester.widget<Text>(find.byKey(const Key('ai.title'))).data, 'Thêm nút đăng xuất');
    expect(find.textContaining('Mình sẽ thêm'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.byType(Image), findsNothing); // markdown never loads images
    expect(find.text('Đọc main.ts'), findsOneWidget);
    final status = tester.widget<Text>(find.byKey(const Key('ai.status'))).data!;
    expect(status, startsWith('Claude · Đang chạy · 0:0'));
  });

  testWidgets('a permission: big buttons in the agent\'s order, one tap answers once, '
      'nothing is ever answered without a tap', (tester) async {
    final ai = machine.ai.session(sid);
    ai.desktop.beginTurn(const [TextBlock('Sửa')], DateTime.now());
    final p = ai.desktop.addPermission(perm(), DateTime.now());
    await open(tester);
    expect(find.byKey(const Key('ai.permission')), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Edit src/main.ts'), findsWidgets);
    expect(find.text('Cho phép'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Luôn cho phép'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Yes, allow all edits during this session'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.text('Từ chối'), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.pump(const Duration(seconds: 10));
    expect(machine.ai.permissionAnswers, isEmpty);

    machine.ai.gate = Completer<void>();
    await tester.tap(find.byKey(const Key('ai.option.allow')));
    await tester.pump();
    await tester.tap(find.byKey(const Key('ai.option.reject')));
    await tester.pump();
    expect(machine.ai.permissionAnswers, [(sid, p.id, 'allow')]);
    machine.ai.gate!.complete();
    ai.desktop.resolvePermission(p.id, const PermissionSelected('allow'));
    ai.sync();
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.byKey(const Key('ai.permission')), findsNothing);
    expect(find.text('Bạn chọn: Cho phép (Yes)'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('answered on another device: says so', (tester) async {
    final ai = machine.ai.session(sid);
    ai.desktop.beginTurn(const [TextBlock('Sửa')], DateTime.now());
    ai.desktop.addPermission(perm(), DateTime.now());
    await open(tester);
    machine.ai.failWith = 'stale';
    await tester.tap(find.byKey(const Key('ai.option.allow')));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(popups.errors, ['Đã được trả lời ở thiết bị khác.']);
  });

  testWidgets('send, queue while running, stop; a refused send keeps the text', (tester) async {
    final ai = await open(tester);
    expect(find.text('Chưa có tin nhắn. Viết tin đầu tiên ở ô dưới.'), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.enterText(find.byKey(const Key('ai.composer')), 'Thêm nút đăng xuất');
    await tester.pump();
    await tester.tap(find.byKey(const Key('ai.send')));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(machine.ai.prompts, [(sid, 'Thêm nút đăng xuất')]);
    expect(find.text('Thêm nút đăng xuất'), findsNothing); // cleared (the desktop has not echoed it yet)

    ai.desktop.beginTurn(const [TextBlock('Thêm nút đăng xuất')], DateTime.now());
    ai.sync();
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.byKey(const Key('ai.stop')), findsOneWidget);
    await tester.tap(find.byKey(const Key('ai.stop')));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(machine.ai.cancels, [sid]);

    machine.ai.failWith = 'queue_full';
    await tester.enterText(find.byKey(const Key('ai.composer')), 'thêm nữa');
    await tester.pump();
    expect(tester.widget<IconButton>(find.byKey(const Key('ai.send'))).tooltip, 'Xếp hàng');
    await tester.tap(find.byKey(const Key('ai.send')));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(popups.errors, ['Đã có 5 tin đang chờ.']);
    expect(find.text('thêm nữa'), findsOneWidget);
  });

  testWidgets('a question: Gửi only once the form accepts the values', (tester) async {
    final ai = machine.ai.session(sid);
    ai.desktop.beginTurn(const [TextBlock('go')], DateTime.now());
    final ask = ai.desktop.addAsk(
      ElicitationRequest.fromJson({
        'mode': 'form',
        'message': 'Màu nào?',
        'requestedSchema': {
          'type': 'object',
          'properties': {
            'pick': {
              'type': 'string',
              'oneOf': [
                {'const': 'a', 'title': 'Xanh'},
                {'const': 'b', 'title': 'Đỏ'},
              ],
            },
          },
          'required': ['pick'],
        },
      }),
    )!;
    await open(tester);
    expect(find.byKey(const Key('ai.ask')), findsOneWidget);
    final submit = find.byKey(const Key('ai.ask.submit'));
    await tester.tap(submit);
    await tester.pump();
    expect(machine.ai.askAnswers, isEmpty);
    await tester.tap(find.text('Đỏ'));
    await tester.pump();
    await tester.tap(submit);
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    final (_, id, response) = machine.ai.askAnswers.single;
    expect(id, ask.id);
    expect((response as ElicitAccept).content, {'pick': 'b'});
  });

  testWidgets('closed on the computer, or reconnecting: says so', (tester) async {
    final ai = machine.ai.session(sid);
    ai.desktop.beginTurn(const [TextBlock('go')], DateTime.now());
    await open(tester);
    ai.lose();
    await tester.pump();
    expect(find.text('Đang kết nối lại… nội dung có thể chưa mới nhất.'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(
      find.descendant(of: find.byKey(const Key('ai.timeline')), matching: find.text('go')),
      findsOneWidget,
    ); // the mirror stays
    ai.close('desktop_closed');
    await tester.pump();
    expect(find.text('ShellX trên máy tính đã thoát. Mở lại để tiếp tục.'), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.byKey(const Key('ai.composer')), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('the computer cannot be reached: says so instead of a spinner', (tester) async {
    tester.view.physicalSize = const Size(1170, 2532);
    tester.view.devicePixelRatio = 3;
    addTearDown(tester.view.reset);
    await tester.pumpWidget(
      routedApp(
        open: () => AiSessionRoute(agentId: agentA, machineName: 'MacBook', sid: sid),
        routes: [AutoRoute(page: AiSessionRoute.page)],
      ),
    );
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.text('open'));
    await tester.pump();
    machine.emitState(const MachineFailed(MachineConnectionFailure.agentOffline));
    await tester.pump(const Duration(milliseconds: 500));
    expect(find.byKey(const Key('ai.offline')), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.byType(CircularProgressIndicator), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('a session that cannot run says why, without a text field (D14)', (tester) async {
    final ai = await open(tester);
    ai.mirror.apply({
      'g': 0,
      'from': 0,
      'n': 0,
      'reset': true,
      'head': {'phase': 'stopped', 'agent': 'claude', 'name': 'Claude', 'unavailable': 'no_folder'},
      'items': <Object?>[],
    }, snapshot: true);
    ai.sync(); // nothing new from the desktop state; the link stays live
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(find.text('Chọn thư mục trên máy tính'), findsWidgets);
    expect(find.byKey(const Key('ai.unavailable')), findsOneWidget);
    expect(find.byKey(const Key('ai.composer')), findsNothing);
  });

  testWidgets('older items come with Tải thêm; leaving detaches', (tester) async {
    final ai = machine.ai.session(sid);
    ai.desktop.beginTurn(const [TextBlock('go')], DateTime.now());
    for (var i = 0; i < 100; i++) {
      tool(ai.desktop, 'c$i', {'title': 'Read f$i.ts', 'kind': 'read'});
    }
    await open(tester);
    expect(ai.mirror.hasEarlier, isTrue);
    await tester.drag(find.byKey(const Key('ai.timeline')), const Offset(0, 30000));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    await tester.tap(find.byKey(const Key('ai.loadEarlier')));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect((ai.earlierCalls, ai.mirror.from), (1, 0));
    await tester.tap(find.byKey(const Key('ai.back')));
    await tester.pumpAndSettle();
    expect(ai.detached, isTrue);
  });
}
````

`apps/mobile/lib/features/ai/presentation/controllers/ai_session_controller.dart`:

````dart
import 'dart:async';

import 'package:flutter/foundation.dart';
import 'package:shellx_acp/shellx_acp.dart';
import 'package:shellx_ai/remote.dart';
import 'package:shellx_client/shellx_client.dart';
import 'package:shellx_mobile/core/core.dart';

/// V2-2 §5.2: one AI session on the phone. It mirrors the session while the
/// page is open (attached when made, detached on [dispose]) and sends only
/// what the user explicitly asked for. A request answers its failure code
/// (null: done) so the page can say it; nothing here is ever logged but
/// error types.
@injectable
class AiSessionController extends Controller {
  AiSessionController(@factoryParam this.agentId, @factoryParam this.sid, SessionHolder session)
    : _backend = session.backend.peek() {
    final backend = _backend;
    if (backend == null) {
      _closed.value = true;
      return;
    }
    try {
      _machine = backend.machine(agentId);
    } on StateError {
      _closed.value = true; // not pinned any more
      return;
    }
    final machine = _machine!;
    _machineState.value = machine.state;
    _machineStates = machine.states.listen((s) => _set(_machineState, s));
    final link = machine.ai.attach(sid);
    _link = link;
    _changes = link.changes.listen((_) => _onChange());
    _onChange();
  }

  final String agentId;
  final String sid;
  final ShellxBackend? _backend;
  MachineSession? _machine;
  AiSessionHandle? _link;
  StreamSubscription<void>? _changes;
  StreamSubscription<MachineConnectionState>? _machineStates;
  Timer? _tick;
  bool _disposed = false;

  final Signal<int> _version = signal(0);
  final Signal<bool> _closed = signal(false);
  final Signal<MachineConnectionState> _machineState = signal(const MachineIdle());
  final Signal<bool> _sending = signal(false);
  final Signal<int?> _answering = signal(null);
  final Signal<bool> _stopping = signal(false);
  final Signal<DateTime> _now = signal(DateTime.now());

  /// The backend the page shows the relay state of; null when signed out.
  ShellxBackend? get backend => _backend;

  /// Bumps on every change of the mirror or the link (read it in a Watch).
  ReadonlySignal<int> get version => _version;

  /// The page cannot show this session (signed out, machine not pinned).
  ReadonlySignal<bool> get closed => _closed;
  ReadonlySignal<MachineConnectionState> get machineState => _machineState;
  ReadonlySignal<bool> get sending => _sending;

  /// The permission or question being answered (its buttons wait).
  ReadonlySignal<int?> get answering => _answering;
  ReadonlySignal<bool> get stopping => _stopping;

  /// Ticks every second while a turn runs ("Đang chạy · 0:12").
  ReadonlySignal<DateTime> get now => _now;

  AiMirror? get mirror => _link?.mirror;
  MirrorHead? get head => _link?.mirror.head;
  AiLinkState get linkState => _link?.state ?? AiLinkState.closed;
  String? get closedReason => _link?.closedReason;

  /// The page's text field may send: live, the computer takes prompts.
  bool get canSend => !_closed.peek() && linkState == AiLinkState.live && (head?.canSend ?? false) && !_sending.peek();

  /// Sends [text] (queued on the computer while a turn runs). Null when
  /// taken; else the failure code.
  Future<String?> send(String text) async {
    final machine = _machine;
    if (machine == null || text.trim().isEmpty || _sending.peek()) return 'refused';
    _set(_sending, true);
    try {
      await machine.ai.prompt(sid, text);
      return null;
    } on AiRequestException catch (e) {
      return e.code;
    } catch (error) {
      return _connectionCode(error);
    } finally {
      _set(_sending, false);
    }
  }

  /// The user's pick on the permission panel (V2-1 D10): one explicit tap.
  Future<String?> answerPermission(MirrorPermission permission, PermissionOption option) =>
      _answer(permission.id, () => _machine!.ai.answerPermission(sid, permission.id, option.optionId));

  /// The user's answer to a question (V2-1 D22).
  Future<String?> answerAsk(int ask, ElicitationResponse response) =>
      _answer(ask, () => _machine!.ai.answerAsk(sid, ask, response));

  /// Stop the turn (the desktop's Esc).
  Future<String?> stop() async {
    final machine = _machine;
    if (machine == null) return 'refused';
    _set(_stopping, true);
    try {
      await machine.ai.cancel(sid);
      return null;
    } on AiRequestException catch (e) {
      return e.code;
    } catch (error) {
      return _connectionCode(error);
    } finally {
      _set(_stopping, false);
    }
  }

  /// "Dừng hẳn", once the computer offers it.
  Future<String?> forceStop() => _simple(() => _machine!.ai.forceStop(sid));

  /// Drops queued prompt [id].
  Future<String?> unqueue(int id) => _simple(() => _machine!.ai.unqueue(sid, id));

  /// "Tải thêm".
  void loadEarlier() => _link?.loadEarlier();

  void dispose() {
    if (_disposed) return;
    _disposed = true;
    _tick?.cancel();
    unawaited(_changes?.cancel());
    unawaited(_machineStates?.cancel());
    _link?.detach();
  }

  Future<String?> _answer(int id, Future<void> Function() run) async {
    if (_machine == null || _answering.peek() != null) return 'refused';
    _set(_answering, id);
    try {
      await run();
      return null;
    } on AiRequestException catch (e) {
      return e.code;
    } catch (error) {
      return _connectionCode(error);
    } finally {
      _set(_answering, null);
    }
  }

  Future<String?> _simple(Future<void> Function() run) async {
    if (_machine == null) return 'refused';
    try {
      await run();
      return null;
    } on AiRequestException catch (e) {
      return e.code;
    } catch (error) {
      return _connectionCode(error);
    }
  }

  String _connectionCode(Object error) {
    if (kDebugMode) debugPrint('AI request error: ${error.runtimeType}');
    return 'offline';
  }

  void _onChange() {
    if (_disposed) return;
    final running = _link?.mirror.head?.turn != null;
    if (running && _tick == null) {
      _tick = Timer.periodic(const Duration(seconds: 1), (_) => _set(_now, DateTime.now()));
    } else if (!running) {
      _tick?.cancel();
      _tick = null;
    }
    _version.value++;
  }

  void _set<T>(Signal<T> s, T value) {
    if (_disposed) return;
    s.value = value;
  }
}
````

`apps/mobile/lib/features/ai/presentation/widgets/ai_markdown.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:flutter_markdown_plus/flutter_markdown_plus.dart';
import 'package:shellx_mobile/core/core.dart';
import 'package:shellx_mobile/features/ai/domain/ai_text.dart';
import 'package:shellx_mobile/shared/shared.dart';

/// An agent's answer as markdown (V2-2 §5.2), split into blocks so a
/// streaming answer re-renders only its last block. Images are never
/// loaded (their alt text shows); a link opens on tap, http/https only
/// (V2-1 B15).
class AiMarkdown extends StatelessWidget {
  const AiMarkdown({super.key, required this.text, this.dim = false});

  final String text;

  /// A thought: secondary colour.
  final bool dim;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final blocks = markdownBlocks(text);
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        for (final (i, block) in blocks.indexed)
          Padding(
            padding: EdgeInsets.only(top: i == 0 ? 0 : 8),
            child: _Block(key: ValueKey(i), text: block, dim: dim),
          ),
      ],
    );
  }
}

/// Builds its markdown once per text: an unchanged block hands Flutter the
/// same widget, so nothing below it rebuilds while the answer streams.
class _Block extends StatefulWidget {
  const _Block({super.key, required this.text, required this.dim});

  final String text;
  final bool dim;

  @override
  State<_Block> createState() => _BlockState();
}

class _BlockState extends State<_Block> {
  Widget? _built;

  @override
  void didChangeDependencies() {
    super.didChangeDependencies();
    _built = null;
  }

  @override
  void didUpdateWidget(_Block oldWidget) {
    super.didUpdateWidget(oldWidget);
    if (oldWidget.text != widget.text || oldWidget.dim != widget.dim) _built = null;
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => _built ??= _make(context);

  Widget _make(BuildContext context) {
    final colors = context.colors;
    final base = context.typo.paragraph.copyWith(color: widget.dim ? colors.secondaryText : colors.primaryText);
    final mono = TextStyle(fontFamily: FontFamily.jetBrainsMono, fontSize: 13, color: colors.primaryText);
    return MarkdownBody(
      data: widget.text,
      selectable: true,
      styleSheet: MarkdownStyleSheet(
        p: base,
        code: mono.copyWith(backgroundColor: colors.primarySoft),
        codeblockDecoration: BoxDecoration(color: colors.primarySoft, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
        codeblockPadding: const EdgeInsets.all(10),
        a: base.copyWith(color: colors.link, decoration: TextDecoration.underline),
        h1: context.typo.heading20.bold.copyWith(color: colors.primaryText),
        h2: context.typo.subHeader.bold.copyWith(color: colors.primaryText),
        h3: base.copyWith(fontWeight: FontWeight.w700),
        listBullet: base,
        blockquote: base.copyWith(color: colors.secondaryText),
      ),
      imageBuilder: (uri, title, alt) => Text(alt ?? '', style: base.copyWith(color: colors.secondaryText)),
      onTapLink: (text, href, title) {
        final uri = href == null ? null : Uri.tryParse(href);
        if (uri == null || !(uri.scheme == 'http' || uri.scheme == 'https') || uri.host.isEmpty) return;
        getIt<ExternalLinks>().openOrToast(uri);
      },
    );
  }
}
````

`apps/mobile/lib/features/ai/presentation/widgets/ai_items.dart`:

````dart
import 'package:shellx_acp/shellx_acp.dart';
import 'package:shellx_ai/remote.dart';
import 'package:shellx_mobile/core/core.dart';
import 'package:shellx_mobile/features/ai/domain/ai_text.dart';
import 'package:shellx_mobile/features/ai/domain/ai_words.dart';
import 'package:shellx_mobile/features/ai/presentation/widgets/ai_markdown.dart';
import 'package:shellx_mobile/shared/shared.dart';

/// One conversation item on the phone (V2-2 §5.2). Keyed by index and rev
/// by the timeline, so it rebuilds only when the desktop changed it.
class AiItemView extends StatelessWidget {
  const AiItemView({super.key, required this.item, required this.agentName});

  final MirrorItem item;
  final String agentName;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final item = this.item;
    return switch (item) {
      MirrorText(kind: MirrorTextKind.user) => _UserBubble(item),
      MirrorText(kind: MirrorTextKind.agent) => _AgentText(item),
      MirrorText(kind: MirrorTextKind.thought) => _Thought(item),
      MirrorTool() => AiToolCard(tool: item),
      MirrorAsk() => _AskLine(item, agentName),
      MirrorTurnEnd() => _TurnEnd(item),
      MirrorNotice() => _Notice(item, agentName),
      MirrorUnknown() => const _OnComputer(),
    };
  }
}

class _UserBubble extends StatelessWidget {
  const _UserBubble(this.item);
  final MirrorText item;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final colors = context.colors;
    return Align(
      alignment: Alignment.centerRight,
      child: ConstrainedBox(
        constraints: BoxConstraints(maxWidth: MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).width * 0.82),
        child: Container(
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 10),
          decoration: BoxDecoration(
            color: colors.primarySoft,
            borderRadius: const BorderRadius.only(
              topLeft: Radius.circular(16),
              topRight: Radius.circular(16),
              bottomLeft: Radius.circular(16),
              bottomRight: Radius.circular(4),
            ),
          ),
          child: SelectableText(item.text, style: context.typo.paragraph.copyWith(color: colors.primaryText)),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _AgentText extends StatelessWidget {
  const _AgentText(this.item);
  final MirrorText item;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Column(
    crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
    children: [
      AiMarkdown(text: item.text),
      if (item.fullLength != null) _Hint(S.of(context).aiCutText),
    ],
  );
}

/// Folded by default (V2-1 §4.2).
class _Thought extends StatefulWidget {
  const _Thought(this.item);
  final MirrorText item;

  @override
  State<_Thought> createState() => _ThoughtState();
}

class _ThoughtState extends State<_Thought> {
  var _open = false;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final colors = context.colors;
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        InkWell(
          onTap: () => setState(() => _open = !_open),
          child: Padding(
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 6),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                Icon(_open ? Icons.expand_less : Icons.expand_more, size: 18, color: colors.secondaryText),
                4.sizedWidth,
                Text(S.of(context).aiThought, style: context.typo.paragraph13.copyWith(color: colors.secondaryText)),
              ],
            ),
          ),
        ),
        if (_open) AiMarkdown(text: widget.item.text, dim: true),
      ],
    );
  }
}

/// The question's place in the conversation; its form is in the dock.
class _AskLine extends StatelessWidget {
  const _AskLine(this.item, this.agentName);
  final MirrorAsk item;
  final String agentName;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final s = S.of(context);
    final colors = context.colors;
    final status = switch (item.status) {
      'open' => s.aiAskWaiting,
      'accepted' || 'completed' => s.aiAskAnswered,
      'declined' || 'auto_declined' => s.aiAskDeclined,
      'cancelled' => s.aiAskCancelled,
      _ => item.status,
    };
    return _CardFrame(
      warn: item.open,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
        children: [
          Text(s.aiAskTitle(agentName), style: context.typo.paragraph13.bold.copyWith(color: colors.secondaryText)),
          4.sizedHeight,
          Text(item.request?.message ?? '', style: context.typo.paragraph.copyWith(color: colors.primaryText)),
          4.sizedHeight,
          Text(
            item.status == 'auto_declined' ? s.aiAskUnsupported : status,
            style: context.typo.paragraph13.copyWith(color: item.open ? colors.warn : colors.secondaryText),
          ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _TurnEnd extends StatelessWidget {
  const _TurnEnd(this.item);
  final MirrorTurnEnd item;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final s = S.of(context);
    final colors = context.colors;
    final time = AiWords.clock(item.elapsed);
    final head = switch (item.outcome) {
      'cancelled' => s.aiTurnStopped(item.turn, time),
      'interrupted' => s.aiTurnInterrupted(item.turn),
      'failed' => s.aiTurnFailed(item.turn),
      _ => s.aiTurnSummary(item.turn, time),
    };
    final files = item.files;
    final parts = [
      head,
      if (files != null && files > 0) '${s.aiTurnFiles(files)} +${item.plus} −${item.minus}',
    ];
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 4),
      child: Column(
        children: [
          Row(
            children: [
              Expanded(child: Divider(color: colors.divider)),
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8),
                child: Text(
                  parts.join(' · '),
                  style: context.typo.metadata12.copyWith(color: colors.secondaryText),
                ),
              ),
              Expanded(child: Divider(color: colors.divider)),
            ],
          ),
          if (item.error != null) _Hint(item.error!),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _Notice extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Notice(this.item, this.agentName);
  final MirrorNotice item;
  final String agentName;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final colors = context.colors;
    return Opacity(
      opacity: item.resolved ? 0.55 : 1,
      child: _CardFrame(
        warn: !item.resolved,
        child: Row(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
          children: [
            Icon(Icons.info_outline, size: 18, color: item.resolved ? colors.secondaryText : colors.warn),
            8.sizedWidth,
            Expanded(
              child: Text(
                AiWords.notice(S.of(context), item, agentName),
                style: context.typo.paragraph13.copyWith(color: colors.primaryText),
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _OnComputer extends StatelessWidget {
  const _OnComputer();

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => _CardFrame(child: _Hint(S.of(context).aiFromComputerOnly));
}

class _Hint extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Hint(this.text);
  final String text;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) => Padding(
    padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 4),
    child: Text(text, style: context.typo.paragraph13.copyWith(color: context.colors.secondaryText)),
  );
}

class _CardFrame extends StatelessWidget {
  const _CardFrame({required this.child, this.warn = false});
  final Widget child;
  final bool warn;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final colors = context.colors;
    return Container(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 12, vertical: 10),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: warn ? colors.warnSoft : colors.surface,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(12),
        border: Border.all(color: warn ? colors.warn.withValues(alpha: 0.5) : colors.divider),
      ),
      child: child,
    );
  }
}

/// One tool call (V2-1 §4.2 on a phone): a one-line header with its state;
/// a tap shows the diff, the output or the input. Open by itself while it
/// waits for a permission.
class AiToolCard extends StatefulWidget {
  const AiToolCard({super.key, required this.tool});

  final MirrorTool tool;

  @override
  State<AiToolCard> createState() => _AiToolCardState();
}

class _AiToolCardState extends State<AiToolCard> {
  bool? _open;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final s = S.of(context);
    final colors = context.colors;
    final t = widget.tool;
    final waiting = t.permission == MirrorPermissionState.waiting;
    final open = _open ?? waiting;
    final hasBody = t.diffs.isNotEmpty || t.output != null || t.input != null;
    return _CardFrame(
      warn: waiting,
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          InkWell(
            onTap: hasBody ? () => setState(() => _open = !open) : null,
            child: Row(
              children: [
                Icon(_icon(t.kind), size: 18, color: colors.secondaryText),
                8.sizedWidth,
                Expanded(
                  child: Text(
                    AiWords.toolTitle(s, t),
                    maxLines: open ? 4 : 1,
                    overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                    style: context.typo.paragraph13.copyWith(
                      fontFamily: FontFamily.jetBrainsMono,
                      color: colors.primaryText,
                    ),
                  ),
                ),
                8.sizedWidth,
                _ToolState(tool: t),
                if (hasBody) Icon(open ? Icons.expand_less : Icons.expand_more, size: 18, color: colors.secondaryText),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          if (t.answerKind != null) ...[
            4.sizedHeight,
            Text(
              s.aiYouChose(
                t.answerName == null || t.answerName!.isEmpty
                    ? _optionWord(s, t.answerKind!)
                    : '${_optionWord(s, t.answerKind!)} (${t.answerName})',
              ),
              style: context.typo.metadata12.copyWith(color: colors.secondaryText),
            ),
          ],
          if (open) ...[
            for (final d in t.diffs)
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8),
                child: AiDiffView(diff: d),
              ),
            if (t.moreDiffs > 0) _Hint(s.aiMoreDiffs(t.moreDiffs)),
            if (t.output != null) Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8), child: _Output(t.output!)),
            if (t.output == null && t.kind == ToolKind.execute && t.end == 'completed') _Hint(s.aiNoOutput),
            if (t.input != null) Padding(padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 8), child: _Mono(t.input!)),
          ],
        ],
      ),
    );
  }

  static String _optionWord(S s, PermissionKind kind) => switch (kind) {
    PermissionKind.allowOnce => s.aiPermAllowOnce,
    PermissionKind.allowAlways => s.aiPermAllowAlways,
    PermissionKind.rejectOnce => s.aiPermRejectOnce,
    PermissionKind.rejectAlways => s.aiPermRejectAlways,
    PermissionKind.other => '',
  };

  static IconData _icon(ToolKind? kind) => switch (kind) {
    ToolKind.read => Icons.description_outlined,
    ToolKind.edit => Icons.edit_outlined,
    ToolKind.delete => Icons.delete_outline,
    ToolKind.move => Icons.drive_file_rename_outline,
    ToolKind.search => Icons.search,
    ToolKind.execute => Icons.terminal,
    ToolKind.think => Icons.psychology_outlined,
    ToolKind.fetch => Icons.public,
    ToolKind.switchMode => Icons.swap_horiz,
    ToolKind.other || null => Icons.extension_outlined,
  };
}

/// "chờ", "chờ bạn", "đang chạy · 2,1 s", ✓, "bị từ chối", "đã dừng", "lỗi".
class _ToolState extends StatelessWidget {
  const _ToolState({required this.tool});
  final MirrorTool tool;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final s = S.of(context);
    final colors = context.colors;
    final t = tool;
    final style = context.typo.metadata12;
    if (t.permission == MirrorPermissionState.waiting) {
      return Text(s.aiToolAwaiting, style: style.copyWith(color: colors.warn));
    }
    switch (t.end) {
      case 'completed':
        return Icon(Icons.check, size: 16, color: colors.ok, semanticLabel: 'ok');
      case 'failed':
        final rejected = t.answerKind?.isReject ?? false;
        return Text(rejected ? s.aiToolRejected : s.aiToolFailed, style: style.copyWith(color: colors.error));
      case 'stopped':
        return Text(s.aiToolStopped, style: style.copyWith(color: colors.secondaryText));
    }
    if (t.status == ToolCallStatus.inProgress && t.startedAt != null) {
      final secs = DateTime.now().difference(t.startedAt!).inMilliseconds / 1000;
      return Text(s.aiToolRunningFor('${secs.toStringAsFixed(1)} s'), style: style.copyWith(color: colors.link));
    }
    return Text(s.aiToolPending, style: style.copyWith(color: colors.secondaryText));
  }
}

/// A file's change as −/+ lines (V2-2 D6), scrolling sideways.
class AiDiffView extends StatelessWidget {
  const AiDiffView({super.key, required this.diff});

  final MirrorDiff diff;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final s = S.of(context);
    final colors = context.colors;
    final lines = diffLines(diff.oldText, diff.newText);
    const mono = TextStyle(fontFamily: FontFamily.jetBrainsMono, fontSize: 12, height: 1.35);
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        Text(
          [
            AiWords.fileName(diff.path),
            if (diff.oldText == null) s.aiCreated,
            '+${diff.plus} −${diff.minus}',
          ].join(' · '),
          style: context.typo.metadata12.bold.copyWith(color: colors.secondaryText),
        ),
        4.sizedHeight,
        Container(
          decoration: BoxDecoration(color: colors.background, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
          padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(vertical: 6),
          child: SingleChildScrollView(
            scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
            padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 8),
            child: SelectableText.rich(
              TextSpan(
                children: [
                  for (final (i, l) in lines.indexed)
                    TextSpan(
                      text: '${l.mark} ${l.text}${i == lines.length - 1 ? '' : '\n'}',
                      style: mono.copyWith(
                        color: l.mark == ' ' ? colors.secondaryText : colors.primaryText,
                        backgroundColor: switch (l.mark) {
                          '+' => colors.okDot.withValues(alpha: 0.16),
                          '-' => colors.error.withValues(alpha: 0.12),
                          _ => null,
                        },
                      ),
                    ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
        if (diff.cut) _Hint(s.aiFromComputerOnly),
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _Output extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Output(this.output);
  final MirrorOutput output;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final s = S.of(context);
    return Column(
      crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
      children: [
        _Mono(output.head),
        if (output.cut) ...[_Hint(s.aiCutLines(output.lines)), _Mono(output.tail)],
      ],
    );
  }
}

class _Mono extends StatelessWidget {
  const _Mono(this.text);
  final String text;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final colors = context.colors;
    return Container(
      decoration: BoxDecoration(color: colors.background, borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(8)),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(8),
      child: SingleChildScrollView(
        scrollDirection: Axis.horizontal,
        child: SelectableText(
          text,
          style: TextStyle(fontFamily: FontFamily.jetBrainsMono, fontSize: 12, height: 1.35, color: colors.primaryText),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

````dart
import 'package:flutter/services.dart';
import 'package:shellx_acp/shellx_acp.dart';
import 'package:shellx_ai/remote.dart';
import 'package:shellx_mobile/core/core.dart';
import 'package:shellx_mobile/features/ai/domain/ai_words.dart';
import 'package:shellx_mobile/shared/shared.dart';

/// V2-1 D10 on a phone (V2-2 §5.3): the waiting permission with one big
/// button per option, in the agent's order. Every answer is one explicit
/// tap; nothing is ever picked for the user, and the buttons wait while
/// the answer travels.
class AiPermissionPanel extends StatelessWidget {
  const AiPermissionPanel({
    super.key,
    required this.permission,
    required this.agentName,
    required this.busy,
    required this.onAnswer,
    this.onDetails,
  });

  final MirrorPermission permission;
  final String agentName;

  /// The answer is on its way: buttons disabled.
  final bool busy;
  final ValueChanged<PermissionOption> onAnswer;

  /// Shows the tool card (the diff) in the conversation.
  final VoidCallback? onDetails;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final s = S.of(context);
    final colors = context.colors;
    return Semantics(
      liveRegion: true,
      container: true,
      child: Container(
        key: const Key('ai.permission'),
        margin: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(12, 8, 12, 0),
        padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(14, 12, 14, 12),
        decoration: BoxDecoration(
          color: colors.warnSoft,
          borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
          border: Border.all(color: colors.warn.withValues(alpha: 0.5)),
        ),
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            Text(s.aiPermTitle(agentName), style: context.typo.paragraph13.bold.copyWith(color: colors.warn)),
            4.sizedHeight,
            Text(
              permission.title,
              maxLines: 3,
              overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
              style: context.typo.subHeader15.bold.copyWith(color: colors.primaryText),
            ),
            if (permission.more > 0) ...[
              2.sizedHeight,
              Text(s.aiPermMore(permission.more), style: context.typo.metadata12.copyWith(color: colors.secondaryText)),
            ],
            10.sizedHeight,
            for (final o in permission.options) ...[
              _OptionButton(option: o, busy: busy, onTap: () => onAnswer(o)),
              8.sizedHeight,
            ],
            if (onDetails != null)
              TextButton(
                key: const Key('ai.permission.details'),
                onPressed: onDetails,
                child: Text(s.aiPermDetails),
              ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

class _OptionButton extends StatelessWidget {
  const _OptionButton({required this.option, required this.busy, required this.onTap});

  final PermissionOption option;
  final bool busy;
  final VoidCallback onTap;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final s = S.of(context);
    final colors = context.colors;
    final allow = option.kind == PermissionKind.allowOnce;
    final reject = option.kind.isReject;
    final always = option.kind == PermissionKind.allowAlways || option.kind == PermissionKind.rejectAlways;
    final label = AiWords.option(s, option);
    return Semantics(
      button: true,
      label: always ? '$label, ${option.name}' : label,
      child: Opacity(
        opacity: busy ? 0.45 : 1,
        child: Material(
          color: allow ? colors.primaryFill : colors.surface,
          shape: RoundedRectangleBorder(
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
            side: allow ? BorderSide.none : BorderSide(color: reject ? colors.error : colors.divider),
          ),
          child: InkWell(
            key: Key('ai.option.${option.optionId}'),
            borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(14),
            onTap: busy
                ? null
                : () {
                    HapticFeedback.mediumImpact();
                    onTap();
                  },
            child: ConstrainedBox(
              constraints: const BoxConstraints(minHeight: 52),
              child: Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 8),
                child: Column(
                  mainAxisAlignment: MainAxisAlignment.center,
                  children: [
                    ExcludeSemantics(
                      child: Text(
                        label,
                        textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                        style: context.typo.subHeader15.bold.copyWith(
                          color: allow ? colors.onPrimary : (reject ? colors.error : colors.primaryText),
                        ),
                      ),
                    ),
                    if (always && option.name.isNotEmpty)
                      ExcludeSemantics(
                        child: Text(
                          option.name,
                          textAlign: TextAlign.center,
                          maxLines: 2,
                          overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis,
                          style: context.typo.metadata12.copyWith(color: colors.secondaryText),
                        ),
                      ),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
        ),
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// V2-1 D22 on a phone: the waiting question's form. "Gửi câu trả lời"
/// works only when the form accepts the values (the desktop checks again).
/// A URL question, or a form too big for the phone, is answered on the
/// computer; it can still be declined here.
class AiAskPanel extends StatefulWidget {
  const AiAskPanel({
    super.key,
    required this.ask,
    required this.agentName,
    required this.busy,
    required this.onAnswer,
  });

  final MirrorAsk ask;
  final String agentName;
  final bool busy;
  final ValueChanged<ElicitationResponse> onAnswer;

  @override
  State<AiAskPanel> createState() => _AiAskPanelState();
}

class _AiAskPanelState extends State<AiAskPanel> {
  final _values = <String, Object>{};

  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    final form = widget.ask.request;
    if (form is ElicitationForm) {
      for (final f in form.fields) {
        final d = switch (f) {
          ElicitTextField(:final defaultValue) => defaultValue,
          ElicitNumberField(:final defaultValue) => defaultValue,
          ElicitBoolField(:final defaultValue) => defaultValue,
          ElicitChoiceField(:final defaultValue) => defaultValue,
          ElicitMultiChoiceField(:final defaultValue) => defaultValue,
          ElicitUnsupportedField() => null,
        };
        if (d != null) _values[f.key] = d;
      }
    }
  }

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final s = S.of(context);
    final colors = context.colors;
    final request = widget.ask.request;
    final form = request is ElicitationForm && request.supported ? request : null;
    final ok = form != null && form.accepts(_values);
    return Container(
      key: const Key('ai.ask'),
      margin: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(12, 8, 12, 0),
      padding: const EdgeInsets.all(14),
      constraints: BoxConstraints(maxHeight: MediaQuery.sizeOf(context).height * 0.5),
      decoration: BoxDecoration(
        color: colors.warnSoft,
        borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(16),
        border: Border.all(color: colors.warn.withValues(alpha: 0.5)),
      ),
      child: SingleChildScrollView(
        child: Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            Text(s.aiAskTitle(widget.agentName), style: context.typo.paragraph13.bold.copyWith(color: colors.warn)),
            4.sizedHeight,
            Text(request?.message ?? '', style: context.typo.subHeader15.copyWith(color: colors.primaryText)),
            8.sizedHeight,
            if (form == null)
              Text(
                request is ElicitationForm ? s.aiAskUnsupported : s.aiAskOnComputer,
                style: context.typo.paragraph13.copyWith(color: colors.secondaryText),
              )
            else
              for (final f in form.fields) _field(context, f),
            10.sizedHeight,
            if (form != null)
              AppButton(
                key: const Key('ai.ask.submit'),
                title: s.aiAskSubmit,
                height: 48,
                state: widget.busy || !ok ? ButtonState.disable : ButtonState.available,
                onTap: () => widget.onAnswer(ElicitAccept(Map.of(_values))),
              ),
            TextButton(
              key: const Key('ai.ask.decline'),
              onPressed: widget.busy ? null : () => widget.onAnswer(const ElicitDecline()),
              child: Text(s.aiAskDecline),
            ),
          ],
        ),
      ),
    );
  }

  Widget _field(BuildContext context, ElicitField f) {
    final colors = context.colors;
    final label = f.title ?? f.key;
    void set(Object? v) => setState(() => v == null ? _values.remove(f.key) : _values[f.key] = v);
    final title = Text(label, style: context.typo.paragraph13.bold.copyWith(color: colors.primaryText));
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 10),
      child: switch (f) {
        ElicitBoolField() => SwitchListTile(
          contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
          title: title,
          value: _values[f.key] == true,
          onChanged: (v) => set(v),
        ),
        ElicitChoiceField(:final options) => Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            title,
            RadioGroup<String>(
              groupValue: _values[f.key] as String?,
              onChanged: set,
              child: Column(
                children: [
                  for (final o in options)
                    RadioListTile<String>(contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero, value: o.value, title: Text(o.title)),
                ],
              ),
            ),
          ],
        ),
        ElicitMultiChoiceField(:final options) => Column(
          crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
          children: [
            title,
            for (final o in options)
              CheckboxListTile(
                contentPadding: EdgeInsets.zero,
                value: ((_values[f.key] as List?) ?? const []).contains(o.value),
                title: Text(o.title),
                onChanged: (on) {
                  final now = [...((_values[f.key] as List?)?.cast<String>() ?? const <String>[])];
                  if (on == true) {
                    now.add(o.value);
                  } else {
                    now.remove(o.value);
                  }
                  set(now.isEmpty ? null : now);
                },
              ),
          ],
        ),
        ElicitNumberField(:final integer) => TextFormField(
          initialValue: _values[f.key]?.toString(),
          decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: label, helperText: f.description),
          keyboardType: TextInputType.numberWithOptions(decimal: !integer, signed: true),
          onChanged: (t) => set(integer ? int.tryParse(t) : num.tryParse(t)),
        ),
        ElicitTextField() => TextFormField(
          initialValue: _values[f.key] as String?,
          decoration: InputDecoration(labelText: label, helperText: f.description),
          onChanged: (t) => set(t.isEmpty ? null : t),
        ),
        ElicitUnsupportedField() => const SizedBox.shrink(),
      },
    );
  }
}

/// The prompts waiting for the turn to end, each with "Bỏ khỏi hàng đợi".
class AiQueueRow extends StatelessWidget {
  const AiQueueRow({super.key, required this.queue, required this.onRemove});

  final List<MirrorQueued> queue;
  final ValueChanged<int> onRemove;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final s = S.of(context);
    final colors = context.colors;
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(12, 6, 12, 0),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          for (final q in queue)
            Container(
              margin: const EdgeInsets.only(bottom: 4),
              padding: const EdgeInsets.only(left: 12),
              decoration: BoxDecoration(
                color: colors.surface,
                borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(10),
                border: Border.all(color: colors.divider),
              ),
              child: Row(
                children: [
                  Text(s.aiQueued, style: context.typo.metadata11.bold.copyWith(color: colors.secondaryText)),
                  8.sizedWidth,
                  Expanded(
                    child: Text(q.text, maxLines: 1, overflow: TextOverflow.ellipsis, style: context.typo.paragraph13),
                  ),
                  IconButton(
                    key: Key('ai.unqueue.${q.id}'),
                    tooltip: s.aiRemoveQueued,
                    icon: Icon(Icons.close, size: 18, color: colors.secondaryText),
                    onPressed: () => onRemove(q.id),
                  ),
                ],
              ),
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// The text field and its buttons (V2-1 D16 on a phone): Send, or Queue
/// while a turn runs; Stop while a turn runs and the field is empty; Force
/// stop once the computer offers it. The draft stays when sending fails.
class AiComposer extends StatefulWidget {
  const AiComposer({
    super.key,
    required this.controller,
    required this.agentName,
    required this.enabled,
    required this.running,
    required this.stopping,
    required this.canForceStop,
    required this.sending,
    required this.onSend,
    required this.onStop,
    required this.onForceStop,
  });

  final TextEditingController controller;
  final String agentName;
  final bool enabled;
  final bool running;
  final bool stopping;
  final bool canForceStop;
  final bool sending;
  final VoidCallback onSend;
  final VoidCallback onStop;
  final VoidCallback onForceStop;

  @override
  State<AiComposer> createState() => _AiComposerState();
}

class _AiComposerState extends State<AiComposer> {
  @override
  void initState() {
    super.initState();
    widget.controller.addListener(_changed);
  }

  @override
  void dispose() {
    widget.controller.removeListener(_changed);
    super.dispose();
  }

  void _changed() => setState(() {});

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final s = S.of(context);
    final colors = context.colors;
    final hasText = widget.controller.text.trim().isNotEmpty;
    final showStop = widget.running && !hasText;
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(12, 8, 8, 8),
      child: Row(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.end,
        children: [
          Expanded(
            child: TextField(
              key: const Key('ai.composer'),
              controller: widget.controller,
              enabled: widget.enabled,
              minLines: 1,
              maxLines: 6,
              textInputAction: TextInputAction.newline,
              keyboardType: TextInputType.multiline,
              decoration: InputDecoration(
                hintText: s.aiComposerHint(widget.agentName),
                filled: true,
                fillColor: colors.surface,
                contentPadding: const EdgeInsets.symmetric(horizontal: 14, vertical: 10),
                border: OutlineInputBorder(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                  borderSide: BorderSide(color: colors.divider),
                ),
                enabledBorder: OutlineInputBorder(
                  borderRadius: BorderRadius.circular(20),
                  borderSide: BorderSide(color: colors.divider),
                ),
              ),
            ),
          ),
          4.sizedWidth,
          if (widget.canForceStop)
            IconButton(
              key: const Key('ai.forceStop'),
              tooltip: s.aiForceStop,
              icon: Icon(Icons.dangerous_outlined, color: colors.error),
              onPressed: widget.onForceStop,
            ),
          if (showStop)
            IconButton.filledTonal(
              key: const Key('ai.stop'),
              tooltip: widget.stopping ? s.aiStopping : s.aiStop,
              icon: widget.stopping
                  ? const SizedBox.square(dimension: 18, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2))
                  : const Icon(Icons.stop_rounded),
              onPressed: widget.stopping ? null : widget.onStop,
            )
          else
            IconButton.filled(
              key: const Key('ai.send'),
              tooltip: widget.running ? s.aiQueue : s.aiSend,
              icon: widget.sending
                  ? const SizedBox.square(dimension: 18, child: CircularProgressIndicator(strokeWidth: 2))
                  : Icon(widget.running ? Icons.playlist_add : Icons.arrow_upward),
              onPressed: widget.enabled && hasText && !widget.sending ? widget.onSend : null,
            ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}

/// The agent's plan in one line ("Kế hoạch · 2/5"), opened by a tap.
class AiPlanStrip extends StatefulWidget {
  const AiPlanStrip({super.key, required this.plan});

  final List<PlanEntry> plan;

  @override
  State<AiPlanStrip> createState() => _AiPlanStripState();
}

class _AiPlanStripState extends State<AiPlanStrip> {
  var _open = false;

  @override
  Widget build(BuildContext context) {
    final colors = context.colors;
    final done = widget.plan.where((e) => e.status == PlanStatus.completed).length;
    return Padding(
      padding: const EdgeInsets.fromLTRB(12, 6, 12, 0),
      child: Column(
        crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.stretch,
        children: [
          InkWell(
            key: const Key('ai.plan'),
            onTap: () => setState(() => _open = !_open),
            child: Row(
              children: [
                Icon(Icons.checklist, size: 16, color: colors.secondaryText),
                6.sizedWidth,
                Text(
                  S.of(context).aiPlan(done, widget.plan.length),
                  style: context.typo.paragraph13.copyWith(color: colors.secondaryText),
                ),
                const Spacer(),
                Icon(_open ? Icons.expand_more : Icons.expand_less, size: 18, color: colors.secondaryText),
              ],
            ),
          ),
          if (_open)
            for (final e in widget.plan)
              Padding(
                padding: const EdgeInsets.only(top: 4),
                child: Row(
                  crossAxisAlignment: CrossAxisAlignment.start,
                  children: [
                    Icon(
                      switch (e.status) {
                        PlanStatus.completed => Icons.check_box,
                        PlanStatus.inProgress => Icons.indeterminate_check_box_outlined,
                        PlanStatus.pending => Icons.check_box_outline_blank,
                      },
                      size: 16,
                      color: colors.secondaryText,
                    ),
                    6.sizedWidth,
                    Expanded(child: Text(e.content, style: context.typo.paragraph13)),
                  ],
                ),
              ),
        ],
      ),
    );
  }
}
